# Raf Carpentry — Project Context for Claude
# Updated: 13 November 2026

## Owner
Rafal Janczy — carpenter based in London, UK. Builds bespoke kitchens, wardrobes, cabinets, garden offices. Also building an app called Cabinetos and is a SketchUp expert. Active on Instagram (@rafcarpentry), TikTok (@rafcarpentry), Facebook.

## Project
- **Site**: https://www.rafcarpentry.com (main address). DNS at GoDaddy points to Vercel; rafcarpentry.com and raf-carpentry.vercel.app redirect to www (set in Vercel > Domains, not in code)
- **rafcarpentry.co.uk (21 Oct 2026)**: bought by Raf at Cloudflare Registrar (about £4 a year, nameservers elijah / kinsley.ns.cloudflare.com) only to send people to the .com, never a second copy of the site. In Vercel (raf-carpentry project, added with the connector) rafcarpentry.co.uk and www.rafcarpentry.co.uk both redirect 308 to www.rafcarpentry.com; in Cloudflare DNS: A @ 76.76.21.21 and CNAME www cname.vercel-dns.com, both DNS only (grey cloud) so Vercel issues the certificate.
- **Vercel address**: https://raf-carpentry.vercel.app
- **GitHub**: https://github.com/rafmoney503/raf-carpentry
- **Stack**: Next.js 16 + Tailwind CSS v4 + TypeScript + TinaCMS
- **Hosting**: Vercel (free tier, auto-deploys from GitHub main branch)
- **Server function size (10 Oct 2026)**: blog pages and the sharing pictures (src/lib/og-card.tsx) check or read files in public/ with fs, so Next.js used to trace the whole public folder (photos, videos, models; over 200 MB) into every server function. With the third batch of blog photos the functions reached about 255 MB and Vercel refused the deploy (limit 250 MB; the GitHub status only says 'Deployment has failed'). next.config.ts now has `outputFileTracingExcludes: { '**': ['public/**/*'] }`, which brought each function down to about 41 MB. public/ files are served from the CDN, and the pages that read them are built at deploy time, so nothing needs them inside a function. Check with the .nft.json sizes in .next/server if a deploy fails after adding lots of files.
- **Vercel connector (10 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Use Vercel for this')**: connected in Claude. Project raf-carpentry = prj_IkzuBXXOBx9bpqK3nZEduUPQyPAM, team 'rafals-projects-ecbcd9de' = team_pc2mKRP43Mre23IApQxx0jyl. Works when called WITHOUT a team/slug argument: list_projects, list_deployments (state READY/ERROR per commit, use this instead of the GitHub status), filter_project_envs (names only, never decrypt), count_pageviews / aggregate_pageviews (Web Analytics is on: data from 9 Oct 2026). Refused with 403 'must re-authenticate to this scope': build logs (list_deployment_events), runtime logs and runtime errors, get_project by slug; Raf needs to reconnect the connector and pick the 'Rafal's projects' team when Vercel asks which scope to allow. Env on 10 Oct: JOBKIT_PIN and JOBKIT_GITHUB_TOKEN (sensitive, production), TINA_TOKEN, NEXT_PUBLIC_TINA_CLIENT_ID (production), NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL / _ANON_KEY (all environments).
- **Blog**: Markdown files in content/blog/ — parsed with gray-matter + remark

## Design (redesign, October 2026: "drafting paper")
- **Look**: light paper background, pencil-grey text, one blueprint-blue accent, square 2px corners, photos on a white mount with a hairline border and soft shadow.
- **Colours** (Tailwind tokens in src/app/globals.css `@theme`): paper #f1f2ef, raised #e6e7e2, mount #fbfbf9, ink #16191c, muted #4f555b, faint #5e646a, accent #2547d0 (hover #1b38ad). Use `bg-paper`, `bg-raised`, `text-ink`, `text-muted`, `text-faint`, `border-line`, `border-line-strong`, `bg-accent`, `text-accent`, `text-on-accent`. Do not use hex colours or zinc/gray classes in pages.
- **Fonts** (self-hosted in src/fonts, loaded in src/app/layout.tsx with next/font/local): Bricolage Grotesque for headings (`font-display`), Geist for body (`font-sans`), Geist Mono for small labels and measurements (`font-mono`).
- **Shared pieces**: `.btn .btn-primary .btn-ghost .btn-sm`, `.link-more`, `.kicker`, `.mount` (globals.css); `Container`, `PageHeader`, `SectionHeading`, `MountedImage` (src/components/ui.tsx); `QuoteCta` (src/components/QuoteCta.tsx, quote panel used on home and contact).
- **Homepage**: src/app/page.tsx + src/app/home.css, content from content/pages/home.json (editable in TinaCMS). Since 6 Oct 2026 (Raf asked for the home page not to look the same every time; a rotating slider was advised against): (1) "Main photo of the day": home.json heroJobs (TinaCMS "Main photo of the day": job slug, optional photo + description, optional crop like "62% 50%") take turns, one per London calendar day (londonDay() % count); the photo links to the job with a caption "Title, Area / See the job". Picks: birch ply wardrobe Chiswick (IMG_8405, the old hero), walnut desk Highbury, kitchen panelling Crouch End, reception desk Brentwood, bookcase radiator cover East Finchley, TV unit Haringey. Empty list = heroImage. (2) "Latest jobs": the 4 newest job pages (getAllProjects order) after "What I build", with month and area and "All N jobs"; a swipe row on phones. The page has `export const revalidate = 3600` (ISR), so it refreshes at most hourly: the photo changes soon after midnight and new jobs appear without a redeploy. The content files it reads are traced into the page bundle (checked in .next/server/app/page.js.nft.json), so the refresh works on Vercel.
- **Rules (taste-skill)**: no em-dashes in new copy, no emoji icons, one accent colour, one "Get a quote" label for every quote button, max 20 words under the hero headline.
- **Logo**: Crisp black R on white circle (public/images/r-logo-final.png). Browser tab icon (9 Oct 2026, replaced the leftover Vercel triangle): src/app/icon.svg = the solid R traced from Raf's drawing, ink, turning white when the browser is in dark mode; src/app/favicon.ico (16/32/48/256) = the same R on a white rounded square for older browsers and Safari. Phone home-screen icons (icon.png 192, apple-icon.png 180) still show the outline R on warm white.
- **Timber Stack game (9 Oct 2026, Raf's idea; since 25 Oct 2026 one of Workshop Games, see below: /tools/timber-stack redirects (308) into it, the old files and picture are gone, and its best score (raf_timber_best) and mute carry over the first time the games open)**: was /tools/timber-stack (src/app/tools/timber-stack/: page.tsx, timber-stack-game.tsx, opengraph-image.tsx with public/images/tools/timber-stack.jpg). The aim (Raf, 9 Oct): stack as much timber as possible, until almost nothing is left to put on top. A board slides in from the right; tap, click, Space or Enter drops it; the part hanging over the board below is cut off and falls (sawdust, a saw sound), so the next board is that much narrower ('only 45 mm left' under 80 mm); within 8 mm counts as Perfect (snaps square), and from the third Perfect in a row each one grows the board back 20 mm. Each board is 47 mm sawn timber: a yellow builder's tape measure up the left edge shows the height (mm ticks, cm numbers, a blue box every metre), and dashed height marks (seat 450 mm, worktop 900, door 1981, ceiling 2400, upstairs ceiling 4800, roof ridge 7500, ten metres) give the top board 40 mm back when passed, with a chime; under the score: boards, height and 'Door height in 17'. Speed starts at 300 mm/s and rises 6 per board to 720. Missing the stack ends the game; the view steps back to show the whole stack and tape above the score card (boards and height, Play again, Share score with the height, Get a quote). A new game starts by itself 4 seconds later ('New game in 3', Raf asked 9 Oct 2026); tapping outside the card starts it at once; pressing Share, or the page going into the background, stops the countdown. Units are mm across a 1,000 mm area, first board 600 mm, a dimension line shows the width; timber changes every 6 boards (birch ply, oak, walnut, pine, sapele). Canvas, no library; sounds made in code with a mute button; best score kept in the browser (localStorage `raf_timber_best`, `raf_timber_mute`). On its own page so it fills a phone screen (no floating buttons there, LayoutShell); linked from a 'Free game' card on the Tools page beside the Spirit level card, under the calculator and tips cards (line drawing 'stack' in sketches.tsx) and the sitemap.
- **Spirit level on your phone (17 Oct 2026, Raf: 'a digital level using iphone, because phones have different buttons it is difficult to make it level so ... a tara button which set level to zero and then move left right as you like to find right angle'; ideas and mockups on the 'Digital tools for the Tools page' design canvas, then 'go')**: /tools/spirit-level (src/app/tools/spirit-level/: page.tsx with How to use it and How accurate is it?, spirit-level.tsx client, spirit-level.css, opengraph-image.tsx with public/images/tools/spirit-level.jpg, a screenshot of the level set to zero). The page shows a start card (a level that tips and settles, Start the level; on a computer a QR code of the page to scan with a phone). Start asks for motion on an iPhone (DeviceOrientationEvent.requestPermission inside the tap, the beep's AudioContext too), then the level covers the whole screen (fixed .lv-app, page scroll locked, screen kept on with the Wake Lock API, Done closes); denied and no-sensor (a computer sends no reading within 1.6 s) get a plain message. The sums are in src/lib/level-math.ts (no imports): up in the phone's axes from beta and gamma; on its edge (worked out by itself: lying flat when up is within about 40 degrees of the screen normal, back on the edge under about 50) the tilt round the screen from the up axis of whichever edge is down (nearestAxis with 10 degrees of hysteresis; leaning the phone back doesn't change it), positive = right end high, 'Raise the left end 3.1 mm over 600'; lying flat both ways, turned to the screen with screen.orientation.angle, a bullseye with rings at level and 1 degree, 'Raise the left side ... and the near end ...'. Length chips 600 / 900 / 1200 / 2400 for the mm (raf_level_len). Set zero here: waits 0.35 s for the tap's wobble, averages 0.65 s, then reads from that surface; on an edge it keeps the edge it was set on, so angles past 45 still read from it (e.g. 60.0 degrees). Flip check: first reading, turn end for end on the same spot (on an edge the same edge down, screen facing away; flat a half turn), Second reading; the average of the two is the phone's own error for that edge (or flat), taken off every reading and kept on the phone (raf_level_bias per edge x+ x- y+ y- and flat); a different edge or mode on the second reading is caught. Hold freezes the reading; Beep sounds once each time it comes level (0.05, shows 0.0 with the green tick; ready again past 0.15; vibrates where the phone can; raf_level_beep); face down shows 'Turn the phone face up'; if the screen hasn't turned with the phone (rotation lock) it says so. Phone sideways: two columns, nothing to scroll. Honest note on the page: a phone reads to about 0.1 degree, about 1.7 mm over a metre. Linked from a 'Free phone tool' card on the Tools page (small level drawing), the Tools menu 'Free to use', site search ('Spirit level') and the sitemap; no floating buttons on the page (LayoutShell). Tested (scratchpad level/math-test.mts, 23 checks, and level/test.mjs, 56 checks as an iPhone 13 with made-up sensor readings: 0.3 right end high and the mm, bubble to the high end, 2400 chip, zero, 12.5 and 60 from zero, Hold, Clear, flip check finds a 0.4 error and the wrong edge, flat both ways, flat flip, beep saved, face down, phone sideways with the screen turned 90, rotation lock note, permission denied, a computer's QR and no-sensor message, 320 with no sideways scroll, no page errors). Not tried on a real phone yet. Other tools from the canvas, not built: angle finder (corner to mitre saw setting), plumb and scribe, floor walk, equal spacing (any screen). **Version 2 (22 Oct 2026, Raf: 'go' to upgrading the live level from the 'Version 2' page of the 'Digital tools for the Tools page' design canvas, where all 12 tools got v2 mockups; previewed on the `spirit-level-v2` branch, live since 22 Oct 2026, Raf: 'merge')**: the reading sits on a dark instrument panel (.lv-panel: ink with a faint blue grid and corner ticks, its own colours as --lv-* in spirit-level.css; 'On its edge · from your zero' and Live / Held (amber) along the top; light vial or bullseye; the number green with the tick at level), the action as the big line ('Raise the left end 3.1 mm', an up arrow, or a tick) and 'over 600 · 5.2 mm per metre' under it. The words come from levelWords() in src/lib/level-math.ts (replaced edgeWords / flatWords: main, sub, icon, detail for a saved reading, say for Speak; the numbers are the same sums). Hands-free row: **Tone** (replaced Beep, same raf_level_beep setting, on by default): within 2 degrees of level (TONE_RANGE) short 880 Hz beeps come faster nearer level (toneGap: 700 ms at 2 down to about 110 ms), then the old chirp at level and quiet until it has moved 0.15 off; five bars on the button show how close (closeness()). Fixed with it: the chirp never re-armed after the left end had been high (the old check used the signed tilt). **Speak** (raf_level_speak, off by default; 'No voice' where the browser has no speechSynthesis): says the reading once the words have stayed the same for 0.65 s, not the same words twice running, at most every 1.4 s ('Raise the left end, 3 mil', 'Right end up, 6 mil', '60.0 degrees', 'Level'; sayMm: one decimal under 3 mm, whole mm above; en-GB voice), a 'Saying: ...' line on the panel; started by speaking inside the tap that turns it on (and a silent word inside Start), which is what lets an iPhone talk. **Big numbers** (raf_level_big): no vial, the number as big as the panel allows (min(112px, (100cqi - 52px) / 3.15)). Length chips: a fifth pencil chip for your own length (LengthForm, readLength(): 50 to 10000 mm, '1,830' and '1830 mm' read; raf_level_own, raf_level_len keeps it); one tap picks it, a tap on the picked one changes it. Dock at the bottom of the screen: Set zero here, then Hold / Flip check / **Save**. Save keeps the reading from 250 ms before the tap (a 1.5 s ring of shown readings, so the tap's wobble isn't in it) or the held one, never face down, as {value '0.3°', detail 'raise the left end 3.1 mm over 600' (lying flat 'lying flat, raise ...', from a zero 'from the zero, ...'), label, place, mode, deg, len} in **Your readings** (src/lib/readings.ts: localStorage raf_readings, newest first, up to 200, memory only if storage is blocked, other tabs follow; the last place in raf_readings_place). A sheet drops under the top bar (so the keyboard never covers it): 'Saved 0.3°, raise ...', What is it? (optional), Place chips (places used, newest first, No place, + New place), All readings (N), Done; it goes by itself after 6 s unless touched. The top bar has a bookmark link with the count, the start card 'Your saved readings (N)' once there are any. **/tools/readings** (src/app/tools/readings/page.tsx + readings-list.tsx; noindex, not in the sitemap, no floating buttons (LayoutShell), linked only from the level): place chips with counts (All, each place, No place), a card per reading (tool, when: 'Today 09:42' / 'Yesterday' / 'Tue' / '12 Oct', name, value, detail, place) with Edit (name, place with the places as suggestions) and Delete (two taps, wears off after 5 s), Show what gets sent, Delete these / all N (two taps), and a sticky bar: Send N on WhatsApp (wa.me/?text= with no number, so they pick the chat) and Copy. messageFor(): '<place> · 22 Oct' (or '20 to 22 Oct'), the readings oldest first as 'Worktop, left run: 0.3°, raise the left end 3.1 mm over 600', for All with several places a block per place and No place last, then 'Measured with the free spirit level from Raf Carpentry / rafcarpentry.com/tools/spirit-level'. Empty: 'Nothing saved yet.' with Open the spirit level. The level page's How to use it has Hands free and Hold and Save steps. Fits 390 x 664 (an iPhone 13 with Safari's bars) without scrolling, flat with Speak on too (tighter under 700 px tall, tighter again under 600 in portrait), and 320 x 568. Tested (scratchpad level2/sums.mts, 38 checks on the words, tone sums, typed lengths and the message; level2/test.mjs, 102 checks in Chromium as an iPhone 13, at 320 and sideways, plus a computer, with made-up motion readings and stand-ins for sound and voice: dark panel, words and bubble, 4 bars and ticks at 0.3, slower at 1.6, quiet past 2, one chirp at level and again after the left end high, Tone off quiet and kept, Speak said in the tap, the mm said once and not repeated, Level, en-GB, Big numbers fits 12.4 and the tick, own length refused at 49 and taken as 1,830 with the mm over it, second tap picks it, zero and 12.5 from it, flip check 0.4, Save from before the tap, name and new place, sheet stays while used and goes by itself, held reading saved, flat words and Speak, face down no Save, fits 390 x 664, two columns sideways, readings page chips, the WhatsApp text, preview, copy, rename, delete two taps and wear-off, All message, send bar on screen, delete all and the empty state, 320 with no sideways scroll, computer QR and no-sensor message, readings page noindex, no page errors). Not tried on a real phone yet: whether the iPhone speaks and the beeps come through (ring switch on). Since 25 Oct 2026 the same component is the Spirit level tile in the Workshop Tools app (see 'Workshop Tools app', One spirit level).
- **Workshop Tools app (25 Oct 2026, Raf: 'add Workshop Tool App to Tools in rafcarpentry, also figure out how to mingle and show this app on pages and maybe on emails sent to clients so they can install it for free on ios or android'; previewed on the `workshop-tools` branch, live since 25 Oct 2026 with One spirit level, Raf: 'Merge')**: the free phone app Raf designed on the 'Raf Workshop Tools' design canvas (artifact SCUdCH33cg1ySDUC1tN1em; ten tools: unit sizes, door hinge positions, drawer runners, wall panelling with picture frames / stairs / batten grid, flooring, stud wall, spirit level, mm to inches, calculator, My kit with Amazon links; saved jobs; Pro at £19.99 a year as a preview). **Source**: scripts/workshop-tools/Main.dc.html is a copy of the canvas file; `node scripts/workshop-tools/build.mjs` (no dependencies) turns it into src/lib/workshop-tools/app.generated.js (the template as a JSON tree in React prop names with static styles already objects, the helmet CSS, and the logic class `class Component extends DCLogic` copied word for word; 100vh becomes 100dvh and the outer box 100%). Never edit the generated file: change the canvas, copy the file over, run the script. src/lib/workshop-tools/dc-runtime.tsx draws it with React: DCLogic (state, setState, a redraw; a setState while drawing waits a microtask), holes looked up through the sc-for scopes, sc-if / sc-for as fragments (sc-for keyed by index), dynamic style strings parsed and cached. The app is drawn inside a **shadow root** (`:host{all:initial}` plus the canvas CSS), so the site's Tailwind reset never reaches it and it looks exactly as on the canvas; fonts self-hosted (public/fonts/workshop, latin, OFL, @font-face in src/app/tools/workshop/app/workshop-app.css, document level because Chrome ignores @font-face in a shadow root): since 25 Oct 2026 the website's Bricolage Grotesque, Geist and Geist Mono (Archivo and JetBrains Mono before, see New look). **Routes**: /tools/workshop/app (the app full screen, noindex, canonical the page; no menu, footer or floating buttons: LayoutShell isMiniSite) and /tools/workshop (the page: Open the app, InstallPanel for the phone it's on: iPhone Share then Add to Home Screen (on newer iPhones Share is in the menu beside the address bar), Chrome on iPhone, Android the browser menu, the browser's own Install button when Chrome offers beforeinstallprompt (src/lib/workshop-tools/install.ts listens as soon as it loads), a computer a QR code of rafcarpentry.com/app?utm_source=qr-web; PhonePreview with the home screen picture and, on a computer, Try it here (the app in an iframe); Ten tools in one; three screens; Put it on your phone for both; Questions; TradeNudge; no floating buttons). Opened from the home screen, /tools/workshop goes straight to the app. rafcarpentry.com/app redirects (307, next.config.ts) to /tools/workshop keeping ?utm_source. **Installable and offline**: public/workshop-tools.webmanifest (id and start_url /tools/workshop/app?utm_source=pwa, scope /tools/workshop, standalone, #f1f2ef (#f2efe7 until 25 Oct 2026), icons in public/images/tools/workshop: a cream carpenter's square with mm ticks and a blue pencil line on ink, 192 / 512 / maskable 512, apple-touch-icon; src/app/tools/workshop/apple-icon.png overrides the site's R for both routes). public/workshop-tools-sw.js (cache workshop-tools-v2 since the new look's fonts, scope /tools/workshop): keeps the app page, manifest, fonts and icons on install; navigations network first with the saved copy offline (any page in scope falls back to the app); /_next/static, fonts and icons cache first; keepOffline() posts the files the page loaded (performance entries) so they're kept, and from the app itself clears older versions' scripts. The app hands the canvas logic what only the page knows (state instStandalone / instOs / instCan, inst.installPrompt): its home card 'Keep it on your phone' gives the steps for that phone, an Install the app button when the browser offers one, and is hidden once opened from the home screen. **Pro**: shown on previews and locally; on www.rafcarpentry.com it's hidden until card payments are connected (proOnSale(): state proLive false), so Save works for everyone and the home banner reads 'Your saved jobs'; ?pro=test (sessionStorage rc-tools-pro-test) shows it on the live site. Saved jobs stay in the browser (localStorage rc-tools-pro-v1). **Around the site**: Tools page card under the planner (AppNudge 'card', src/components/AppNudge.tsx), Tools menu 'Free to use', footer Explore, site search ('Workshop Tools app', words like noggins, hinges, panelling), sitemap (the page only), /tips (More free tools chip and a line before TradeNudge), /calculator (a line under the calculator), the blog's Tips, SketchUp and CabinetOS posts (a line under Enjoyed this post?; job stories left alone), /ms card 'Workshop Tools App', and a P.S. in the thank-you email after Send to Raf (src/lib/brief-email.ts, rafcarpentry.com/app?utm_source=email-brief). **Stats**: the campaign's utm tags (pwa, qr-*, email-*, quote, booked, invoice, review, launch-email, gbp, groups) are now kept as their own source in the visit counter (sourceOf in src/lib/site-stats-server.ts, labels in SOURCE_LABEL in src/lib/site-stats.ts), so the Job Kit's Website stats show each one; visits from pwa are the installs being used. The plan for the rest (ServiceM8 email P.S. lines, QR codes on quotes, cards, stickers and the van, reels, targets) is in the 'Workshop Tools launch campaign' doc (artifact UhfcLwyFFuQrLrWkRFDHUb). Not built, waiting for Raf: a band on the home page and the tool used on each job page. **New look (25 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Workshop tools do you think they are ok or should we upgrade the look a bit show mockup', mockups (now beside new) on the 'Workshop Tools, new look' design canvas (artifact 23PHnLMd1ZUzsm9VkVEFpf), then 'Go'; previewed on the `workshop-tools-look` branch, live since 25 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: same tools and sums, the website's look. **Colours, fonts, corners**: the cream and navy became the site's paper #f1f2ef, ink #16191c and blue #2547d0 (a colour map over the whole file: UI colours only; timber, warning and green colours kept), Bricolage Grotesque titles, Geist text, Geist Mono numbers, square 2px corners, size boxes 52 px tall with 21 px numbers and a darker edge, the blueprint grid only in drawings and tiles (.gp). **Home**: Raf's R on ink, 'Workshop Tools / from Raf Carpentry', a Saved button (your saved jobs, with the count; 'Saved' word hidden under 360 px), 'Every size in mm. Works with no signal on site.', a **Carry on** card (the last sizing tool used and its answer, e.g. 'Carry on · Stud wall · Today 08:12 / 7 studs / cut each to 2324 / 3600 × 2400 wall / Open'), the tools in three groups of three with a line drawing each (Cabinets: Unit sizes, Door hinges, Drawer runners; Walls and floors: Wall panelling, Flooring, Stud wall; Quick tools: Spirit level, mm ↔ inches, Calculator), My kit as a row, the Pro row only where Pro is on sale (homeProShow), Keep it on your phone. **Answer first** on the six sizing tools: under the top bar a dark panel like the spirit level's (.ans: ink, faint blue grid, corner ticks .tk) with a context line, the big number (sized by its length, clamp to the screen), one key figure on the right, a grid of figures, foot lines and a flag (hinges: 4 hinges, door weight, cup centres 100 · 712 · 1284 · 1896, '2 moved to miss shelves'; drawers: 3 drawers, box inside 522, runner lines, outside width, side length, depth flag and warnings; unit: 2200 full height, doors 2 × 2096 × 496, the four inside sizes, the plinth + unit + infill sum; panelling frames: 8 frames, rail top 1000, lower and upper frames, moulding and rail lengths; stairs: 12 frames, pitch 41.6°, frames on the flight 811.1 × 597.2, mitres 65.8° / 24.2°, rail mitre, what to buy; batten grid: panels across, opening sizes, batten; flooring: 10 packs, to order 17.82 m², rows, last row; stud wall: 7 studs, cut each to 2324, timber, sheets, noggins, insulation and screws), built per tool in renderVals with mkAns() (v.ansHinges, ansDrawers, ansUnit, ansGrid / ansFrames / ansStairs, ansFloor, ansStud; with bad inputs '–' and the tool's own error in amber). The old dark result grids are gone (the panel shows the same figures). The drawing comes straight under the panel (stud wall and the three panelling Elevations, drawer Mark-out on the side panel, unit Mark-out, flooring's rows across), then the inputs; panelling's Picture frames / Stairs / Batten grid switch sits above the panel. Once the panel has scrolled up under the top bar a one-line strip hangs below the bar ('7 studs · cut to 2324 · 12 × 2400'; .strip, absolutely placed so nothing jumps; ansScroll() on the scroll box compares the panel's bottom with the bar's, setHead / setAns refs). Titles: 'Door hinges' (was Door hinge positions). Drawings are sized to the screen (drawMax(): 330 on most phones, less on narrow ones, redrawn on resize; the batten grid ran 36 px off the side at 320 before). **Remembered**: every change in a sizing tool is kept on the phone 700 ms later (localStorage rc-tools-last-v1 {screen, at, snap}: every input of every tool, not Pro, jobs or the level), and the app opens with them (restoreLast: only keys and kinds today's defaults have), which is what Carry on shows and opens. Fixed with it: the Pro screen's Monthly / Yearly switch was dark grey on the dark panel. Stills retaken (screen-home-e87ea5ad with Carry on, screen-stud-fb359b7d, screen-stairs-5304eb3f, screen-calc-89fdcad9, og-f7e8dfbc; the old ones deleted); /tools/workshop says the answer comes first and the app opens where you left off; /privacy names 'the sizes you last typed and the jobs you save in Workshop Tools'. The canvas's Main.dc.html was updated to match (version 35). Tested on a production build (scratchpad look/test.cjs, 150 checks as an iPhone 13 with Safari's bars, at 320 and 1280: header, three groups, ten tools, no Carry on on a first visit, fonts loaded, every tool's answer against the figures before the change (look/base.json), the drawing next, panelling switch above, strip after scrolling and gone at the top, a new length changing the answer, kept on the phone, an error in the panel, Carry on after a reload and opening the tool as it was, the level tile, no sideways scroll, no page errors; look/test2.cjs, 60 checks: the strip on all six tools, the other screens, the calculator, Save via Pro, the saved toast and count, the plan switch readable, offline with the new fonts in workshop-tools-v2, a computer's 520 column; look/page.cjs: /tools/workshop, /tools and the level page with the new stills; the earlier wt/level.mjs 53 checks still pass). **Batten grid from the floor or above the skirting (4 Nov 2026, Raf: 'In wall panelling batten grid add height option from floor, from skirting so it can be space between skirting and bottom part or no space if type 0'; previewed on the `batten-grid-skirting` branch, live since 4 Nov 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: Height and battens starts with a From the floor | Above the skirting switch (state pgFrom 'floor' (default, as before) | 'skirting'). Above the skirting adds SKIRTING HEIGHT (the same pfSkirt as Picture frames and Stairs, so one skirting for the room) and GAP ABOVE (pgGap, default 0; empty counts as 0; 0 = the bottom rail sits right on the skirting), and the height box reads TOP FROM FLOOR: pH is always the top of the panelling from the floor, so the grid is pH less skirting less gap (panelCalc returns H, G, base, SK, GP, fromSk; at least 150 of grid, else 'Only N mm from the bottom rail to the top...'; below 0 refused). A blue line under the boxes says 'Grid 880 high: bottom rail at 120, top at 1000 from the floor. 20 mm gap above the skirting.' (or 'Gap 0: it sits right on the skirting.'); the answer panel's foot 'Bottom rail at 120, top at 1000, 20 above the skirting'; the elevation draws the skirting band, the gap and the grid from the floor up; a new 'Heights from the floor' list under the uprights (top of every rail, top down, middle rails counted from the bottom, then the bottom rail's underside and the skirting top; from the floor 'Bottom rail, on the floor 0'); the note says where the bottom rail sits. Tested on a production build (scratchpad grid/test.cjs, 108 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1280: the floor figures unchanged (5 panels, 517.2 x 862), skirting 100 gap 0 gives 762 openings, gap 20 gives 742 and the bottom rail at 120, empty gap 0, negative refused, a 900 skirting too high, three rows' middle rails (459.3), labels on one line at 320, the skirting shared with Picture frames, kept after a reload, back to the floor, no sideways scroll, no page errors). In embed mode there is no start card; EmbedNote (`.lv-embed` in spirit-level.css) covers asking, a refusal ('Close the app, open it again and tap Allow') and no sensor (a computer: a QR code of rafcarpentry.com/app?utm_source=qr-level, made in app/page.tsx); Done goes back to the tools; Your readings (the top bar bookmark and the Save sheet's All readings) closes the level and opens ReadingsSheet over the app (`.wt-readings`, the same ReadingsList with onBack, whose 'Back to the spirit level' starts the level again), so nothing leaves the app's scope and it works offline. The canvas file still has its own level screen (shown on the canvas only). The level's page /tools/spirit-level is unchanged apart from a line linking the app (AppNudge 'Also in Workshop Tools'), and on both a computer's refused requestPermission (Chrome has it) now counts as no sensor rather than 'turned down'. - **Workshop Games (25 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Make mockups and make them interesting visually as well with signal or beeps, show mockups first', rounds of changes on the 'Workshop Games mockups' design canvas (artifact QyVeSY4hvFSkxLg253HSC5: tape made understandable, bigger boards and parts, Nail It redone as Nail Pop, level screens and more levels), then 'Go'; previewed on the `workshop-games` branch, live since 25 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: four free games in one installable, offline web app, like Workshop Tools. **Games**: Measure Up (daily: five tapes picked by the London date, the same for everyone, harder through the week (more hidden, no grid at the weekend); today's result kept so it can't be replayed, a streak; four practice packs), Sheet Saver (daily board = days since 5 Jul 2026, so 25 Oct 2026 is board 112, made by a seeded guillotine-cut generator with plain / grain / saw-cut rules; boards 1 to 30 to work through, 31 to 40 'coming soon'; Show me shows one way, not scored, with Now you try and no Share), Timber Stack (10 levels from seat height to ten metres plus the free stack), Nail Pop (9 levels from a hallway floor to the whole house, 30-second rounds, three squeaks). Sounds made in code, one AudioContext for the whole visit (games-app.tsx shares the boards' _c / _ac), vibrate on Android. **Source**: scripts/workshop-games/*.dc.html, one per screen (home, stack, stack-levels, nail, nail-levels, measure, measure-packs, sheet, sheet-boards; copies of the canvas boards made ready for the app: hash links, raf_games_* keys, makeFit stages); `node scripts/workshop-games/build.mjs` turns them into src/lib/workshop-games/boards.generated.js (never edit it) with the parser shared with Workshop Tools (scripts/dc/convert.mjs; scripts/workshop-tools/build.mjs uses it too, output unchanged), drawn by src/lib/workshop-tools/dc-runtime.tsx in a shadow root. Each screen was designed at 390 x 844 and has a mode in build.mjs: fill (the games: full height, up to 520 wide, laid out at 390 and scaled down on narrower phones with --fs, the stage inside scales itself), contain (Sheet Saver: the whole screen scaled with --fit), scroll (home and level screens: width-fit, the page scrolls, --h). **App** /tools/games/app (src/app/tools/games/app/: page.tsx noindex, canonical the page, statusBarStyle black-translucent, viewportFit cover; games-app.tsx: the screen from the hash (#/stack, #/nail/levels ...) so Back goes back a screen; --fit/--fs/--h from the room inside the safe areas; a phone on its side in a game gets 'Turn your phone upright to play' with Back to the games; ?play=<game>:<level> (playKey in site.ts) picks the level or board before the game opens and comes off the address, utm kept; ?levels=all / none opens every level for trying them; shareScore; install glue for the home card; games-app.css: @font-face for Bricolage Grotesque, Geist and Geist Mono from public/fonts/games (copies of src/fonts, OFL), the blueprint box). LayoutShell isMiniSite; no floating buttons on any /tools/games page. **Share**: each game's Share passes its score ({g:'stack', n, h} / {g:'nail', n, lv} / {g:'measure', m, d or p, res} / {g:'sheet', b, t, n}); readScore / scoreWords / scoreUrl in src/lib/workshop-games/site.ts (no imports) make the link https://www.rafcarpentry.com/tools/games/beat?g=... and the message ('I stacked 9 boards of timber, 0.47 m high, in Timber Stack. Can you beat it?', 'I knocked 14 nails flush on level 3 of Nail Pop (Loft boarding)', Measure Up with a row of ■□ squares per tape like its result card, 'Sheet Saver, today's board (112): all 6 parts on in 1:24'); the phone's share sheet straight from the tap, else copied with a toast. **Score page** /tools/games/beat (noindex, canonical /tools/games; '<title>. - **Level Best, the fifth game (27 Oct 2026, Raf: 'in workshop games can we add a game where you have to balance the phone and the ball on screen goes right left top ... connected with woodworking and contemporary look with multilevel game', playable mockups on the 'Workshop Games mockups' canvas (artifact QyVeSY4hvFSkxLg253HSC5, version 13: how it plays, the game, the boards, and the home tile), then 'go'; previewed on the `level-best` branch, live since 28 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: tilt the phone and a steel bearing (r 8) rolls round a timber board; pick up three brass screws, drop it in the brass hinge cup, then hold the phone level (under 2.5 degrees, 0.8 s, 1.5 s on board 10) to seat it; over 15 degrees in the cup jumps it out. Stars: 1 for seating, +1 all three screws, +1 at or under par. **Ten boards** (LEVELS in scripts/workshop-games/level.dc.html, each with its own wood from WOOD): 1 Offcut (birch, learn the tilt), 2 Knotty pine (knot holes), 3 Hinge holes (white MDF door, only the brass cup is home, 21 and 35 mm marked), 4 Worktop (oak, slippy oil, sink and hob cut-outs), 5 Dovetails (walnut, angled walls), 6 Pegboard (fifty holes), 7 Sawdust (ply, slow heaps, quicker along the grain), 8 Magnetic catches (steel catches grab it, cable grommets), 9 Router table (phenolic, two moving cutters), 10 Straight edge (beech bench, a steel rule across the bench gap); pars 22 to 34 s. Falling in a hole or touching a cutter puts it back on the last pencil mark passed (a drop). **Physics** (per frame, at least 4 substeps, more on slow frames): acceleration 3000 x sin(tilt) (ACC), tilt clamped to 25 degrees and smoothed; friction per wood x 0.92 (GRIP), sawdust 5.5, oil 0.14, grain makes it quicker along than across; magnets pull within 64 (760 at the catch); a hole pulls once the bearing is over its edge and drops it past 0.25 r in; walls are line segments with a 0.32 bounce; speed cap 700 (MAXV). Quicker since 28 Oct 2026 (Raf: 'ball could roll a bit faster'; was 2400, friction x 1, 620 and 560: about a third quicker at the same tilt). Sounds made in code (no rolling sound: previewed on the `level-best-roll` branch with the quicker ball, both live since 28 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Merge' (Raf: the first one, filtered noise, was 'like a sea'; five other rolling sounds were rendered for him, then 'Maybe just loose this sound and only create beeping when near the finish hole') the bearing rolls quietly and beeps like a parking sensor once it is within 170 px of the cup's middle (NEAR): short sine beeps from 520 Hz at the edge up to 780 Hz at the rim, the gap 400 ms at the edge down to 70 ms (sounds() in level.dc.html, timed with performance.now()); then the cup's own chime and the level beeps at 880 Hz and up; scratchpad lbsound/render3.cjs renders it with the game's own code; knocks, screw tink, plunk, cutter whine, beeps quicker as it nears level in the cup, a chord when seated), buzz on Android. **Motion**: Start ('Set zero and start') asks for motion inside the tap (iPhone DeviceOrientationEvent.requestPermission), waits up to 0.9 s for a reading, then plays by tilt with the way it's held as level (Set zero any time), else drag on the board (a stick from where the finger went down) or the arrow keys / WASD; a tablet's turned screen is allowed for (screen.orientation.angle); the screen is kept on while tilting (Wake Lock, let go when seated or the game closes); the intro says to turn on rotation lock. **In the app**: screens `level` (fill: the 358 x 520 board scales to the room with makeFit, 0.8 on an iPhone 13 with Safari's bars; the mode line is wg-foot, hidden under 760 tall) and `level/boards` (scroll: ten cards with a drawing of each board, best time or par, 'Needs a star on N' when locked; a star on a board opens the next; ?levels=all opens them all) in scripts/workshop-games/build.mjs; the home screen has a wide Level Best tile under the four (a mini board with the bearing rolling into the cup, NEW until a star, then '★ N / 30 · 10 boards'; the other tiles' pictures went from 104 to 92 px and the gaps from 14 to 10 so the home still fits 844, Nail Pop's line is '9 levels, one house'). Kept on the phone: raf_games_level_level (the board), raf_games_stars_level, raf_games_best_level (best seconds per board), and the shared raf_games_sound. **Share**: {g: 'level', b, t (tenths of a second), s (stars)} (src/lib/workshop-games/site.ts LEVEL_BOARDS, readScore, scoreWords: 'I rolled the bearing home on board 4 of Level Best (Worktop) in 0:18.4, 3 stars. Can you beat it?', score page 'Board 4 in 0:18.4. Can you beat it?' with Play board 4 = ?play=level:4#/level; playKey 'level:1' to 'level:10'); the score picture (beat/card/route.tsx levelArt) is a birch board with the route dashed into the cup and a level vial. **Around the site**: 'five games' on /tools/games (Five games in one row of five on a computer, a still of board 1, a question 'How does Level Best know which way I tilt?'), its sharing card's words, the score page, the app's and manifest's descriptions, the Tools page card line, the Tools menu ('Workshop Games, five free games'), site search (tilt, balance, ball, bearing, marble, maze). Stills: screen-level-5364dfef.jpg and a new home still screen-home-eae6e783.jpg (the old 22a164ea deleted). Tested on a production build (scratchpad lbapp/test.cjs, 80 checks as an iPhone 13 (since 28 Oct 2026 also: no beeps far from the cup, rising beeps on the way in, nothing playing on a loop) with Safari's bars, at 320 x 568 and at 1280, with made-up motion readings: the home tile and the home still fitting 844, the intro and rotation tip, the game fitting with no scrolling, motion asked once in the tap, the screen lock taken and let go, tilting right and down, Set zero, board 1 played start to finish by tilt with an autopilot (three screws, cup, seated), stars and best kept, Share's link and words, a knot hole and back to the mark, a pencil mark remembered, jumping out of the cup and seating it, the boards screen's locks, a locked board staying shut, ?play=level:7 and a bad one, the shared sound setting, all ten boards drawing and playing, one sound player, offline, drag and arrow keys on a computer, sharing copied with a toast, the score page, its picture under 300 KB, a broken score, Play board 4, the games page, search index and manifest, no page errors; the earlier gamesapp/test.cjs 120, taps.cjs and sheetdrag.cjs still pass). Not tried on a real phone yet: whether the tilt feels right on an iPhone (sensitivity, the 25 degree clamp), the motion prompt, the screen staying on, and the sounds with the ring switch on.
- **Find the Difference, the sixth game (29 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Find the difference. Create something smart', then 'make fomo when clock is nearly finish', 'make it with plenty levels', each tried on a test page (artifact KWa1FnMz8pLpDLyEyD7ca6, version 3), then 'I love it add to games merge'; previewed on the `find-the-difference` branch, live since 29 Oct 2026)**: two pictures of the same room, SIGNED OFF (the drawing the customer signed off) over AS FITTED (the job as fitted); tap a snag on either, hold a finger for a 2.4x loupe (drawn from a 4x copy), a wrong tap costs 3 s, hints ring the area in blue. **Rooms**: drawn in code on canvas (scripts/workshop-games/diff.dc.html: buildWardrobe / buildAlcoves / buildKitchen / buildOffice / buildHall, seeded mulberry32), the snags are changes to the room's parts (cands(): a handle, a door, a shelf, a lamp, a picture, a tool left out...; labels like 'Door should be flat', 'Lamp switched off', 'Tape measure left out'), each measured by a pixel diff and graded easy / medium / hard by how many pixels change, never under the tags, kept 9 px apart, no label twice and at most two handle snags, and every changed pixel checked to be inside a snag's box (makeJob tries 10 seeds). **60 levels** in six rooms of ten (Bedrooms, Living rooms, Kitchens, Home offices, Hallways, The whole house mixing them; spec(n): 5 snags rising to 7, 90 s down to 70, 2 hints then 1 from Hallways, harder snags later; seed 7919 n + 31337); a level opens once the one before has a star (?levels=all opens them all). Stars: 1 for all found, +1 no hints, +1 with half the time left. **Today's job**: 5 snags in 90 s, the room by the London date (board = days since 5 Jul 2026, so 29 Oct 2026 is job 116; seed 100003 board + 977, room KINDS[board % 5]), the same for everyone; only the first go counts. **The last 15 seconds** (Raf's 'fear'): a heartbeat that gets louder and quicker, the clock pulses and shakes, red edges, the snag list turns red ('3 STILL OUT THERE'), the story at 15 / 10 / 5 s (the customer's car pulls up, footsteps on the drive, key in the door), then TOO LATE stamped across the picture, a red flash and the missed snags ringed in red; calmer with reduced motion. **In the app**: screen `diff` (fill; the stage of both pictures and the list scaled to the room, 0.77 on an iPhone 13 with Safari's bars); the levels open over the game from the grid button (clock stops while they are open), each room with a picture drawn by the game. Kept on the phone: raf_games_stars_diff, raf_games_best_diff, raf_games_diff_days (the last 60 daily results), raf_games_level_diff (the level or 'daily' picked; ?play=diff:7 or diff:daily sets it, and a picked level opens even before it is reached, as Level Best does), raf_games_open_all, raf_games_sound. **Home**: a wide 'Find the Difference' tile under Level Best (two little rooms, a lime ring popping on the snag; 'Spot the snags · 60 levels', then 'Today’s job done · ★ 12 / 180'); six games and the 'Keep it on your phone' card no longer fit 844, so the home is 920 high in the browser and 844 once installed (home.dc.html homeH; the screen tells the page with this.setScreenHeight(h), games-app.tsx screenH sets --h). **Share**: {g: 'diff', l (level, 0 for daily), d (daily job), f, n, t (seconds), s (stars)} (site.ts DIFF_CHAPTERS, diffRoom, readScore, scoreWords: 'I found all 5 snags on level 1 of Find the Difference (Bedrooms) in 0:24, 3 stars. Can you beat it?', 'I found 2 of 5 snags on today’s job (116) in Find the Difference before the customer walked in. Can you beat it?'; Play level N = ?play=diff:N#/diff, Play today’s job); the score picture (beat/card/route.tsx diffArt) is the signed-off bedroom over the fitted one with the snags ringed lime (found) or red dashed (missed) and a dot per snag. **Around the site**: 'Six games' on /tools/games (two rows of three on a computer, the picture beside the words; Daily tag), its description, sharing card words, the app's and manifest's descriptions, the score page, the Tools card, the Tools menu ('Workshop Games, six free games'), site search (find the difference, spot differences, snag). - **Workshop Games in the Job Kit's Website stats (6 Nov 2026, Raf: 'In workshop games I have seen big activity, is it possible to see which games have been used and being popular and how many downloads', then 'Yes' to counting each game; previewed on the `games-stats` branch, live since 6 Nov 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: what the stats showed first (25 Oct to 6 Nov): Australian schools, found through ChatGPT and posted for classes on Google Classroom (Tamworth NSW, about 110 minutes on 6 Nov), East Kimberley College's Compass and the WA Education Department's portal; the 8 home-screen opens were all Raf's own phone; no one else had installed it; no score shared. **Each game its own page**: the games are one page with the screen after '#', so SiteStats now counts a page view per screen: statPath() in src/lib/site-stats.ts turns /tools/games/app#/stack into /tools/games/app/stack (stack, nail, measure, sheet, level, diff, with /levels, /packs or /boards; anything else is the games' home, /tools/games/app); a hashchange starts a new view (prev = the screen before); a jump like #brief on another page isn't a new view (the first send has its own timer, firstSend, so it isn't lost). pageHref() turns it back into the address for the Job Kit's heatmap iframe and Open the page; gameScreenName() names them ('Workshop Games: Timber Stack, levels'). Before 6 Nov every game counted as /tools/games/app. **Taps inside the apps**: Workshop Tools and Games are drawn in a shadow root, so a tap's words (and link) now come from the button inside it (event.composedPath()), while its place stays on the app's box for the heatmap; after the first 50 taps of a view only buttons and links are kept, up to 80 (cleanHit keeps 80), so a Share after a long game still counts. **Installs**: the first time Workshop Games or Workshop Tools opens from a phone's home screen (display-mode standalone or navigator.standalone), SiteStats tags that visit pwa-games-new / pwa-new (homeTag; the phone keeps the day in localStorage raf_games_home / rc-tools-home), every open after that pwa-games / pwa as before (also when iOS reopens without ?utm_source); both tags in CAMPAIGN_TAGS and SOURCE_LABEL. iPhones keep a home-screen app's storage apart from Safari's, so a first open there is the install; Raf's own phone counts once. **Data**: Supabase migration 'rafsite_games' (new function only, applied 6 Nov 2026): public.rafsite_games(p_secret, p_days) over rafsite.visit_rows: totals (visits that played the app, visitors a day, time played, visits to /tools/games, home-screen opens, installs, shares = taps on the app whose words start Share, score pages opened, visits that came from a score link or the QR), per game (visits, people, opens, time, levels screens opened, shares; 'home' = the home screen and every game before 6 Nov), sources (how each visit began: kind and the referring host, e.g. google via classroom.google.com, chatgpt via a school portal), places, daily, the latest 30 visits with their games. /api/job-kit/stats 'report' brings it as `games`. **Job Kit**: Website stats has a Workshop Games band after Blog (GamesBand in src/app/job-kit/stats.tsx): Visits that played, Time played, Put on a home screen (with home-screen opens), Scores shared (with friends who opened one); Which games (time played and visits, a bar by time, share of the time, levels opened, shared; tap for the game's own heatmap); How they found them; Where they are; Latest players (place, when, how they came, the games, time, shared); a note that a school class on the same computers can show as one or two visitors. The Pages list names each game screen. /privacy names the first-open day kept in the browser and that each game counts as a page (updated 6 November 2026). - **Money pots, its own app at rafcarpentry.com/pots (29 Oct 2026, Raf: the claude.ai artifact version opened only inside the Claude app, 'not a separate app on screen', and a home screen shortcut to it showed claude.ai's Page not found in Safari, then 'go' to moving it onto the website; replaced the launcher of the same evening, branch `money-pots-app`)**: Money pots is Raf's money and deadlines app for Rafal Solutions Ltd (pots for VAT, corporation tax, PAYE, garages, materials, dividends and what he can spend; HMRC and Companies House deadlines with payment details; Starling balances; Ask Claude that records payments and marks bills paid). It came from the claude.ai artifact https://claude.ai/artifact/X5sV5mmaSZb32gF8zMuz5k (kept as it was; its data was copied across on 29 Oct). **Page**: src/app/pots/app.html, one HTML file (the artifact's page with a proper head, a PIN screen and a small stand-in for the artifact's window.claude), served by src/app/pots/route.ts (force-static, built at deploy, X-Robots-Tag noindex); edit app.html directly. The stand-in gives the page `use('db')` (doc / collection / set / update with a deep merge / delete / onSnapshot) over /api/pots, `use('sample')` (the conversation and its tools run in the page; each round goes to /api/pots/ask) and `use('user')`. A copy of the data stays on the phone (localStorage raf_pots_cache) so it opens with no signal ('No signal: showing what's saved on this phone'); saving with no signal says 'Couldn't save: no signal' and keeps nothing half-saved; changes from another device come in every 30 s, when the app comes back on screen and when back online. **PIN**: the Job Kit's (JOBKIT_PIN), typed once and kept on the phone (raf_pots_pin), sent as x-pots-pin (src/lib/pots-server.ts potsGuard). **Data**: Supabase cabinetos project, table jobkit.pots (kind 'book' = settings and chat, 'entry' = money in and out; data null = deleted; rev from jobkit.pots_rev; RLS on, no policies), through public.jobkit_pots(p_secret, p_action load|put, p) with the Job Kit's sync secret (migration 'jobkit_pots'); /api/pots: check, load {since}, put {docs}. Settings also hold cisContractors (the contractors who pay under CIS), used in Claude's instructions so no references or names are in the page itself. **Ask Claude**: /api/pots/ask passes one round to the Claude API with ANTHROPIC_API_KEY (the Job Kit's key, prepaid credit, not Raf's Claude plan), model POTS_MODEL or ANTHROPIC_MODEL or claude-sonnet-5-5 (claude-sonnet-5 if the account hasn't got it); the artifact's first 'rules' turn goes as the system prompt; photos are shrunk to 1600 px JPEG in the phone (up to 4); errors in plain words (ai-key, ai-limit 'Your Claude API credit has run out', ai-busy, offline, pin). **Home screen**: public/money-pots.webmanifest display standalone, start_url and scope /pots; icons public/images/money-pots/ (apple-touch-icon 180, icon-64, 192, 512; the navy pound sign over a cut board on mint, drawn in the scratchpad pots/icon). **Text size** (29 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Can we add A+ to Money Pots my sight is so weak'): A- and A+ buttons in the drawing's top line (46 px, beside the name and the Saved line) step every text 100 / 115 / 130 / 150 % (all font sizes in app.html are calc(Npx * var(--fz)); the big You can spend number, the A buttons and, past 115, the company name stay as they are), a 'Text size 130%' note, kept on the phone (raf_pots_text, set in the head before anything draws); at 150 the pots go one under another on a phone and at 130+ the drawing's buttons stack under 440 px wide (scratchpad potsapp/fz.cjs, 20 checks at 390 and 320: the steps, 1.5x headings, to-do lines and dock words, the number unchanged, no sideways scroll, nothing clipped in the drawing or on top of the buttons, the sheet, kept after a reload, back to 100). **Paid to HMRC** (30 Oct 2026, Raf: 'could i have also history of payments to hmrc vat corporation tax and paye'; Header: Rafal Solutions Ltd, Money pots, the sync line, A− / A+, and a The company | You switch (#today / #you; hidden on History and Rules; the dock's Today stays lit on You). **Safe to spend** counts every pound the company has: have = main + every Space (Free to Spend too, new `bank.spaces.free` and a field in Balances) and need = what the pots must hold (max 0 each) + the cushion; spare money in a Space counts, so the top line matches the Starling total (30 Oct: have £4,584.22, need £9,733.71, gap £5,149.49; the old way said £6,306.18 short). bankNow(): the typed balances plus entries added since (`bank.savedAt`, set when balances are saved; money in lands in main and moves to Spaces as marked Moved, money out comes out of its Space then main; Self Assessment never counts), so recording a payment or paying a bill never flatters the figure ('plus the N entries added since'). **Your Spaces**: a row of cards (a grid from 700 px), short ones first, then Free to Spend and the main account; tap one for a line about it (CT: last year's bill and this year's so far). **The calendar** (calItems / renderCal): groups Late, Today · date, This week, then each month; a date block per row; each item folds open to its pay box (gov.uk button, bank details with Copy, reference check, I've paid it), late items open by themselves once. (I've paid it, HMRC shows a new amount) and the pay details. - **Wardrobe planner (14 Oct 2026, Raf: 'online web configuration with standard width sizes and custom unit sizes', then 'add in first section plinth height and top infill and also free standing unit, go')**: /wardrobe-planner, live since 14 Oct 2026 (Raf: 'Go'). Files: src/app/wardrobe-planner/page.tsx (metadata), wardrobe-planner.tsx (client frame, imports the engine on mount so three.js only loads on this page), planner-engine.ts (plain DOM + three.js, `// @ts-nocheck`, ported from the Claude preview artifact 'Raf Carpentry Wardrobe Planner'; listeners and the render loop are removed on unmount), planner.css (scoped under .wp, site tokens, 2px corners; buttons are .wbtn so the site's .btn does not leak in). Five steps: Space (width, ceiling, walls both sides / open left / open right / freestanding with its own height, plinth 70/100/150/custom, top infill 50/100/150/custom scribed to the ceiling, depth 600 hinged / 650 sliding / 450 shallow / custom), Units (standard carcass widths 300 400 450 500 600 800 900 1000 or custom 150 to 1200; 'Fill my wall' picks standard sizes leaving 20 to 100 mm scribe fillers, or up to 150 mm spare when freestanding; live fit bar), Doors (hinged: one door up to 600 wide, a pair above; sliding; shaker / flat / mirror; handles; top cupboards from 2000 (2100 until 17 Oct 2026) when 250+ mm is left), Colour (preview only: built in MDF and left for the decorator, never says Raf paints), Send. Views: 3D (orbit, tap a door to open it, tap a unit to edit it) and Drawing (front elevation with every width, filler, plinth, door and infill height dimensioned, door-swing lines). All sizes, colours and limits are in CFG at the top of planner-engine.ts. Send uses Send to Raf: POST /api/brief {name, mobile, email, address: postcode, message, photos: 0, company (hidden box), ms, choices.what ['Fitted wardrobe']}; the message is in the brief format parseBrief reads ('Hi Raf, this is ...', What, Finish, Rough size, When, then the planner's lines and Notes), so the ServiceM8 job description is the full design. Needs name plus a mobile (10+ digits) or email. Tested locally with the request intercepted (nothing sent to ServiceM8) and the message run through parseBrief. No floating buttons on the page (LayoutShell); listed in the Tools menu 'Free to use', site search and the sitemap; the Fitted wardrobes service page has a 'Plan yours in 3D' button beside Get a quote. More ways in (14 Oct 2026, Raf: 'So it is not accessible from mobile? I can't find it'): its own line 'Wardrobe planner' in the phone menu after 3D designs (phone only; the computer menu bar is full at 1024), a 'Free planner / Plan your own wardrobe in 3D' row on the home page under the 3D chips (home.css .hero-plan-tool), the first card on the Tools page, and a 'Plan a wardrobe in 3D' button in the 'Nothing quite like yours?' box on /designs and /designs/wardrobes. With a picture (14 Oct 2026, Raf: 'should have a visual miniature little bit big on home page and tools page popup menu and inside page tools'): src/components/PlannerTeaser.tsx, 'row' (square still left, 'Free planner / Plan your own wardrobe in 3D / Your sizes, units and doors. Then send it to me.', sized by container queries so it fits the hero, the designs box and narrow phones) on the home page under the 3D chips and in the designs box (replacing the button); 'feature' (4:3 still with a '2800 x 2400 · 3 units' tag, 'Wardrobe planner in 3D', three points, Start planning) as the first card on the Tools page; the Tools menu panel on a computer shows the same picture as a link (MenuPanel `feature`, in place of the tool photo), the phone menu shows it at the top of Tools under the + (MenuFeatureRow) and a 48 px picture on the 'Wardrobe planner' line. The stills (src/lib/planner-picture.ts: public/images/tools/wardrobe-planner-<hash>.jpg 1280 x 960 and wardrobe-planner-square-<hash>.jpg 720 x 720) are the planner's own 3D view of its starting design with the doors open, taken with Playwright from a local run with the selection box patched out of the served script (scratchpad pic/still.mjs; FACTOR moves the camera back); new file names if they are ever retaken. **Side infill and unit sizes (14 Oct 2026, Raf: 'In units tab ... standard sizes, custom and equal split, in space can we have side infill where we can put in number')**: Space has 'Side infill' (not when freestanding): Auto (as before: whatever is left at the walls, warned outside 20 to 100) or Set the size (Left / Right infill number boxes, only the sides with a wall, 0 to 300 mm = CFG.sideInfill.max, CabinetOS's MAX_SCRIBE_FILLER_MM; switching to Set starts from what the drawing shows, so nothing moves until a number is typed). Units has 'Unit sizes' on one row: Standard sizes (width chips, Fill my wall; switching snaps each unit to the nearest standard width), Custom (a width box per unit, 150 to 1200) and Equal split (a Number of units counter; the width between the side infills and end panels is shared, the odd mm going 1 each to the first units, so it fits exactly; with the infill on Auto it uses 50 mm a side, CFG.sideInfill.def). Sums in calc(): fixedSides() (null = Auto), unitSpace(), equalRange(), equalise(); calc returns fillL / fillR (the side infills actually drawn, used by the 3D, the drawing and the CabinetOS payload) and, with fixed infills, warns '<n> mm gap to fill' / 'Too wide by'; the drawing's top dimension chain shows the gap as 'gap 40'. Fill my wall with set infills looks for standard sizes that leave the smallest sensible gap and says 'Equal split fills it'. The message to ServiceM8 gets a 'Side infill: 30 left, 80 right' line (or '(auto)') and '(equal split)' after the units; the design payload adds unitMode and sideInfill (CabinetOS ignores them and reads fillers, unchanged). Tested as an iPhone 13 and at 320 and 1280 (scratchpad units/test.mjs: seeded 50/50 fits exactly, left 30 gives a 20 mm gap, equal split 907/907/906, + to 4, right 80 follows, custom, standard snap, Fill my wall, open on the right shows one box, freestanding none, the sent payload and message). **Classic shaker doors (16 Oct 2026, Raf picked option 1 of six on the 'Shaker door options' design canvas: classic, two-panel, slim 40, top panel, tongue and groove, bevel)**: the planner's Shaker style is one panel inside a 70 mm frame on every door (CFG.shakerFrame, drawn at real size in faceMat), no longer two panels on tall doors; the Job Kit 3D designs (scripts/wardrobe/build.py) already drew shaker that way. The planner pictures were retaken with the new doors (since replaced by the clip's first frames, see Moving drawings across the site). The ServiceM8 key is production only, so Send fails on preview deployments by design. **Quick guide (16 Oct 2026, Raf: 'create simple visual guide for wardrobe planner next to planner for user to learn before start designing')**: src/app/wardrobe-planner/planner-guide.tsx. 'Quick guide / Five steps to your wardrobe / Rough sizes are fine: I measure properly on the free visit, and nothing is cut until you sign off the 3D drawing.', then the five steps (Space, Units, Doors, Colour, Send), each with a line drawing in the site's sketch style (graph paper, ink, blue for the dimensions, the picked unit, the door-swing lines, the picked colour and the send button), and 'In the 3D view' (drag to turn, pinch or scroll to zoom, tap a door, tap a unit, Drawing shows every size). It opens by itself on the first visit (localStorage `raf_wp_guide` = '1' once closed; storage blocked = every visit) and sits in the steps panel's grid cell beside the 3D view from 900 px; under 900 px it covers the 3D view too (rows 2 to 3) so it can be read. 'Start designing' (first visit) or 'Back to my design' closes it, as do Escape and the header button; the steps panel is `inert` while it is open. Header button 'Quick guide' ('Guide' under 400 px) with a ? icon, right of the title, blue while open; reopening focuses the guide title and keeps the step the customer was on. The view and steps markup moved into a memo'd PlannerFrame (wardrobe-planner.tsx) so opening the guide never re-renders what the engine has changed; the grid now places .view and .panel explicitly. Tested (scratchpad guide/test.mjs) as an iPhone 13, at 320, 768 and 1280: opens on the first visit, covers the right area, no sideways scroll, 3D still running, Start designing remembers, not shown after reload, reopen with focus, Escape keeps the Doors step, button toggles. **Moving drawings (16 Oct 2026, Raf: 'instead of pictures put some movable gifs')**: the five drawings are animated in CSS rather than GIF files (sharp at any size, almost no weight; planner.css .ga-* keyframes, 5 to 6 s loops): Space, a tape runs from the left wall to the right and the width is marked, then floor to ceiling; Units, a tap ripple on each unit in turn and the dashed selection box and its width follow; Doors, the pair swings open on its hinges (scaleX towards the hinge, non-scaling strokes) to show a rail and shelves, then closes; Colour, each chip is picked and the wardrobe takes its colour (colours passed from SWATCHES as --ga-c0..4); Send, the details are typed, Send is pressed, the arrow flies and the reply bubble comes back with its clock going round. Only with prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference; without it the base styles show the finished drawing. Lines drawn with a dash are hidden until they start (their round end would show as a dot). Checked frame by frame (scratchpad guide/frames.mjs pauses every animation at set times) and with reduced motion. **To CabinetOS (14 Oct 2026, Raf: 'connect to cabinetos.co.uk so the design is sent to my account', then 'Go'; live since 14 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: Send also carries `design` (designPayload() in planner-engine.ts: space, doors, colour, units, fillers); /api/brief, after the ServiceM8 job, sends it with the customer's name, postcode and the ServiceM8 job number to CabinetOS (src/lib/cabinetos-inbound.ts: POST CABINETOS_INBOUND_URL, Bearer CABINETOS_INBOUND_SECRET, 6 s timeout, best effort), where it becomes a project in Raf's account (cabinetos-site branch `website-designs`, src/app/api/inbound/website-design/route.ts + src/lib/inbound/websiteDesign.ts; see its AGENTS.md). Raf's 'New brief' email says 'In CabinetOS as "Website: <name>, <postcode>"' plus CabinetOS's notes (sliding doors come in as hinged, mirror/shaker as flat fronts, long hanging + drawers in one unit), or 'Not in CabinetOS (<reason>)'; nothing is said when the two settings are missing. - **Planner units are CabinetOS units (16 Oct 2026, Raf: 'how can we improve wardrobe planner with cabinetos taking into account cabinetos set of rules for creating units and presenting them', mockup on the 'Wardrobe planner, CabinetOS rules' design canvas, then 'Go')**: src/lib/cabinetos-wardrobe.ts (no imports) holds CabinetOS's rules (COS: 18 mm board and back, doors 3 short at the top and 4 off each door's width, max door 600, shelves 2 shorter than the space in all (1 each side; 2 each side until 10 Nov 2026, when CabinetOS made the gap count once) and 2 back, drawer gaps 3, rail 100 from the top, hinge clearance 30) and the planner's own choices (PLAN: long hanging 1400 clear, short 900, shelves at least 200 apart, 200 per drawer so 3 = CabinetOS's 600 zone, up to 8 shelves and 6 drawers, a pair needs 400 a half). unitLayout(box, inside) lays out one CabinetOS unit the way CabinetOS does (shelves with equal gaps over the clear space or above the drawer zone; under a short rail they sit at set heights sent as CabinetOS 'custom' offsets; none under a long rail or double hanging, as CabinetOS only shows shelves above a full-length rail; double hanging's lower rail halves the space under the top rail; drawers behind the doors (doors_drawers_internal) or below them (doors_drawers_external, doors shortened); doors and top-box doors placed as elevationDoorsMm) and gives the sizes as cut (doors, shelves, drawer fronts, box), the problems ('builds as drawn' or why not), whether one more shelf or drawer fits (a drawer may take a shelf out), and the CabinetOS settings (`cos`). planPieces / payloadUnits / runFor turn the planner's units into CabinetOS units ('Hanging beside drawers' = two units side by side, 2a long hanging hinged left, 2b drawers + shelves hinged right). Planner (planner-engine.ts): each unit is {w, inside: {rail, shelves, drawers, below}, split, edit}; the Units step opens one unit at a time (others fold to one line with a tick or 'Check'): width, Inside presets (Long hanging, Double hanging, Short hanging + shelves, Drawers + shelves, Shelves, Hanging beside drawers), 2a / 2b tabs for a pair, Hanging rail None / Long / Short / Double, Shelves and Drawers counters (+ off when it wouldn't build as drawn), Drawers go Behind / Below the doors (hinged only; sliding doors move them behind), the sizes ('In mm as I cut them') and 'Builds as drawn' or the problem. The 3D view, the drawing (the button under it is 'Show the inside' / 'Show the doors' in Drawing) and the sizes all come from the same layout; freestanding units now stand between two full-height end panels with no top panel over them (as CabinetOS cuts them). Send lists every CabinetOS unit ('Unit 2a: 450 wide, long hanging; door 446 x 2247 hinged on the left'), a Check line for any unit that doesn't build as drawn, and 'Every unit builds as drawn. It goes straight into CabinetOS, the app I cut from.' The design goes as version 2 (units[].pieces with `cos`), which cabinetos-site's mapper takes in as sent; version 1 still works there. Shaker doors stay flat fronts in CabinetOS (its Shaker Door is a unit of its own), so Raf's note lists each door size as a Shaker Door would cut it. `bash scripts/cabinetos-check/run.sh` (needs ../cabinetos-site with node_modules; N=, SEED=) runs random designs through cabinetos-wardrobe.ts and CabinetOS's own mapper, shelf / rail / drawer layout, front view and generateParts and fails on any difference (16 Oct 2026: 8,576 units in 3,000 designs, every shelf, rail, drawer, door and cut-list size equal; a deliberate 1 mm change to the shelf gap was caught). Run it after changing either side. **Shelf at 2000, dust shelf, Drawers + short hanging (17 Oct 2026, Raf: 'there should be shelf just above when drawers finish dust shelf, also usually on a height 2000 is a shelf or dividing unit where under you fit hanging pole for long or short hanging, and can we have also preset drawers with short hanging')**: (1) every unit with drawers gets CabinetOS's dust shelf straight on top of them (drawerBank.dustShelfEnabled, 18 mm, gap 0; cut as 'Dust shelf (above drawer zone)', the size of a shelf), and the space above starts on it (Layout.floor, .dust; 'Dust shelf' row in the sizes). (2) Any unit with a rail (long, short, double) gets a fixed shelf with its top face 2000 from the floor (PLAN.hatShelf; carcass height 2000 less the plinth) when at least 200 mm is clear above it (PLAN.hatMinAbove), and the top rail hangs just under it: its centre 20 mm under the shelf's underside, CabinetOS's own rail-under-a-shelf gap (COS.railBelowShelf = ANGLED_RAIL_GAP_BELOW_SHELF_MM; sent as positionFromTopMm). Without that shelf (low ceilings, freestanding units about 2000 high, top cupboards) the rail hangs the same 20 mm under the box top. In a tall room more shelves go over it so no gap is over 500 (PLAN.aboveGap; e.g. one more at 2900). These are CabinetOS 'custom' shelves (offsets from the shelf space bottom), so CabinetOS draws them above a long rail too. Top cupboards now start at 2000 (CFG.topSplit = PLAN.hatShelf), the same dividing line. Double hanging needs 850 under each rail (PLAN.hangDouble; was 900) so it fits under the 2000 shelf on plinths up to about 240. The texts say 'Long hanging under a shelf' (and '1 more shelf at the top'); the sizes have a 'Top shelf' row; the inside drawing has a 2000 dimension on the right. (3) New preset 'Drawers + short hanging' (drawhang: short rail, 3 drawers behind the doors, no shelves). (5) The picked unit (17 Oct 2026, Raf: 'I just don't understand this blue selection, can you hide it and when selecting a unit make sure to emphasise current selected unit'): the blue wire box round the selected unit is gone; only on the Units step, with 2+ units, the other units fade back a little (Raf, 06:09: 'just a little different than not selected so they all still look same'; highlight() in planner-engine.ts swaps their materials for lighter clones, emissive white at 0.2, kept in a WeakMap; build() and a step change re-apply it, so picking a unit no longer rebuilds the scene), and the drawing tints the picked unit only on that step. Picking (Raf: 'when pressed any where else then unit deselect'): on the Units step a tap on another unit picks it, a tap on the picked unit's door opens or closes that door, a tap anywhere else picks none (S.sel = -1, every card folded); the drawing does the same (drawHits); on other steps a door tap opens it and a tap inside a unit opens it in Units. The door button follows the pick (Raf: 'when unit selected doors open close button doesn't work'): 'Open unit 2' / 'Close unit 2' for the picked unit's doors, 'Open doors' / 'Close doors' for all when none is picked or on other steps; its label is worked out from the doors' state. Sliding doors play (Raf, 06:41: 'option for moving one by one with some delay ... just to see how they work and the current option'): with sliding doors a small play button (#playBtn, a triangle, a square while playing) sits left of the door button in 3D: each door slides open, stays 1.9 s, slides back, and the next follows 0.8 s later, once through; the door button ('Open doors' / 'Next door') still moves them one at a time, and pressing it, tapping a door or switching to hinged stops the play. The design stays on screen on a phone (Raf, 06:29: 'when selecting a unit ... design vanishes as page go up. I need the design to be visible always and just selections movable'): showSel() scrolls only the steps panel (it used scrollIntoView, which also scrolled the page and pushed the 3D view under the menu), and after typing a size the page goes back to the top if the keyboard pushed it up (focusout on the planner, under 900 px). The starting view (Space step) has no selection, so picture scripts no longer need the box patched out. - **Doors off for every door, and edge pulls (22 Oct 2026, Raf: 'in wardrobe planner we should have another button door off next to 3d button fitted smartly, also in mirror door advise and design pull handle door that are fitted on edge of the door and screwed to the board and not through the mirror like in ikea style'; branch `planner-doors-off-edge-pulls`)**: **Doors off** is a toggle in the top bar of the view, right of the size chip and left of 3D | Drawing (wardrobe-planner.tsx `.vtools`, `#offBtn.vtog`: the same height as the switch, a door icon and 'Doors off', blue with an icon of the inside while pressed; aria-pressed, title 'Take the doors off to see inside' / 'Put the doors back on'). It takes every door off, hinged, sliding and top cupboards (S.doorsOff, which replaced S.drawInside, so one switch serves both views): in 3D every door pivot and its handle are hidden (tracks and drawer fronts below the doors stay), taps go through to the units, the door button and the sliding play button hide; in the drawing it shows the inside ('INSIDE, DOORS OFF'). The drawing's bottom 'Show the inside' button is gone (the bottom right is empty in Drawing). Turning it off closes the doors first; changing the door type, style, handle or height puts the doors back on so the change shows. The old sliding-only bottom 'Doors off' button is gone. Narrow views (container query on .view, `wpview`): under 520 px the size chip drops '· 3 units' (chips() writes it in a `.dn` span), under 370 px the switch is the icon alone. **Edge pulls**: CFG.handles adds 'Edge pull' (chrome, `edge`) and 'Black edge pull' (`edgeb`); CFG.edgePull {len 100, stagger 110, slideLen 600}. On a hinged door it's a small metal pull screwed to the back of the board that wraps round the opening edge with a lip standing off the front to pull by (edgePull() in planner-engine.ts: back leg, strip round the edge, lip), so nothing goes through the face; on drawer fronts below the doors the same on the top edge (topPull()); on sliding doors a slim profile on the edge each door is pulled by, standing 4 mm proud so it clears the doors on the other track. Where two opening edges meet (a pair, the two halves of hanging beside drawers, two single doors side by side; meetOffsets()), the left door's pull sits 55 mm higher and the right one's 55 mm lower, so the two don't rub in the 4 mm gap (pullUp(): grip height 1050 on a tall door, near the bottom of a top cupboard's door, near the top of a low one). The drawing shows them as a heavy line on the edge (staggered the same). **Mirror rule** (CFG.throughDoor: bars and knobs bolt through the door): with Mirror the handle chips are only Edge pull, Black edge pull and Push to open; picking Mirror with a bar or knob switches it to the edge pull (black bar to the black one) and the fit chip says 'Edge pull: nothing drilled through the mirror'; other styles offer every handle and keep an edge pull. A note under the handle chips (pullNote()) says what an edge pull is, for mirror that nothing is drilled through the glass (or push to open), and where doors meet that one pull sits just above the other. Send and the brief say e.g. 'Hinged · mirror · edge pull'; CabinetOS takes the handle word as text (websiteDesign.ts) and doesn't use it. Advice given to Raf: for mirror glued to an 18 mm board, a wrap-round edge pull screwed into the back of the board (IKEA's OPPARYD, 45 mm, two screws 32 mm apart, is the one IKEA matches to its PAX VIKEDAL mirror doors); stagger a pair's pulls or allow a slightly wider gap; on sliding doors an edge profile for 18 mm doors (e.g. Emuca Adige, fixed by pressure, glue or screws, no machining). Tested (scratchpad edgepull/test.mjs, 26 checks each at 1280, iPhone 13, 360, 320, 1024 and 900: the switch beside 3D | Drawing at the same height and clear of the size chip, off and on, door button hidden, a tap through to a unit, still off after a rebuild, the drawing follows it with no bottom button, mirror leaves only edge pulls and push and picks the edge pull, the note, changing doors puts them back on, the drawing's staggered pulls where doors meet, shaker brings every handle back, sliding mirror note, sliding doors off hides play, Send lists it, no sideways scroll, no page errors; edgepull/shots.mjs screenshots).
