- **Got an IKEA PAX design? (23 Oct 2026, Raf: 'is there a way to connect with ikea pax planner on rafcarpentry website', then 'yes'; previewed on the `planner-ikea-pax` branch, live since 23 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: IKEA's PAX planner has no API and can't be embedded; a saved design gives a design code (IKEA's help: press Finalise, the code is at the bottom of the page; the share button gives a link or emails the code; any change makes a new code) that opens it again in IKEA's planner ('Open design', on a computer) or in store. So the customer sends the code and Raf opens it. **The sheet** (src/app/wardrobe-planner/pax-send.tsx, styles `.paxs-*` / `.paxlink` in planner.css): covers the planner's view and steps (both `inert` behind it; on a computer the drawing sits where the 3D view was and the form where the steps were, under 900 px one column), title 'Send me your IKEA design', × or Back or Escape closes it and focus goes back to what opened it; the planner's own design is left as it was. 1 Your IKEA design code (code or share link; how to find it; 'Open IKEA's PAX planner' link to ikea.com/gb/en/planner/pax-planner), 2 What you'd like from me (ticks: Build and fit it, Fillers to the walls, Up to the ceiling (top boxes or an infill), A plinth, My own doors (MDF, ready for your decorator); the first three start ticked) with a drawing of the PAX built in (PaxBuiltIn: walls, ceiling and floor hatched, three PAX frames 100 / 100 / 50 with IKEA's doors; what Raf makes blue with the scribe lines, what isn't asked for dashed ('gap to the ceiling', 'gap'); tapping a part of the drawing ticks it too; 'You order the PAX from IKEA. I make what's blue in the drawing, in MDF ready for your decorator.'), 3 Screenshots or IKEA's list (optional: up to 6 pictures, shrunk on the phone with the Job Kit's shrinkPhoto, and/or the product list pasted in), 4 Your space (width and ceiling, optional; taken from Space when they were typed there, S.sized), 5 Your details (taken from the Send step if typed there; name plus mobile or email). It needs the code, a screenshot or the list ('Add your IKEA design code, a screenshot or the list, so I can see your design.'). Send to Raf posts /api/brief like the planner (choices what Fitted wardrobe, material IKEA PAX built in, so the thank-you email picks PAX jobs) with **no `design`**, so nothing goes to CabinetOS; the screenshots go onto the ServiceM8 job as the brief's photos do (/api/brief/photo, /api/brief/done). Message (src/lib/pax-design.ts paxMessage, no imports): 'What: Fitted wardrobe', 'Wardrobe: IKEA PAX built in', 'Rough size: 2600 wide x 2450 high (mm)', When, then 'My IKEA PAX design (sent from the wardrobe planner):', 'IKEA design code: QX7K2M' (or 'IKEA design link: https://...', never a bare link line, which the pasted-brief reader drops), 'From me: build and fit it, fillers scribed to the walls, ...' (nothing ticked: 'not sure yet, advise me'), "Doors: IKEA's, in the design" unless own doors, 'Screenshots: 2 with this brief', "IKEA's product list:" with '- ' lines (40 lines / 1500 characters at most), Notes. **Ways in**: a slim 'Got an IKEA PAX design? Send me the code' row at the top of the Space step and a fuller one on the Send step (planner-engine.ts paxLink, data-act pax, opts.openPax), one in the quick guide under its lede ('Skip the steps: ...'), /wardrobe-planner#ikea-pax (opens on it without the guide, the guide not marked seen; the hash comes off on closing; a link to it on the planner page itself is caught, as Next changes the address without a hashchange), and site search 'Send your IKEA PAX design'. mountPlanner takes a fourth `opts` {openPax, bind} (bind hands the sheet the sizes and details typed in the planner). **Plan your project**: with IKEA PAX built in picked, the wardrobe card has 'Already designed it in IKEA's PAX planner? Add the design code.' (wardrobe-window.tsx WardrobeCard code / onCode, brief-builder ikeaCode); the message gets 'IKEA design code: ...' after the Wardrobe line, left out for made to measure. Tested (scratchpad ikea/sums.mts, 37 checks on reading codes, links, lists and sizes, what's missing and the message through parseBrief; ikea/test.mjs, 213 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320, 1024 and 1280 with /api/brief intercepted: the guide's link, the sheet covering view and steps with them inert, focus, starting ticks, ticking in the chips and in the drawing, asks for the code then the name then a mobile or email, two screenshots added, removed, added, the list, digits-only sizes, the body sent (contact, photos 2 then done, no design, choices, time on the page) and every message line, the done card, back to the planner, sizes from Space and details from Send, Escape, the guide button swapping, #ikea-pax, the brief's code box and message, no sideways scroll, no page errors; ikea/link.mjs: site search opens it on the planner and from /tools). ServiceM8 is production only, so a real send needs the live site.
- **CabinetOS assembly guide, step by step (21 Oct 2026, Raf: research and 3 mockups on the 'Wardrobe box assembly mockups' design canvas, then 'go' to upgrading the existing guide in mockup A's style, then 'merge'; live from cabinetos-site main 7a4aadd)**: in CabinetOS, Exports → Assembly → Stages for a wardrobe or combined unit is now What you need (the finished unit, every part to scale with its cut list code, size, qty and step; hardware counts; a Confirmat 7 × 50 at real size on paper; tools), then one 3D picture per step (new parts green with their codes, fitted parts grey, notes: diagonals, 2 people, hinge cup 35 mm at 21 mm, drawer box groove; shaker doors glued up first); the same in the printed guide (steps two to a row) and the Parts sub-tab lists the real codes. Built only from the unit's cut list rows and the hardware list: no calculations changed (Raf asked). Details in cabinetos-site AGENTS.md 'Assembly guide, step by step'; check: `bash scripts/assembly-steps/run.sh` there. **Every unit type (21 Oct 2026, Raf: 'can we adapt this for all units?', then 'merge'; live from cabinetos-site main 03b4eea)**: TV unit and chest of drawers (worktop step with its overhangs), wall unit ('Hang it on the wall', the height of its bottom from the floor, 2 people), angled unit (flat top and slope on, the pentagon back, doors cut to the slope, all drawn to their real outline), alcove unit (one tall box with the fixed shelf and inset doors, or the deeper cupboard with the bookcase stood on it), night stand (end panels before the worktop, drawers, the door under them; no wall step), shaker door (rails into one stile, panels in, second stile and square, hinge holes) and divided wardrobes (divider and fixed shelf, then each side's own steps) all get the step-by-step guide on screen and in print, English and Polish. Still built only from each unit's cut list rows and hardware list, no calculations changed. Checked on 4,000 random units of every type, at 1440, 390 and 320 and in print. The old stage list now only shows for a unit with no cut list rows. Found and NOT fixed, waiting for Raf: a combined unit with tall doors and outside drawers gets its doors cut full height over the drawer fronts; an angled unit with the back over the carcass has its sides cut full depth but the bottom, flat top and slope 18 short, so the back sits 18 proud behind the sides; an angled unit's end panel on the low side is cut full height while its filler is cut to the low side; a divided wardrobe with push to open has its latches counted twice in the hardware list; the divider's screws aren't in the hardware list; Exports' cut list renumbers codes when a part is hidden, while the Cutlist tab and board labels don't.
- **Moving drawings across the site (16 Oct 2026, Raf, after the planner guide's moving pictures: 'its not static and draws attention, i think we need to use this approach and all static designs on website to make it more interactive')**: src/app/motion.css (loaded in layout.tsx) + src/components/MotionObserver.tsx (in layout.tsx). A drawing's wrapper has `data-motion`; the observer adds `data-play` while at least a quarter of it is on screen (IntersectionObserver, a MutationObserver picks up drawings added later), and `[data-motion]:not([data-play])` pauses every `mv-*` animation, so nothing moves off screen and each drawing acts out as it scrolls in. Every loop is 8 s with the action in the first 1 to 3 s; 0% and 100% of every keyframe are the drawing as drawn, so paused, before the script runs and prefers-reduced-motion (animations only inside `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: no-preference)`) all show it finished; `.mv-hidden` parts (camera flash, old skirting) are hidden at rest. `--mv-d` on the wrapper staggers a list (Sketch `delay`, RuleSketch `delay`, CompareSketch: the With side 1.2 s after Without); `--mv-o` on a part sequences parts (`o(s)` helpers); `at(x, y)` + `.mv-vb` turns a part about a point; `.mv-ol/.mv-or/.mv-ot/.mv-ob` set where a part grows from. General moves: mv-draw (a line draws again from its start: needs pathLength={1}; not on dashed lines or non-scaling strokes), mv-pop, mv-show, mv-beat, mv-drop, mv-in-right, mv-grow-x/-y, mv-swing, mv-tilt, mv-wiggle, mv-nudge, mv-spin, mv-flip, mv-flash, mv-twinkle, mv-close / mv-settle (by --mv-dx/--mv-dy), mv-swing-door (+ mv-away for labels while doors are open). CSS transforms override an SVG transform attribute, so a moving part never carries its own transform= (wrap it). Done: all 18 drawings in src/app/how-it-works/sketches.tsx (Plan your project need/get lists, Workshop tips, Tools cards): photos (focus and flash), sizes (W then H measured from the middle out), uses (coats swing), look (picture rocks, heart beats), details (a car reverses into the space under the P sign; the car is now part of the drawing), budget (ring moves day to day, coin flips), reply (lines typed, clock beats), visit (tape runs out, quote ticked), drawing (signed, ticked), board (hatch drawn line by line, 18 mm pops), fitted (scribe line drawn, sparkle), skirting (W measured out), backSkirting (unit off the skirting, skirting goes, unit pushed back), compass (runs down the wall copying it), hinge21 (cup bored, 21 measured), screw (driven in), hingeJig (drill comes down onto the jig, hole shows), stack (board slides in, offcut drops); the 8 CabinetOS Without / With drawings (cross-out and redo, parts dotted then waste hatched, size crossed out, scrawl and mug ring / list fills and ticks, parts drop in packed, 600 on a clean line, labels come out); the 6 sizing rules (box closes, box lifted off its plinth and back, back pops in, shelf pulled out and back, doors open with the gap callouts hidden, edge tape runs round); and the three service drawings (src/components/ServiceDrawing.tsx, door fitting / flooring / stud walls, ported from scripts/service-drawings.mjs, which still makes the JPEGs used for sharing pictures; shown in their place on /services cards and the service page's 'Photos of these jobs' block via serviceDrawingFor(image)): the door swings open and shut and W is measured, the floorboards draw in and the blue board drops in, the plasterboard comes off while studs rise and noggins go in, then goes back on with its screws. The wardrobe planner picture moves too (src/components/PlannerClip.tsx): a 5.2 s silent loop of the planner's own 3D view, doors opening and closing, over the still in PlannerTeaser (home page row and the Tools page card; the menus keep the still): loaded only within 200 px of the screen, paused off screen, shown only once it is playing (fades in), never for reduced motion or when a phone refuses autoplay (the still stays). Files (src/lib/planner-picture.ts): wardrobe-planner-loop-square-fe064ec5.mp4/.webm (480 x 480, about 35 KB) and wardrobe-planner-loop-fe814901.mp4/.webm (960 x 720, about 64 KB); the stills are the loops' first frames (wardrobe-planner-square-fe064ec5.jpg, wardrobe-planner-fe814901.jpg; retaken 17 Oct 2026 with flat doors and the shelf at 2000, the older e4df2c75 / 525a18cc and 8f575966 / b6318656 were deleted). Made with scratchpad clip/clip.mjs (planner page patched like pic/still.mjs, the guide marked as seen, then Playwright's page.clock: 0.8 s closed, Open doors, 2.9 s, Close, 1.5 s at 25 fps; FACTOR 0.98 square, 1.02 for 4:3) and clip/enc.sh (starts the loop on an open frame so the still and video match; H.264 CRF 26 veryslow faststart, VP9 CRF 38, no sound). Checked frame by frame on every page (scratchpad motion/frames.mjs pauses all animations at set times and makes a sheet), motion/play.mjs (as an iPhone 13, 320 and 1280 on /how-it-works, /cabinetos, /tips, /tools: nothing off screen moves, what is on screen plays, no sideways scroll, no page errors; reduced motion: no animations, flashes hidden, lines whole) and motion/clip-test.mjs (the loop plays over the still on / and /tools, pauses off screen, reduced motion downloads nothing). Still static on purpose: the to-scale drawings on the CabinetOS page (the example wardrobe and the parts list), the Job Kit icons, button icons, and the 'woodwork motion' Raf had removed (section joints, pencil strokes under headings, ruler lines, page fades).
- **Spirit level on the home page (17 Oct 2026, Raf: 'when page scrolls level moves, when stop levels to 0 giving green check mark on side', mockup 'rafcarpentry.com · scroll it' on the Rafal Solutions design canvas, then 'perfect do it')**: under the hero title a spirit level (src/components/ScrollLevel.tsx HeroLevel + scroll-level.css: mount-white bar with an ink outline and end holes, vial tinted blue with two marks, white bubble ringed in blue, a Geist Mono reading, a green tick). While the page scrolls the bar tips with the scroll speed (down = clockwise, up to 2.4 degrees) and the bubble runs to the high side (up to 20 px), with a springy transition; 240 ms after the page stops it settles to 0.0 (blue) and the tick pops in. Once the hero level has gone up under the header, a small copy (MiniLevel, 56 x 18 + a 16 px tick) takes the place of the 'Raf Carpentry' name beside the R and does the same (the name stays for screen readers; the menu bar is full from 1024 px, so it can't sit beside the name). State in src/lib/scroll-level.ts: one passive scroll listener (sums once per animation frame) attached only while a level is on the page, and a dock flag the navbar listens to (so other pages get no scroll listener). Reduced motion: no listener, the level sits level with its tick. Pictures only (aria-hidden). Tested (scratchpad rclevel/test.py) at 390, 1024, 1280, 360 and 320 and with reduced motion: tilted while scrolling, level and tick after stopping, docked in the header past the hero, gone again at the top, no sideways scroll. **Every page (17 Oct 2026, Raf: 'can we have it on every page when scroll down switching with Raf Carpentry name next to logo like in home page')**: on every other page with the menu the small level takes the name's place once the page has scrolled 160 px, and the name comes back at the top (Navbar.tsx: an invisible 160 px marker at the top of the page watched by an IntersectionObserver, so no scroll listener until the level shows; the home page still docks by its big level). Tested (scratchpad also/level.mjs, 83 checks) on /about, /portfolio, a service page, /blog, /contact, /designs, /tools and / at 1280, iPhone 13 size and 320, and with reduced motion: name at the top, level after scrolling, tips while scrolling, settles with the tick, name back at the top, no sideways scroll, no page errors.
- **Mini Raf beside the logo (13 Nov 2026, Raf: 'mini me walking kick the log and a cupboard pop up', then 'make some variations of units, maybe running and making new stuff or dancing make it work infinite units', mockups on the 'Mini Raf logo animation' design canvas (artifact TehTE3kYzEwc12jJsN3k5U: close up, phone and computer headers, Endless, Six variations), then 'Go'; previewed on the `mini-raf-logo` branch, live since 13 Nov 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: each time a page opens, a little Raf (blue top, cap, tool belt) plays once in the name's place beside the R (7 s, MINI_RAF_MS): he walks, runs or dances in, kicks a log, sawdust puffs and a piece pops up (cupboard, chest of drawers, wardrobe with clothes on the rail, bookcase whose books pop in, bedside table whose lamp lights, TV unit whose screen comes on) with its width as a blue dimension line (600, 800, 1000, 600, 450, 1800), its doors or drawers open and shut, he cheers, then it fades and 'Raf Carpentry' fades back in from 6.3 s. Each page gets the next round (src/lib/mini-raf.ts miniRafVariant: six pieces, three ways in, six colours (sage, off-white, navy, oak, pale blue, slate), 36 before one repeats; the round is kept in localStorage raf_mini_raf, memory only if storage is blocked; the first ever is the sage cupboard, walking). Tapping the R on the home page plays the next one. Drawing src/components/MiniRaf.tsx (one svg, 240 x 80, aria-hidden, data-mini-raf / data-mini-raf-act; moving parts turn about their group's 0 0, never a CSS transform on an element with a transform attribute), moves src/components/mini-raf.css (classes mraf-, so Tailwind never reads them as margins; every keyframe a percentage of the 7 s; made from the canvas's Variations board). Navbar.tsx plays it (requestAnimationFrame after each new pathname, so a background tab waits until it's looked at): while it plays the name keeps its place see-through and the scene sits over it (120 x 40 on phones, 132 x 44 from 768, 120 x 40 between 1024 and 1279 where the menu bar is full, so it never touches Services); it wins over the docked spirit level, which comes in after it. Not for reduced motion, and not under 350 px wide where the header has its own Get a quote (the name makes way there). /privacy's browser line names it (updated 13 November 2026). Tested on a production build (scratchpad mraf/test.cjs, 79 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320, 1024 and 1280: cupboard first and kept, the name hidden then back after 7 s, inside the header and clear of every button, the next page the running chest, scrolling while it plays then the level after it, back to the name at the top, home the dancing wardrobe and the R playing the bookcase without leaving home, reduced motion nothing kept, 320 with the header's own quote no scene, 390 clear of Get a quote, storage blocked still changing piece, no sideways scroll, no page errors; mraf/frames.cjs paused it at set times for each piece).
- **Mini Raf's show beside the name, phones and tablets (13 Nov 2026, Raf: 'make sure he is walking beside the Rafcarpentry name and if scrolled down level pop up and he is still in same section ... after he kicks log new unit pop up ... seamless ... he is dancing then go and make another unit, run and make another, crawl and make another do some jumping jacks make a wardrobe or media wall ... interactive animation', then 'Phones and tablets only' and 'Show, then he rests'; previewed on the `mini-raf-show` branch, live since 13 Nov 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: under 1024 px the header has a room between the name and the buttons (Navbar.tsx `stageRef`, flex-1, `lg:hidden`, 40 / 44 px high); when it is at least 104 px wide (STAGE_MIN in src/components/MiniRafStage.tsx; capped at 248) Raf's show plays there instead of the page-by-page round, and the name stays. **The show** (about 39 s, src/lib/mini-raf-show.ts buildShow, worked out from the time alone by frameAt so it can carry on from any moment): he walks in and kicks a log, a piece pops up; dances beside it (hops, a turn, a disco point, notes rising, the piece bouncing to the beat); walks to the next log and kicks it; runs to the next; crawls on all fours to the next; four jumping jacks; walks to the last log and kicks up the big one (both arms up); then waves by it, and the next show starts by itself (see 'Forever' at the end). Going to the next log he walks on the spot while the floor (hatched ticks), the pieces he has made and the next log move past him, so he never leaves his place; on tablets the pieces line up behind him into a row, on phones the older ones fade off at the left. Pieces per show n: the cupboard, chest and bookcase taking turns first, then a bedside table and a new **media wall** (2400: TV over an electric fire, shelves with books and a strip light, cupboard doors; MediaWall in src/components/MiniRaf.tsx, in PIECES with the others) or a TV unit and the wardrobe, every other show; colours move on one a piece and one a show. The pieces are the rounds' own drawings with their own CSS keyframes, started from the moment each pops up (`--mraf-at` on the piece, rules at the end of mini-raf.css); Raf is a jointed copy of the round's figure moved each frame by attributes (place and turn, lean, bob, legs, the top half about the hip for crawling, arms), the log flies along the rounds' arc. Positions are measured from the scene's right edge, so nothing moves when the room changes. **Scrolling**: with the show on, the spirit level docks over the name (which goes see-through but keeps its place) instead of beside it, so Raf never moves. **Taps**: Raf skips to his next trick (in the wave at the end, to the next show); a piece plays its trick again (doors, drawers, books, the lamp, the TV); a tap while paused plays on. A 24 px button at the right end pauses and plays it (WCAG 2.2.2). It pauses while the tab is hidden (CSS too, `data-paused`), carries on from page to page (the Navbar stays put; the time is kept for the tab in sessionStorage raf_mini_raf_now, so a reload carries on), and each new tab plays the next show (localStorage raf_mini_raf_show). Never for reduced motion. Computers, and phones with no room (320 px), keep the page-by-page round in the name's place. /privacy's browser line names both keys (updated 13 November 2026). For tests the stage puts `window.__miniRafShow` {now(), seek(t)}. Tested on a production build (scratchpad show/sums.mts, 203 checks on the timeline: lengths, acts, smoothness frame to frame, the floor, each log flying into its piece, logs coming in off the right edge, crawling, jacks, the dance, skipping, replays; show/test.cjs, 182 checks as an iPhone 13, at 375, 430, 768, 844 sideways, 320, 1024 and 1280: beside the name and clear of every button, the cupboard after the kick, carrying on to the next page, the level docking over the name with Raf not moving, back at the top, a tap on him skipping to his next trick (a real touch too, not leaving the page), a tap on a piece replaying its trick, pause stopping the clock and every CSS move and play going on, resting at the end with the clock stopped, still resting after a reload, a tap starting show 1 from the last piece with a chest first, a reload mid-show carrying on, a hidden tab pausing, a new tab playing the next show, reduced motion nothing kept, 320 the round as before, computers the round and no room, storage blocked still playing, no sideways scroll, no page errors; show/real.cjs real-time frames of the whole show at 390 and 768). **Forever (13 Nov 2026, Raf: 'can it be played infinitely while someone is on site'; previewed on the `mini-raf-forever` branch, live since 13 Nov 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: when a show ends the next one starts by itself (the next n, new pieces and colours), so he plays for as long as the page is open; the button still pauses him and a hidden tab still stops him. The next show starts with him walking on from the last four pieces he made (buildShow's `legacy` is a list now, oldest first, `legacyOf(show)`; ids `<n>-old`, `<n>-old-1` ...), standing where they stood, so on a tablet the row behind him doesn't vanish at the join; a tab saved by the version before (one piece, or past the end of a show) goes on to the next show. The floor's marks are 15 apart (TICK in MiniRafStage.tsx; they were 16), so a new show starts on the same marks. The clock is a layout effect, so a new show's first frame is set before it's painted (it flickered for a frame on tablets). Each new show moves the visit's count on (raf_mini_raf_show), so a new tab plays the one after. Tested on a production build (show/sums.mts 324 checks, with six hand-overs in a row at four stage widths: Raf, the floor and every piece on screen where they were; show/test.cjs 202 checks: the next show starting by itself at the end, the button still pausing, the same pieces at the same places on screen, the clock going on, a tap in the wave starting the next one, an old tab's saved show going on; show/seam.cjs: the hand-over recorded frame by frame three times at iPhone 13, 430 and 768, every piece moving under 1.3 px a frame and none vanishing).

- **Home page motion (23 Oct 2026, Raf asked what Framer animations could do for the site; mockup 'Raf Carpentry motion mock-up' design canvas (home page with 9 effects and notes), then 'I like them all do it'; previewed on the `home-motion` branch, live since 23 Oct 2026, Raf: 'merge')**: nine pieces, each drawn finished before its script runs and for prefers-reduced-motion (the scripts only add the movement); styles in src/app/home-motion.css (imported by page.tsx) unless named. framer-motion (already a dependency, never used before) is used where springs or dragging help: ReviewTicker, TiltShot and Magnetic, each inside `LazyMotion features={domAnimation}` with `m.*`. The scroll-linked ones share src/lib/use-scroll-fx.ts (useScrollFx: one passive scroll listener per piece, switched on only while it is within half a screen (IntersectionObserver), fx writes CSS variables straight onto elements so React doesn't re-render while scrolling; `watch` lets a card in a sideways row watch its row). (1) **Hero**: the headline is split into words (page.tsx Words, `.hw` with --i) that settle in one after another (CSS only; the h1 lost its `.anim d1`); two sage doors (#a8b8a0, the build story's doors; inset panel line, ink bar handles) cover the photo of the day and swing open on their hinges to 95 degrees while the photo eases back from a 1.12 zoom (src/components/HeroDoors.tsx: no class = the first opening as a CSS animation, 0.6 s in, 1.8 s; `.hd-shut` when the photo is under 30% on screen at load (phones: held shut until it scrolls up); `.hd-open` each time it comes back after going right off screen; aria-hidden, pointer-events none, display none for reduced motion). (2) **Facts**: src/components/CountUp.tsx counts each number up from 0 (1.4 s) when 60% on screen, again after it has gone right off; a strip already on screen at load keeps its numbers; the moving copy is aria-hidden with the real value in an sr-only span. (3) **What I build** is now src/components/ServiceStack.tsx (replaced the tile-a/b/c bento): from 861 px three horizontal cards (photo 5/12, text 7/12, `min(440px, 100vh - 150px)` high) stick under the menu bar at 96 / 118 / 140 px and the one underneath shrinks 5% and darkens (shade up to 42%) as the next comes over it (--sp); the title link covers the card (`.sc-link::after`); phones and tablets get a plain list of cards. (4) **Latest jobs**: src/components/WipeCover.tsx shows the job's 3D still (modelOf(p).poster, object-fit contain on graph paper, multiply) and a blue line wipes it into the cover photo while the page scrolls: it starts as the bottom of the picture comes into view and is done when the top of the picture reaches the top of the screen (the bottom of the menu bar), all four cards together (23 Oct 2026, Raf: 'start scrolling when bottom of picture is visible and finish when top of the picture reaches top'; it ran from 95% to just under half way, card by card, before), with '3D drawing' and 'Built' tags; sent as the plain photo (stage 'still'), so jobs without a 3D model and reduced motion look as before. (5) **Where the jobs are** (JobsNear `bars`, home only; /areas keeps the chips): the 8 busiest areas as blue bars scaled to the top count that grow out one after another (spring, 70 ms apart) when 30% on screen, again after going right off; the rest stay as chips under them. (6) **Reviews** (Reviews `ticker`, home only; job pages and Plan your project keep the grid): src/components/ReviewTicker.tsx drifts the cards left at 34 px/s in a loop (second copy aria-hidden, its links tabIndex -1), stops under the mouse, with focus inside or while a finger is down (1.4 s after), drags with mouse or finger (touch-action pan-y so the page still scrolls; a drag never opens the link it ends on) and carries on from a fling; stars pop in when it comes into view; only moves on screen; before the script and for reduced motion the cards are a row that scrolls sideways. 380 px cards, 300 on phones; edges faded with a mask on computers. (7) **CabinetOS** screen leans in 3D (src/components/TiltShot.tsx, springs): towards the mouse (up to 5 / 6 degrees) on hover-capable screens, with the scroll on touch screens (up to 6). (8) **Quote panel** on the home page: the short original (Get a free quote, the line under it, the three steps from home.json quoteNextSteps) with one button, Get a quote, to the top of Plan your project, where the full brief is (QuoteCta `quoteOnly`, home only; no WhatsApp me button, no WhatsApp lines and no 'Book a visit online' link there; /contact keeps all of them). The button leans towards the mouse (src/components/Magnetic.tsx, 20 px catch zone from 768 px with a mouse). home.json quoteNote is now 'Takes two minutes, and the quote is free.' A chips-and-message version (QuoteBrief) was tried on the preview and taken out the same day (Raf: 'it creates confusion, I prefer the previous short original redirecting to the full form'); brief-builder.tsx still reads every ?what= / ?room= / ?for= and ?when=. (9) **Plan your project pill** (HowItWorksFloat, every page): folds to its icon (and the saved count) while the page scrolls and opens out 650 ms after it stops, the same rhythm as the level (globals.css `.hif`, `data-small`; one passive listener, state changes only on fold / unfold; the words stay for screen readers; never for reduced motion). Left out on purpose (Raf removed them on 9 Oct 2026): page fades, pencil lines under headings, ruler lines, joints between sections, the photo growing into the job page. Tested on a production build (scratchpad hm/test.mjs, 139 checks at 1280 x 800, as an iPhone 13, at 320 x 568 and with reduced motion: headline in 6 words reading as one sentence, doors' first opening on a computer and held shut then opening on a phone, doors never block the link, numbers 0 then 22 / 100+ / 18 / 24, reset and count again, screen-reader numbers, card 1 sticking at 96 and sinking as card 2 comes over, card 2 at 118, cards a list on phones, card link, jobs starting as drawings, the wipe only starting with the picture's bottom just in view, half done half way, all four together with the blue line, nearly done just before the top meets the menu bar and photos once it does, pictures loaded, bars empty then grown to their counts, 8 bars plus chips, ticker live, drifting, 10 cards with 5 hidden, stops under the mouse, drags, CabinetOS leaning to the mouse and with the scroll on phones, the quote panel with three steps and one link, no WhatsApp or booking, the button leaning to the mouse and full width on phones, Get a quote opening the top of Plan your project, pill folding and opening, no sideways scroll, no page errors; reduced motion: no doors, real numbers, plain photos, full bars, reviews as a row, pill never folds); hm/frames.mjs paused the hero animations at set times to check the swing (doors meet, open from 1.0 to 1.8 s); hm/shots.mjs screenshots of every piece at 1280 and iPhone 13; hm/others.mjs: /contact (three steps), /areas (chips), a job page (review grid), /how-it-works and /about unchanged, no sideways scroll, no page errors. The Motion chunk adds about 30 KB gzipped to the home page.

(1) **Drawing-to-photo wipe on job cards**: WipeCover is now generic (props className, imgClassName, priority, `row` = watch the ul, for the home page's sideways row; `low` = tags along the bottom, clear of the heart) and is on every job card of My Work (JobCard has `model`, modelOf(p)?.poster.src from portfolio/page.tsx), the service pages and the area pages (`low` on My Work and area pages, which have the heart), same rule as Latest jobs: starts with the picture's bottom in view, done with its top at the menu bar. A card already on screen when the page opens keeps its photo until it has gone right off screen once (so the first row of My Work doesn't flash from photo to drawing as the page loads); the home page's Latest jobs is below the fold, so it plays as before. (2) **Counting numbers**: About's four stats (20+, 100+, 1, ∞; ∞ stays as it is) and the area pages' 'Jobs here' use CountUp. (3) **Where I work**: each row of 'Every place so far' has a thin blue bar (`.jn-bar-thin`) scaled to the busiest place, growing out as that row comes into view (src/components/GrowList.tsx: one IntersectionObserver on the rows, data-grow straight on each li, rows on screen at load left full, nothing for reduced motion). Fixed with it: on phones that list had been wider than the screen (a single implicit grid column sized to the longest job titles, the row about 590 px on a 390 px phone, so the page could move sideways); it's `grid-cols-1` now. (4) **Leaning screenshots**: TiltShot takes `mouseOnly`, `deg` and `as="span"`; the CabinetOS page's app screens (AppScreens, both the row on computers and the one-at-a-time frame) and the SketchUp page's Learn screenshots (ScreenGrid `tilt`) lean towards the mouse, up to 4 degrees, and stay still on touch screens (they're tapped to open full size). (5) **Leaning Get a quote**: Magnetic on the Get a quote in the bottom quote bands of the service pages, job pages, area pages, /areas, /tips and /designs ('Nothing quite like yours?'), and in QuoteCta on /contact (which keeps WhatsApp me and Book a visit online). Left out on purpose: the wardrobe doors on other photos (only right in front of a wardrobe), word-by-word headings on every page (too close to the page fades Raf removed on 9 Oct), stacking cards on /services (10 cards would be a long scroll). Tested on a production build (scratchpad mp/test.mjs, 160 checks at 1280 x 800, as an iPhone 13, at 320 x 568 and with reduced motion: every My Work card with a model wipes, tags low, cards on screen at load keep the photo and those below start as drawings, below the screen the drawing, half way half wiped with the blue line, photo with the top at the bar, the tag clear of the heart, both pictures in each card, search still filters, the same on a service page and an area page, Jobs here for screen readers and counted, About counting to 20+ / 100+ / 1 / ∞, 17 bars scaled to the busiest place, rows below waiting empty and growing to their counts on screen, CabinetOS and SketchUp screenshots leaning to the mouse and still on phones, the leaning Get a quote on every band and /contact keeping WhatsApp and booking, no sideways scroll, no page errors; reduced motion: plain photos, real numbers, full bars, still screens); hm/test.mjs (139) still passes. The sideways-scroll check in both now also compares the page's layout width with the real screen width, because a phone's layout viewport grows when something is too wide (the old check missed the /areas list); mp/wide.mjs and wide320.mjs sweep 17 pages at 390 and 320 for anything wider than the screen outside a sideways row (none).
- **Mounted card photos and the CabinetOS devices (23 Oct 2026, Raf: 'on main page in what i build section moving up pictures with side description make sure they have same white border like other pictures on site, also on same page in CabinetOs make the picture look from desktop Ipad and iphone with relevant device imitating borders'; previewed on the `home-mounts-devices` branch, live since 23 Oct 2026, Raf: 'merge')**: (1) What I build: each stacking card's photo now sits 10 px in from the card's edge (home-motion.css .sc-photo margin 10px 0 10px 10px; on phones 10px 10px 0), the white mount every other photo on the site has (.mount padding 10px); the hairline beside the photo went with it. Tested (scratchpad home/check.mjs, 177 checks at 1440, 1280, 1024, 390, 360 and 320, and with reduced motion: every card's photo 10 px in, the old single screenshot gone, all three screens loaded with alt text and their own shapes, every device on screen, tablet and phone in front of the computer, the group leaning to the mouse, no sideways scroll, no page errors; home/shots.mjs before and after screenshots).
- **Recently looked at card (22 Oct 2026, Raf, with a screenshot of another shop's 'Michael ordered ... 9 hours ago' pop-up: 'create a random pop up ... saying that someone has just looked at ... or liked ... it has to be discreet'; made-up activity was declined (misleading under UK consumer law, the CMA counts fake social proof), and he chose the real version: 'That even better because we have real visitors'; branch `recent-activity`)**: src/components/RecentDesign.tsx, in LayoutShell with the floating buttons (so not on Plan your project, the planner, the calculator, the tools with their own screens, /review, /d/, /editor, /ms, the Job Kit). Once per visit (sessionStorage raf_recent_shown; raf_recent_t0 = when the visit started), 20 s in (at least 2.5 s after a new page), not while they're typing (a key in the last 8 s with a box focused; tries again every 10 s, up to 6 times), it fetches /api/recent and shows one item picked at random, never the job page they're on: a small card bottom left above Plan your project (84 px up, 16 / 24 px in, up to 360 px wide), the job's 3D still (52 px), a blue dot and 'Looked at 40 minutes ago' / 'Looked at 3 times in the past week' / 'Saved for a brief 2 hours ago' / 'Saved for a brief 2 times in the past week' (Geist Mono 11 px), the title (2 lines at most), '<area> · see it in 3D', linking to /designs/<kind>#<slug> (the job page's #in-3d when it has no kind), and a Close ×. It stays 9 s (longer while the pointer or focus is on it), fades (none with reduced motion), and doesn't come back in that tab. With nothing real in the past week it doesn't come at all. **Data**: /api/recent (force-dynamic, private no-store; robots and headless browsers get an empty list) works out the asker's visitor code the same way as /api/stats/hit and calls public.rafsite_recent(p_secret, p {exclude, days 7}) (Supabase cabinetos project, migrations 'rafsite_recent' and 'rafsite_recent_saves_key'; SECURITY DEFINER, the visit counter's secret, executable by anon and service_role): views of /portfolio/<slug> in the last 7 days (last, views, visits = distinct daily visitor codes) and saves (taps carrying `sv`), both leaving out the asker and the parked 2000-01-01 test rows. src/lib/recent-designs.ts recentItems() keeps only real job slugs with a 3D model and picks the line (recentLine(): 2+ saves, then a save with how long ago, then how long ago it was looked at if under 48 h ('a few minutes', 'N minutes' to the 5, 'an hour', 'N hours', 'yesterday'), then 'N times in the past week', then 'in the past week'). **Saves**: SaveButton puts `data-save=<slug>` on a heart that's about to save a job (not on unsaving, not on customer designs); SiteStats adds `sv` to that tap; cleanHit keeps it (slug shape only). It's kept apart from the get-in-touch goals (`g`), so the Job Kit's Ways to get in touch and the pages' get-in-touch column don't count saves. The list of saved things still never leaves the browser (src/lib/saved.ts says so). /privacy (How visits are counted) now says the site sometimes shows a card that a job was looked at or saved lately, saying nothing about who or where and never your own visit. Raf's own visits count unless his browsers have the /privacy opt-out switched on. Tested (scratchpad recent/test.mjs, 42 checks at iPhone 13, 320 and 1280 with the pretend stats database's rafsite_recent (cutmail/stats-mock.mjs, POST /__recent to set the data): nothing at 15 s, the card at 21 s with a real line, an unknown slug left out, on screen with its gutter, above Plan your project and clear of WhatsApp, no sideways scroll, gone after 9 s, not again after a reload, a job page whose only item is itself shows nothing, held while hovered, Close, the link opens /designs/wardrobes#pax-wardrobe-finchley, none on /how-it-works or /wardrobe-planner, none with an empty week, waits while typing and comes after with the box still focused, a save tap carries the slug and an unsave doesn't, no page errors).
- **The card suggests the app or a blog post (25 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Is there a way to suggest new visitor to check workshop tool or blog discretely', then 'Go'; previewed on the `suggest-card` branch, live since 25 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: the Recently looked at card (src/components/RecentDesign.tsx) is still the only pop-up, once per visit with the same rules (20 s in, not while typing, 9 s, Close), but what it shows depends on the page, from /card-index.json (src/app/card-index.json/route.ts, force-static, built by src/lib/suggest-card.ts buildCardIndex(); fetched only when the card is about to show, about 11 KB): **trade pages** (/tools, /tips, /cabinetos, /sketchup and the SketchUp and CabinetOS blog posts): 'Free for carpenters / Workshop Tools: ten setting-out tools on your phone / Works with no signal · get the app' with the app icon, to /tools/workshop, once per browser and never to someone whose last pages (raf_seen) include /tools/workshop; **a job page**: 'How this one was built' with its own blog post (title, hero photo, 'Step by step, with photos · 2 min · read it'), and on the four PAX jobs the PAX guides after it; **the wardrobe service page, /designs/wardrobes and the four area pages**: the PAX guides in order (or made to measure, height, wall to wall) as 'Wardrobe guide · 4 min read'; **a PAX post**: the PAX guides it isn't. A post already in raf_seen or offered before (localStorage raf_card_shown, up to 30 hrefs) is skipped; with nothing left, or on any other page, the card is the real 'Looked at 40 minutes ago' job as before. Taps carry `cd` ('app', 'post:<slug>', 'recent'; SiteStats from the link's data-card, kept by cleanHit), apart from the ways to get in touch (`g`), so blog stats' 'got in touch after' isn't touched. Supabase migration 'rafsite_cards' (new function only): public.rafsite_cards(p_secret, p_days) counts them from rafsite.visit_rows; /api/job-kit/stats 'report' adds `cards` (with the posts' titles) and the Job Kit's Website stats has a 'Suggestion card (bottom left)' band after the cut list band (taps only, not how often it showed). /privacy's browser line names it (updated 25 October 2026). Tested on a production build (scratchpad card/test.mjs, 49 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1280 with /api/recent and the stats hit stood in: the app on /tools with its words and icon, on screen above Plan your project, the tap opening /tools/workshop and sent with cd app and no goal, not offered again next visit, none after the app page was seen, the app on the SketchUp post, a job page's own post, not twice, a PAX job whose post was read getting guide 1, then the wardrobe page guide 2, an area page guide 3, then the recent card, a guide post never offering itself, home and job posts as before, nothing real = no card, Close and once a visit, none on the full-screen pages, no sideways scroll, no page errors; card/jk.mjs, 18 checks: the band, its counts, titles and order at iPhone 13, 320 and 1440; card/clean.mts: cleanHit keeps app, post:<slug> and recent and drops anything else).
- **Pull down to search (22 Oct 2026, Raf, with a screenshot of the Claude iPhone app: 'when pull down page search turn blue and pop-up whole search and things I might be interested, can we do it on rafcarpentry similar feature?'; live since 22 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: src/components/PullSearch.tsx, mounted in Navbar (off while the phone menu or the computer's search panel is open). Touch screens only (`pointer: coarse`), every page with the menu except the full-screen tools (wardrobe planner, calculator, Timber Stack, spirit level). At scroll 0 a finger pulling down moves the page (`main`) down with some give (half the pull, slower past 140 px, at most 120) and a round magnifier comes out from under the header with a blue ring filling round it; at 58 px the ring is full and it turns blue (a short buzz on Android); letting go opens the search full screen, a short pull springs back. It doesn't start on the header, a box being typed in, a canvas or video (3D views), anything with `data-no-pull`, an open dialog, a box that is itself scrolled, a sideways swipe, two fingers, a zoomed page or when a viewer has locked the page's scroll. While it pulls the browser's own pull-to-refresh and bounce are held back: a touchmove listener that can cancel is attached only while the page is at its very top (an IntersectionObserver on a 1 px mark), so scrolling anywhere else stays passive. **The search** (SearchSheet): SiteSearch's new `sheet` layout (props `replace`, `idle`, `after`; the box with a blue border, Cancel beside it, results as in the phone menu), the keyboard up at once (iPhones only raise it for a focus made inside the touch, so a stand-in box is focused in touchend and the real box takes over), the sheet kept to what the keyboard leaves (visualViewport), and scrolling it puts the keyboard away. Under the Try words, all real: **Jump back in** (the last 4 pages this visitor looked at, kept in their browser only, localStorage raf_seen, up to 8, not the home page, /d/, /review, /privacy or the Job Kit; titles and pictures from the search list), **Saved for your brief** (the hearts, with Open your brief), **Looked at lately** (/api/recent: jobs other visitors looked at or saved in the past week, the same lines as the Recently looked at card, a 3D still each, sideways row), **Designs in 3D, by type** (chips with counts from the search list) and **Quick links** (Plan your project, Wardrobe planner, Jobs near you, WhatsApp me). It pushes its own history entry: Cancel, Escape or Back closes it; a result or suggestion opens with `replace`, in place of that entry, so Back from the page goes to the page underneath. The phone menu's search box has 'Tip: pull down at the top of any page to search.' (touch screens only). /privacy: the browser line now names 'the last few pages you looked at (so search can offer them again)'. Tested (scratchpad pull/test.mjs, 57 checks as an iPhone 13 and at 320 with CDP touch events, plus a computer: a short pull shows the magnifier and springs back, a long pull turns it blue with the page not scrolled, letting go opens the search with the box focused, the four kinds of suggestions, typing finds jobs, a result opens and Back returns to the page underneath, Cancel and Back close it, a Jump back in item, a scrolled page just scrolls, a sideways swipe and the header don't pull, the menu tip, saved items, Escape, none on the planner, no sideways scroll, no page errors; on a computer no magnifier and / still opens the header search). The real test is an iPhone: whether Safari keeps its own pull-to-refresh away and the keyboard comes up.
- **Website stats in the Job Kit, heatmaps and a privacy page (18 Oct 2026, Raf: 'I'd like to have stats page in jobkit showing these numbers to see traffic ... also if possible heat maps where people went through the website, which page they liked, where they stopped and where they decided to leave'; previewed on the `site-stats` branch, live since 18 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Live')**: Vercel's analytics can't be read from the site and has no heatmaps, so the site counts visits itself. **Counter**: src/components/SiteStats.tsx (in layout.tsx): one page view per page (Next client navigation included, the page before kept as prev), time in use (on screen and touched in the last 15 s, counted a second at a time), deepest scroll ((scrollY + window) / page height), time per twentieth of the page (by the middle of the screen), up to 50 taps (the element's CSS path back to the nearest id, where on it as fractions, page y and page height for a fallback, its label: aria-label or text, never typed words; goal quote / send / call / whatsapp / email / planner from src/lib/site-stats.ts goalOf), the referrer or ?utm_source on the first page. Measures only while its own page is showing (here()), sends with sendBeacon (text/plain) after 1.2 s, every 30 s in use, on a goal tap, when hidden or left. Not counted: /job-kit, /admin, /editor, /api, /review/send, pages inside an iframe (the Job Kit's heatmaps), robots, localStorage raf_stats_off = '1' (the /privacy opt-out; the Job Kit sets it on its own storage) and Global Privacy Control. Vercel Analytics now goes through src/components/VercelAnalytics.tsx with beforeSend dropping the same opt-outs and iframes. **Server**: /api/stats/hit (always 204; only on www.rafcarpentry.com unless RAFSTATS_ANY_HOST=1, so previews record nothing; robots and other sites dropped) checks and trims the view (src/lib/site-stats-server.ts cleanHit: device from width, <700 phone, <1024 tablet; source from the referrer or utm: google, bing, instagram, facebook, tiktok, youtube, whatsapp, directory... or 'internal'; country from x-vercel-ip-country; visitor = sha256(secret|London date|IP|user agent) first 16 hex, so no IP is stored and the code changes daily) and calls Supabase. **Database**: Supabase project cabinetos (uzrnnnnfdrqunyrysodz, Frankfurt; the free plan's two projects were both in use), its own schema rafsite (views table, config with the secret's sha256), RLS on with no policies and not exposed; only three SECURITY DEFINER functions in public, executable by anon, each checking the secret: rafsite_hit(p_secret, p) (insert, or update the same id within 12 h and same visitor; 1% of hits delete rows older than 400 days), rafsite_report(p_secret, p_days) (totals, daily, sources, countries, goals, pages with entries/exits, journeys of up to 4 pages) and rafsite_page(p_secret, p_path, p_device, p_days) (reach in 21 steps, dwell in 20 bands, up to 3000 taps, most tapped, next pages, came from); visits = the same visitor code on the same day, under 30 minutes apart (rafsite.visits). Four test rows were parked on 2000-01-01 (deletes need Raf's approval in the Supabase connector; the 400-day clean-up removes them). Vercel env (18 Oct 2026, production + preview): RAFSTATS_URL, RAFSTATS_KEY (publishable key), RAFSTATS_SECRET (sensitive; only its hash is in the database). **Job Kit**: src/app/job-kit/stats.tsx, Today screen band 'Website' -> 'Website stats' (line from the last numbers kept on the phone, raf_jobkit_stats), behind the PIN through /api/job-kit/stats ({action: 'report', days} | {action: 'page', path, device, days}). Overview: 7 / 30 / 90 days, visitors (a person a day), pages viewed, time on the site, left after one page, phone / tablet / computer bar, visitors by day (tap a bar), where they come from, ways to get in touch tapped, pages (most seen / longest read / most left from: views, time, read %, left here %, get in touch), routes through the site. A page: opens on the screen size most people used (Phone / Tablet / Computer chips with counts), numbers, then the real page in an iframe at 390 x 844 / 820 x 1180 / 1280 x 800 (never bigger than real, at most 72% of the window tall), dragged (or wheel, or the map beside it) to scroll, with a canvas layer: Taps (heatmap, amber to red; each tap placed on its element found again by the CSS path, or by page position when that element isn't shown at this size), How far (darker where fewer got, dashed lines at three in four, half, one in four), Time (bands by share of the time, labels over 3%); then most tapped, came from, went next. **/privacy** (new; the site had none though the brief collects names and numbers): who (Rafal Solutions Ltd), quote details (ServiceM8, Microsoft 365, CabinetOS, WhatsApp; the thank-you email; kept as long as the job needs, accounts six years), things kept in the browser, how visits are counted (both counters, 13 months, Supabase Frankfurt; PECR statistics exemption since 5 Feb 2026 (Data (Use and Access) Act 2025) with the opt-out), who else handles data, rights and the ICO. Opt-out switch src/app/privacy/stats-opt-out.tsx; footer legal line links Privacy; in the sitemap. Tested locally with a stand-in database (scratchpad stats/mock.mjs, sim.py five pretend visitors, jk.py the Job Kit screens at 390 and 1280, optout.py): the right page, title, height, scroll, time and taps per view, goals, journeys, opt-out stops both counters, no sideways scroll.

- **Where visits come from (22 Oct 2026, Raf: 'can I have this data in jobkit telling from which area someone looked at something'; live since 22 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: each page view now keeps roughly where it came from, as Vercel reads the connection: the town or city (x-vercel-ip-city, percent-decoded, letters only, else nothing), the region code (x-vercel-ip-country-region) and, in the UK only, the first half of the postcode (x-vercel-ip-postal-code cut to its outward code, e.g. N22; never more). placeOf() in src/lib/site-stats-server.ts; /api/stats/hit passes the headers to cleanHit and calls the new public.rafsite_hit_place(p_secret, p) (Supabase migration 'rafsite_places', 22 Oct 2026: columns city, region, district on rafsite.views; rafsite_hit_place runs the old rafsite_hit (secret check, insert or update, the 13-month clean-up) then fills the place the first time it's known; anon and service_role only). The migration has no DROP or DELETE (the Supabase connector cancels those): rafsite.visits stays as it was and the report and page functions read the new rafsite.visit_rows(since) (the same rows plus city, region, district; postgres only). rafsite_report adds `places` (towns by visits, each with its postcode areas), `placesFrom` (first day a place was kept) and `latest` (the newest 30 visits: when, place, device, source, time in use, pages in order with time on each, the get-in-touch goals tapped, saves); rafsite_page adds `places` and `recent` (the latest 15 views of that page, every screen size). **Job Kit** (src/app/job-kit/stats.tsx, types and placeName() in src/lib/site-stats.ts: 'London N22', 'Enfield', 'Warsaw, Poland', 'Somewhere in the UK'): Website stats has 'Latest visits' after Visitors by day (8 then Show all; each visit: place, Today 18:20, phone / tablet / computer, from Google / typed in or a saved link, time in use, the pages numbered with their time (tap one for its heatmap), 'and N more pages', chips 'Tapped WhatsApp' and 'Saved 1 for a brief') and 'Where they are' after Where they come from (towns with bars and the postcode areas under each, e.g. 'N22 7 · N13 1'); a page's stats end with 'Who looked at it' (every screen size: places, then Latest views with place, time, device, time on the page, how far read, and from where or after which page). Each says it's rough: home broadband usually shows the right town and postcode area, phones on 4G / 5G often only the network's hub (often London); nobody's name or street is ever known. Visits before the change show only the country. /privacy (How visits are counted) now names 'roughly where from (the country, town or city and, in the UK, the first half of the postcode, such as N22 ...)'. The Recently looked at card and pull-down search now say 'Built in Finchley', so the place reads as where the job was built, not where the person who looked was (Raf had read it as the visitor's place). Tested (scratchpad places/: the migration on a throwaway local Postgres 16 with the live schema (base.sql, migration.sql), supa.mjs answering as PostgREST on 4052, start.sh; place-test.mts 14 checks on reading the headers; test.mjs 53 checks: a real page view with Vercel's headers keeps Palmers Green N13 ENG, none without them, then the Job Kit as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1440: newest first, a 7-page visit folded, taps and saves, abroad, no town, 8 then Show all, London with N22 7 · N13 1, the band order, a visit's page opening its stats with Who looked at it and its latest views, no sideways scroll, no page errors). The live migration was checked on the real rows (visit_rows, the latest 30 visits). Places start filling only once this is live.

Route /api/job-kit/stats `{action: 'blog', days}` returns it with every post from content/blog with its topic and format (next.config traces content/blog and content/projects into the route). **Sums** in src/lib/blog-stats.ts (no imports): topicOf (IKEA PAX wardrobes first, then the job the post tells the story of (its type), then words in the title), formatOf (Projects = Job story; Tips, SketchUp, Cabinetos = Guide), postRows (every post, read or not; found = landings from Google, Bing, DuckDuckGo, Yahoo, Ecosia or AI; ai = ChatGPT / AI assistants), topicRows (posts, reads, reads a post, time, found), blogAdvice: at most four, in order: Write more about <topic> (best (reads + found) per post, if above the rest of the blog), <post> led to people getting in touch, ChatGPT is sending readers (answer in the first two lines, real sizes, London), Write it as the question people ask (guides' reads a post 1.5 times job stories', or Tell more job stories the other way), Most leave <post> early (read under 35% in under 30 s, from Google / AI or by 2+), Read to the end: <post> (copy its shape); under 20 reads it says 'Early days ... take these as hints'. **Job Kit**: Website stats has a Blog band before Pages (reads in the days, posts read of all, % to the end, found straight from Google, AI or a link, the top three posts (tap for the heatmap), 'Next: <first advice>', and 'Blog stats: who read what, and what to write next →'); the new screen Blog stats (screen 'stats-blog', src/app/job-kit/blog-stats-view.tsx; back to Stats; a post's page stats opened from it come back to Blog stats): 7 / 30 / 90 days, six numbers (Reads, Posts read of 38, Time on a post, Read to the end, Found on Google or AI, Got in touch after), What to write next (numbered, 'See how it's read' opens the post's heatmap), Posts (Most read / Longest read / Read to the end / Found on Google / AI; each: title, topic · Guide or Job story · date, reads · time · read % · to the end, how they arrived 'Google 2 · ChatGPT 1 · from other pages 1', places 'London N22 · Dubai, United Arab Emirates', 'N got in touch after'; tap for its heatmap) and 'Show the N not read in these days' (links to the posts), By topic (bars by reads, posts, reads a post, from Google / AI, time each), Who read them (newest first, 8 then Show all: place, when, the post, phone or computer, From ChatGPT / From Google / After <page>, time and how far, Then: <next page> or Then left the website, Tapped Send to Raf ...), Where readers are, and the place note. Kept on the phone (localStorage raf_jobkit_blog) so it opens with no signal. Fixed with it: a page's heatmap no longer throws when its screen is left while the page is still loading. Tested (scratchpad blogstats/: the function on the throwaway Postgres with 8 seeded visits, sums.mts 25 checks on topics, rows, topics and advice with the real 38 posts, test.js, 39 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1440 against it: the band and its line, top three and Next, the screen's numbers, the advice in order, seven read posts and their rows, every sort, the 31 not read, topics, who read them with places, arrivals and what next, tap through and back, 7 days, reopening on the screen, offline from the phone, no sideways scroll, no page errors). Live data on 23 Oct 2026: 14 reads of 6 posts since 18 Oct, 10 of them in visits that began on ChatGPT (PAX Haringey, PAX Finchley, kitchen panelling Crouch End), the rest from Google. **Quick opens aren't reads (25 Oct 2026, Raf, after the screen told him to write more about wall panelling and fix the Crouch End post: 'Yes' to ignoring opens under 5 seconds)**: 5 of the week's 15 reads were people closing a post within 1 to 2 seconds (the Crouch End panelling post 3 times from ChatGPT by 2 people), and they had pushed Wall panelling to the top of the advice. Supabase migration 'rafsite_blog_quick' (same function, nothing dropped): a view of a post in use under 5 s (engaged_ms < 5000, or no time sent) is a quick open, left out of every count, post, source and place and of the advice; the report adds totals.quick / quickPeople, quick[] (per post: n, people, last, from) and latest[].quick. The time-in-use counter starts at page load, so a still reader gets 15 s; under 5 s means the post was closed or hidden. src/lib/blog-stats.ts: QUICK_SECONDS, PostRow.quick, quickLine(). The screen: 'Not counted as reads: 5 quick opens by 4 people, closed within 5 seconds. That's usually a tap on a link and straight back.' under the numbers, '+1 quick open' on a read post's line, posts with nothing but quick opens under 'Opened, closed within 5 seconds' (not in the not-read list), quick ones greyed in Who read them ('closed within 1 s, not counted'), and the footer 'A read is one person opening a post and staying at least 5 seconds.' With it the live week read 10 reads, IKEA PAX first (7 of the 10), no 'Most leave ... early' item. Tested (scratchpad quick/: the old and new function side by side on a throwaway Postgres 16 with made-up views (5 quick, 4 reads, null time, exactly 5 s, a quick open before a quote); sums.mts 18 checks on the real 38 posts; ui.mjs as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1440 against it: numbers, the line, advice, posts, the quick-only list, Who read them, tap through and back, no sideways scroll, no page errors).

Now every page under the menu starts with one link in the same place, top left under the header (src/components/BackBar.tsx, rendered by LayoutShell above the page): came from another page on this site in this tab, '‹ Back to Fitted wardrobes', which goes back in the browser (history.back(), so they land where they were on that page; a new tab, Ctrl or middle click follows the link instead); arrived straight from Google, WhatsApp or a link, '‹ Services', the page above (upOf in src/lib/back-trail.ts: jobs to My work, posts to Blog, services to Services, 3D kinds to 3D designs, areas to Where I work, tools pages, tips, the workshop calculator, drawings and the planner to Tools, Your readings to Spirit level, a score page to Workshop Games, wardrobe prices to Fitted wardrobes, the top pages to Home). The tab keeps the pages it went through (sessionStorage raf_trail, 30 at most; LayoutShell's useBackTrail() notes every page, the apps without a menu too): a new page goes on, the one before it again is a back (taken off), a reload or back into the site cuts the trail to that page, and a fresh visit from another site starts it again. Names: a short name per page (NAMES), services, areas and 3D kinds from their address, jobs and posts from their heading. Materials start with plain "MDF" (£). FAQ (corrected by Raf, 3 Oct 2026): he DOES fit kitchens, and the customer should clear the room as much as possible. Nothing typed in the brief is stored on the website; it goes into the WhatsApp message or email, or (since 13 Oct 2026) straight into ServiceM8 with Send to Raf.
- **Plan your PAX in the brief (22 Oct 2026, Raf: people picking IKEA PAX "dont know what they want", so a window should pop up from Material with the units in their space, sizes, doors below and on the top units, what goes inside, and Rough size moved before Material; mockups in the chat, then "go"; live since 22 Oct 2026, Raf: "merge")**: Plan your project's brief now runs 04 Rough size, 05 Material, 06 Finish (the message and the Job Kit's pasted-brief reader follow that order). Ticking the Material chip whose words contain PAX (how-it-works.json briefMaterial, "IKEA PAX built in") opens **Plan your PAX** once per visit (src/app/how-it-works/pax-planner.tsx PaxWindow: a portal, z-[70], full screen on a phone, a 1080 px window from md; Escape, the backdrop and the x close it, Tab stays inside, the page behind doesn't scroll). Wall width and ceiling height are the brief's Rough size width and height (typed in either place). Then 1 Height: PAX 2010 with made-to-measure MDF top boxes when 300+ is left above (else an infill strip), or PAX 2364 when the ceiling is 2380 or more (at 2400: a 36 infill strip); 2 Units, left to right: Fill my wall picks the frames that leave the least filler (at least 20 an end; ties to fewer 75s, then fewer units; 2400 = 100 + 75 + 50 with 78 a side), + PAX 100 / 75 / 50, Remove unit N, swap the picked unit for another size (only sizes that fit); 3 Doors: IKEA below (they choose and buy) or made-to-measure MDF below, ready for their decorator; the top boxes always MDF; a 75 frame always gets MDF doors (IKEA sells no hinged doors for it, the frame is for sliding doors); 4 Inside the picked unit: Long hanging, Two rails, Short hanging + shelves, Drawers + short hanging, Baskets, drawer + hanging, Shelves all the way, Not sure yet (they buy these from IKEA), Same in every unit. The drawing (PaxDrawing, drawn at the box's own pixel width so the 11 px labels stay readable) is the front to scale with every size: Wall, then fillers and each unit at its real width (998 / 748 / 498), Ceiling on the left, Frames and Top (or Infill) on the right, unit names under the floor (100 / 75 / 50 when PAX 75 won't fit), IKEA parts white, MDF parts blue-tinted, fillers hatched; Show the inside / Show the doors; tap a unit to pick it. On a phone the drawing sticks under the window's header (height from the screen, 104 to 172 px) while the steps scroll. Size cards: wall, units across ('998 + 748 + 498 = 2244'), fillers, PAX frames (height, 580 deep), top boxes, the picked unit. Footer: 'It all fits. Rough sizes are fine: I measure properly on the free visit.' or the problem (too wide, too little for fillers, room for another unit), and Done, back to my brief. Under Material a card (PaxCard) says 'Plan it' or the plan ('PAX 100 + 75 + 50 · 2010 + 390 MDF top boxes · IKEA doors' with a small drawing) and Change it; Remove my plan at the end of the steps. While the units are on auto they follow the wall width (resolved()), so changing Rough size refits them; adding, removing or swapping a unit stops that until Fill my wall. The message gets, after Budget: 'My IKEA PAX plan:', Space, Units (names, real widths and total, fillers), Height, Doors ('IKEA below (I’ll buy them), made to measure on the 75 frame and the top boxes. MDF, ready for my decorator'), Inside per unit, and a Check line if it doesn't fit; unticking PAX leaves it out, ticking again brings it back without the window. It goes with Send to Raf, WhatsApp, email and copy, so it reaches the ServiceM8 job description (parseBrief keeps the lines). Sums in src/lib/pax-brief.ts (no imports; the same PAX numbers as the Job Kit planner, src/lib/wardrobe.ts). Tested (scratchpad pax/sums.mts, the sums for every wall 540 to 9000; pax/parse.mts; pax/test.mjs, 131 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320 and at 1280: the step order, the window opening on the tick with the cursor in the width, nothing drawn before the sizes, every dimension label, size cards, tall frames and the infill, remove / add / swap / Fill my wall, doors and the 75 note, picking a unit in the drawing and its inside, the drawing sticking on phones, no labels on top of each other, Done fits, Escape, the card, the message and its order, Rough size refitting the units, untick and tick again, Send to Raf's body, Remove my plan, no sideways scroll, no page errors).
- **Fitted wardrobe in the brief: one button, made to measure or IKEA PAX (22 Oct 2026, Raf: Finishes card "Painted" should be "Bare MDF"; small drawings on 01; Fitted wardrobe should open a planner like the PAX one with standard or custom units, made doors and drawers; IKEA PAX moved from Material into it; name, mobile and email required; budget from under £1,000; "03 what is it for when selected hanging clothes suggest according layout inside"; mockup in the chat, then "go"; live since 22 Oct 2026, Raf: "merge")**: replaces the PAX-from-Material start of the bullet above (its PAX planner lives on inside the new one). **01 What is it?**: every choice has a small line drawing (src/app/how-it-works/what-icons.tsx, picked by the words: wardrobe, alcove, book/shel, seat/bench, desk/office, loft/eaves, panel, kitchen, else a question mark), two to a row on a phone. Ticking the choice with 'wardrobe' in it shows a card under the chips (WardrobeCard in src/app/how-it-works/wardrobe-window.tsx): 'How should your wardrobe be built?' Made to measure / IKEA PAX built in / Not sure yet, each with a drawing; picking Made to measure or IKEA PAX the first time opens the planner; then the card shows the plan (small drawing, 'PAX 100 + 75 + 50 · 2010 + 390 MDF top boxes · IKEA doors' or '5 units: 450 + ... · top cupboards · shaker doors') with Change it; Not sure yet says I'll suggest one on the visit, with Plan it anyway (opens made to measure). **The planner** (WardrobeWindow): one window for both, a Made to measure | IKEA PAX built in switch under the title (switching keeps the sizes; each way keeps its own plan), the wall width and ceiling height shared with Rough size, the drawing at the top (sticky on a phone; on a computer its height comes from the window's height so the sizes, drawing and key always fit, with the size cards moved to the top of the steps). IKEA PAX: as before (pax-planner.tsx: PaxDrawing, PaxMini, PaxSteps, paxCards). **Made to measure** (mto-planner.tsx, sums in src/lib/wardrobe-brief.ts on CabinetOS's rules via src/lib/cabinetos-wardrobe.ts, the same as the 3D planner): 1 Height: Tall doors or Top cupboards too (from 2000, when 250+ is left under the infill), Plinth 70 / 100 / 150 (100 to start), Top infill 50 / 100 / 150 (50); 2 Units: Standard sizes (Fill my wall = the 3D planner's autoFill: 300 to 1000 widths leaving 20 to 100 at each wall; a 2400 wall gets 5 × 450), per-unit width chips (sizes that wouldn't fit greyed), + Add a unit, Remove unit N, Fill my wall; Custom (a width box 150 to 1200); Equal split (− / + units, the wall less 50 at each end shared, odd mm to the first units); 3 Doors: Flat / Shaker (70 mm frame), 'N doors in MDF, ready for your decorator: one door up to 600 wide, a pair above', sliding doors pointed to the 3D planner; 4 Inside the picked unit: Long hanging, Two rails, Short hanging + shelves, Drawers + short hanging, Drawers + shelves, Shelves all the way, Shoe shelves, Not sure yet, with Drawers go Behind / Below the doors, Same in every unit, and the unit's CabinetOS problem if it doesn't build as drawn. The drawing: fillers and top infill hatched, the plinth, each unit's carcass and its doors (shaker panels, handles on the opening edge, drawer fronts below the doors) or the inside (shelves, rails, drawers, dust shelf, the shelf at 2000) from unitLayout, widths over the top, Ceiling on the left, Plinth / Units (or To 2000 and Top) / Infill on the right with level labels pushed apart. Size cards: wall, units across, fillers, plinth and units, top cupboards or infill, the picked unit's doors (e.g. '450 wide · 1 door 446 × 2247'). **Insides follow 03** (suggestInsides in wardrobe-brief.ts, both ways of building, until the customer picks one): Hanging clothes = long hanging, short hanging + shelves, two rails in turn; with Folded clothes too, drawers + short hanging in the second; Folded only = drawers + shelves, shelves; Shoes = one unit of shoe shelves (the narrowest) when there's more than one; a unit under 450 never gets hanging; 'Suggested from what it's for: Hanging clothes, Shoes. Pick any unit to change it.' Picking an inside ticks 03 the other way (rails = Hanging clothes, drawers / shelves / baskets = Folded clothes, shoe shelves = Shoes; usesFor). **05 Material**: IKEA PAX is no longer a chip; with IKEA PAX picked, 'Your PAX frames come from IKEA. Pick the material for what I make: the top boxes, fillers and any made-to-measure doors.' (Designs like yours and /api/brief still get 'IKEA PAX built in' as a material for matching.) **Message**: 'Wardrobe: Made to measure' (or IKEA PAX built in / not sure yet, advise me) after What, and after Budget either 'My wardrobe plan (made to measure):' (Space, Units '450 + ... = 2250 mm (standard sizes), fillers about 75 at the ends', Height 'plinth 100, doors 2247 high, top infill 50' or '... doors to 2000, top cupboards 350 high ...', Doors '5 flat doors, made to measure (one up to 600 wide, a pair above), drawers below the doors on unit 2. MDF, ready for my decorator', Inside '1: long hanging under a shelf; 2: ...', Check) or 'My IKEA PAX plan:' (Inside now '1: long hanging; 2: ...'); unticking Fitted wardrobe leaves them out. The Job Kit's pasted-brief reader knows the Wardrobe line (brief-paste.ts LABELS). **Other changes**: Finishes card 'Painted' is 'Bare MDF'; Finish chips 'Bare MDF, for my decorator', 'Natural wood', 'Not sure' (the two decorator chips were one); budget 'Under £1,000', '£1,000 to £2,000', '£2,000 to £3,000', '£3,000 to £5,000', '£5,000 to £8,000', 'Over £8,000' (no Not sure yet); 07 needs name, mobile (10+ digits) and email for every way of sending (Send to Raf, WhatsApp, Email it, Copy text), pointing at the first missing one ('Add your mobile (10 digits or more), then send.'). - **Get my free quote button and the brief in three steps (28 Oct 2026, Raf on a call: people still didn't know what to do; the floating button 'has to look better' and 'entice to make a quote', and 'make sure that people read all information through so they can give me most information I need'; a Canva mockup of three steps was turned down; previewed on the `quote-steps` branch, live since 28 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: **The button** (src/components/HowItWorksFloat.tsx, bottom left on every page that had the Plan your project pill): solid blue (bg-accent, hover accent-hover, a soft blue shadow), 60 px tall on phones and 56 from md, a tape measure drawn in SVG (TapeIcon: case, reel, blade with marks and its hook), 'Get my free quote' with '3 quick steps, free visit' under it; it opens /how-it-works#brief (the stats still count it as the 'quote' goal). One gentle pulse when the page opens (globals.css .hif-pulse: hifSwell, a small swell, 1.2 s in, and hifRing, a blue ring going out; once, then still; none for reduced motion); still folds to the tape while scrolling (.hif-*), the saved-hearts count as a white badge (the tape makes way under 380 px). It is the one quote button that doesn't say 'Get a quote' (Raf agreed the words on the call). The Recently looked at card sits 4 px higher on phones and at 92 px from md so it clears the taller button. **The brief** (src/app/how-it-works/brief-builder.tsx) is one question to a screen in a white card, under 'Step 1 of 3 · Your project' and a bar in three parts (Progress; each part fills as its questions are answered): 1 Your project: What would you like made? (needed; the wardrobe card and planner and the 'See N in 3D' links here, with the ?job= note and Things you saved above on this screen only), Which room, and what is it for?, Roughly how big is the space? (width and height needed, in mm, a note when a number looks like cm, 'How I'd measure it' to /tips#measure-skirting-to-skirting), Any material or finish in mind? (the PAX note); 2 Your space: Add a photo or two of the space (at least one needed; a big Add photos box, drop works on a computer), Where is the job? (a full postcode needed: both halves; AddressFinder `required` / `invalid`, parking, access); 3 You: When, and roughly what budget?, Who should I reply to? (name, mobile, email needed, then Anything else? optional), Check and send (each answer in a line with Change; 'Back to check and send' once they've been there). Each needed question has a 'Needed' tag and why ('so I know the size of the job before I come', 'so I can see the walls, sockets and pipes before I come', 'so I know how far you are and can plan the visit'); Next with something missing says what and puts the cursor there. Next names the next step when it changes part ('Next: Your space'), Enter moves on, Back keeps the answers, each new screen scrolls its card into view and focuses its question. The message, the planners, Send to Raf, WhatsApp, Email it, Copy text and What happens next are as before, but the send buttons only show on Check and send; on a computer the message builds up beside the questions (sticky), on a phone it shows only on the last screen. Start again goes back to step 1. The 'Rather book a visit straight away? Book online.' line under the send buttons is gone (Raf, 28 Oct 2026: 'creates distraction'). Page: the #brief section has no scroll margin of its own (html's scroll-padding clears the menu) and less space above on phones, so the button lands with the first question on screen; how-it-works.json briefHeading 'Get your free quote in 3 quick steps', briefIntro 'One question at a time ...'. Tested (scratchpad quote/test.cjs, 188 checks: every screen as an iPhone 13, at 320, 1024 and 1280 with /api/brief and postcodes.io stood in: what needed and the cursor on a choice, size needed and the cursor in width then height, the cm note, photo needed, the photo added, half a postcode turned down, name then mobile then email, the check list, the send button only at the end, the message lines, Change size and straight back, Back, Send to Raf's body, photo and done, the Brief sent card, no Change once sent, no sideways scroll, no page errors; quote/test2.cjs, 47 checks: the button's link, words, blue, 60 px, one pulse then still, folding, landing with the first question on screen, counted as a quote, the count at 320 clear of WhatsApp, reduced motion (no pulse, no folding, the brief works), saved items on screen 1 and in the message, ?what=Fitted wardrobe, IKEA PAX planner sizes shared with the size question, the PAX note and plan, WhatsApp's what happens next, ?job= note and message, Enter, Back, the sticky message on a computer, Start again, the home page).

Promises Raf gave: files within 2 working days (dueDay(): 2 working days after the order day, weekends and England's bank holidays to 2028 skipped), up to 2 rounds of changes free (a round = one list of changes to the same design; a different piece is a new order).
