Only WhatsApp floats (LayoutShell, like /cost/). Sharing picture src/app/drawings/opengraph-image.tsx (og-whole-job-<hash>.jpg). **Order** (src/lib/drawings-order.ts): the form needs what they're making (10+ characters), at least one file, a name, an email and two ticks ('I'll check every size ... The cut is my call.' and 'Start straight away. I understand that once the files are sent to me, I can't cancel for a refund.', the UK cancellation rule for services started early); POST /api/drawings/order (hidden box, 6 s on the page, 4 orders per address per 10 minutes, needs SERVICEM8_API_KEY and STRIPE_SECRET_KEY) makes a Quote job with createEnquiry whose description starts 'Drawings order: Drawings and cut list, 3 units' / 'Not paid yet: £234 with VAT.' (ORDER_MARK; an order nobody pays for stays a lead on the Clients board) and answers a signed token (brief-token.ts signRecord, 6 hours); each file goes to /api/drawings/file (up to 10, 4 MB each, photos made 2400 px JPEG on the phone with shrinkPhoto, PDFs, SketchUp, DWG, DXF, spreadsheets; checked by their first bytes; ServiceM8 uuid from the job and file number, named order-01-<name>); POST /api/drawings/pay touches the job (files show in its diary) and makes a Stripe Checkout Session (src/lib/stripe-checkout.ts, plain fetch: the price with VAT, customer email, metadata kind 'drawings' with the job, pack, units and name, statement suffix RAF DRAWINGS, STRIPE_VAT_TAX_RATE_ID if set; success /drawings/thanks?session_id=..., cancel /drawings?cancelled=1#order, on the address the visitor is on). Cancelled: the token is kept for the tab (sessionStorage raf_drawings_order), so the form shows 'The payment was cancelled, so nothing was charged' with Pay £234 (no second job) or Start a new order. **Paid**: /drawings/thanks (noindex) asks POST /api/drawings/paid {session}; confirmPaid reads the session from Stripe and, only if paid and its payment (uuid from the session id) isn't on the job yet: the payment on the job (Card, paid in full), the job to Work Order with line 2 'Paid online Friday 6 November: £234 with VAT. Due by Tuesday 10 November.', a note with the Stripe payment, and two emails through ServiceM8 (Raf's 'Paid drawings order: <name>, <pack>' with replyTo the customer and the whole order; the customer's with the pack, £195 + VAT £39, the due day, what happens next, the 2 rounds, check before you cut, and Rafal Solutions Ltd's company and VAT numbers). The page shows the pack, what was paid and 'Your files by Tuesday 10 November', Send more photos on WhatsApp. POST /api/drawings/stripe (Stripe's webhook, checkout.session.completed, signed with STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET) does the same 25 s later for someone who closed the tab (the thank-you page normally got there first, so it finds the payment and does nothing). CabinetOS's own Stripe webhook leaves these sessions alone (no user_id / priceId, kind not dfy). **Job Kit**: Quotes never reads a drawings order (toRead and the not-worked-out list skip ORDER_MARK); the Job timeline doesn't count a paid one as 'needs dates'; Today lists 'Drawings for <name>: due Tuesday 10 November' (urgent and 'due today' on the day). **Around the site**: footer Explore 'Drawings and cut lists', a 'Done for you' card on the Tools page before Build It in 3D, site search ('Drawings and cut lists, done for you', words cut list, plans, shop drawings...), the sitemap, the wardrobe planner's Send step ('Building it yourself? I can draw it for you ... From £48 with VAT', Get it drawn, while the free cut list box is off for customers), and /privacy's 'When you order drawings' (ServiceM8, Stripe never shows card details, six years for accounts) with Stripe in Who else. **To switch it on (Raf)**: in Vercel raf-carpentry, Production: STRIPE_SECRET_KEY (a restricted key from Raf Carpentry's own Stripe account, kept apart from CabinetOS's (the business name and statement words are Raf Carpentry's; in Stripe: Developers, API keys, Create restricted key, Checkout Sessions Write); Raf is setting it up himself (6 Nov 2026: 'will do it tomorrow'), optionally STRIPE_VAT_TAX_RATE_ID (a 20% inclusive UK VAT tax rate made in that Stripe account) and, for the webhook, an endpoint https://www.rafcarpentry.com/api/drawings/stripe with event checkout.session.completed and its signing secret as STRIPE_WEBHOOK_SECRET; then redeploy. Without the key the form says card payments aren't switched on and to email or WhatsApp instead. The customer's bank statement shows the Stripe account's name plus RAF DRAWINGS. The display: the sum in Geist Mono ('600 − 36' as it's typed, '600 − 36 =' after =; 22 px, shrunk by fit() down to 12 so the longest sum stays on its line), the answer in Bricolage 700 sized to fill the width (fit() measures again at the new size because Bricolage's optical sizing changes its widths; up to half the display's height, 148 px at most), a blue dimension line under it. Keys: 7 8 9 / 4 5 6 / 1 2 3 / 0 . ⌫ big (clamp(34px, min(17cqw, 10.5cqh), 72px): about 70 px in Safari on an iPhone 13), down the right C ÷ × − + and a tall blue = (the waiting operator lit blue). Day look = mockup C2 (rounded white keys on paper, pale blue operators, Bricolage digits); night look = C3 (dark keys, Geist digits, blue outlines): follows the phone's light / dark setting, the moon / sun button switches it and it's kept on the device (localStorage raf_calc_theme 'day' | 'night'); the browser bar (theme-color) and the page behind follow. Short screens (container under 620 px tall) take a little off the display so every key stays 44 px or more. On a computer: a framed column up to 460 wide in the middle, the keyboard works (0 to 9, + - * x /, Enter or =, Backspace, Esc / Delete / C, . or ,) with a one-line hint. Sums in src/lib/basic-calc.ts (no imports): 12 digits at most, thousands commas, 0.1 + 0.2 = 0.3 (12 significant figures), chained sums (2 + 3 × 4 = 20, left to right like a pocket calculator), 5 + = is 10, ÷ 0 = Error 'Can't divide by 0'. Styles in basic-calculator.css (its own --bc-* colours, like the spirit level's). Sharing picture opengraph-image.tsx with public/images/tools/calculator-1f5bd09d.jpg (the page as an iPhone 13 after 600 − 36 =). Just after an operator it shows the number so far ('600 −' over 600); a chain shows the running total ('2 + 3 ×' then '5 × 4' over 20); '5 ÷ 0' before the next digit shows 'Can't divide by 0' lighter in the big line (`wait`, `data-wait`), '5 ÷ 0.5' then 10, and only = on ÷ 0 is the Error. The drawing: the deck from above to scale (an SVG in mm, as wide as the card allows and never taller than 300 px on phones / 420 from 1024), the house wall hatched along the top, the sizes in blue under and beside it; Boards shows every row (alternate shades, the 6 mm gaps as membrane showing through, a black line where boards join), Frame the joists, rims, noggins and the pads (or posts when raised); under it '3.5 × 3.0 m · 10.5 m² · 21 boards' and '↓ falls 15 mm to the front'. What you need, grouped Boards / Frame / Ground / Fixings / Finishing, then the checks (planning: under 30 cm a house usually needs none, over 30 cm it does; the fall; joins; the edge board's width), then a dark card 'Rather I build it?' with Get a quote, Send this list to WhatsApp (wa.me/?text= with no number, so they pick the chat) and Copy the list. Under it How it's worked out (the rules, written from RULES and BOARDS) and Questions (FAQPage and WebApplication JSON-LD; planning answer from the Planning Portal: 30 cm, half the garden, not in front of the house, houses only, not listed buildings), and a band to the Camden Town deck job and Decks and fences in 3D. Only WhatsApp floats (LayoutShell, as on /cost/). No prices (Raf's £250 a m² composite is in the cost calculator, off on the site). **Sums** (src/lib/decking-calc.ts, no imports): rows = width across ÷ (board + gap), rounded up; the board length that buys least (softwood 2.4 / 3.6 / 4.8), +5% spare (at least 1), +10% when boards join end to end (one line of joins over a doubled joist); the edge board cut to fit, and when that would be under half a board the first and last are both cut (softwood 3.0 m: 82 mm each, not a 20 mm sliver); joists across the boards at the centres + 1, rims across their ends, a row of noggins about every 1.5 m (out of 3.6 m lengths), timber lengths 2.4 to 4.8 m (joined over a support beyond); pads under each joist about every 1.2 m, or raised: 47 × 150 joists, doubled bearers and 100 × 100 posts about every 1.8 m; one hidden clip per board per joist + a start clip each joist (composite), 2 screws per board per joist in boxes of 500 (softwood); frame screws 4 per joist end and noggin in 100s; fascia round the three open sides in 3.6 m lengths; membrane +10%; joist tape on every joist and rim; fall 5 mm a metre. Starting deck 3.5 × 3.0 m composite along the house on the ground at 400: 21 boards (last cut to 112), 10 joists of 3.0 m, 2 rims of 3.6 m, 1 noggin length (9 pieces), 40 pads, 210 clips, 100 frame screws, 3 fascia, 12 m² membrane, 37 m tape. Check: `node scripts/decking-calculator/check.mjs` (839,855 checks: the starting deck by hand, softwood, out from the house, joins, raised, reading sizes, then every size 1 to 9 m in both ways with every board, height and spacing: boards cover the width with no row more, sensible edge board, enough board, joist and rim length, joists no further apart than asked, drawing inside the deck, a board drawn a row, supports drawn, no dashes, the brief link and the list text). Board sizes and the rules are common UK ones, not from Raf (he can change BOARDS / RULES). **Get a quote** opens Plan your project with Decking ticked, the size filled in and a note (deckBriefHref: /how-it-works?what=Decking&w=3500&h=3000&note=...#brief): the brief now has a **Decking** choice (how-it-works.json briefWhat, before Something else; a drawing of boards on joists in what-icons.tsx), and when Decking is all that's ticked the size question is 'Roughly how big is the deck?' with Along the house / Out from it / Off the ground, and the message says 'Rough size: 3500 along the house x 3000 out (mm)'; ?note= starts the brief's Anything else box (300 characters at most). The floating WhatsApp button's message on the page is 'Hi Raf, I'd like a quote for a deck.' (whatsappText). Guides in src/lib/learn.ts (no imports): title, two-line answer first, steps {title, body, tip, link}, optional table (the hang guide's which fixing for which wall), What you need ({name, note, key from src/lib/affiliates.ts or href}; a key whose url isn't '#' is an Amazon link with rel sponsored nofollow and the Associate sentence, else plain words), Be careful, questions, the words search finds, a sharing still. Each step has a moving drawing (src/components/learn/scenes.tsx, 360 x 220 SVG, `GuideDrawing({slug, step})`, SCENES per guide) acted out on a 7 s loop (src/app/learn/learn.css: `mv-lg-*` classes so motion.css pauses them off screen; the base is the finished drawing, so reduced motion and paused ones show it done). src/components/learn/GuidePlayer.tsx: one step at a time with Back / numbers / Next (Start again on the last), swipe, arrow keys, Play all steps (one loop each), Watch again, See all steps (every step with its drawing, ids step-N), and #step-3 in the address (on arrival or a change) opens that step. Page: header with the facts (steps, time, Moving drawings), the player, the table, What you need, Be careful, Questions, 'Rather I do it?' (Get a quote, WhatsApp naming the guide: whatsappText's /learn/ case) and the other guides; HowTo, FAQPage and BreadcrumbList JSON-LD; sharing pictures from public/images/learn/<slug>-<hash>.jpg (the card drawing, made from the page by scratchpad learn/stills.cjs with the floating buttons hidden). First two guides: 'How to hang something heavy on any wall' (knock and listen, find the studs and keep out of the cable zones, mark level with the phone spirit level, drill with tape on the bit, plug / screw into the stud / cavity fixing, hang and check; a table of five walls) and 'How to hang a cabinet or mirror with a French cleat' (how it works, cut one 18 mm ply strip at 45°, cleat and spacer on the cabinet, wall half level, lift it on). The words are written from standard UK practice for Raf to check; his own parts are the phone level, the track saw and his confirmed Amazon links (level, impact driver, tape, track saw; stud detector, drill bits, plugs and spring toggles have no product in his sheet yet, so they show as words). Links in: footer Explore 'Learn to fix it yourself', the Tools menu's Free tools, /tips under the header, site search (the page and each guide), sitemap; back link: a guide goes up to Learn, Learn to Tools. Next guide: hidden push-in panel connectors, once Raf sends the product name, its Amazon link (Products tab, Confirmed) and photos. **Drawings redone (9 Nov 2026, Raf: 'Redo those players with fable')**: all 11 scenes redrawn in src/components/learn/scenes.tsx: materials in their own quiet colours (brick, timber, plywood, steel, the tool's dark body: --lg-* on `.lg-art` in learn.css, only inside these drawings), each step in two or three beats with its own line of words along the top (`Cap`), rings where a knock, a detector or a screw lands, a tick disc when it's right, and a thin blue line along the bottom edge that fills over the 7 s loop. Timing is now in the class names as % of the loop (mv-lg-show-A, off-A, win-A-B, pop-A, draw-A-B, in-A, go-A-B, slide-A-B, turn / rot-A-B, growx-A-B, tap-A, ping-A, puff-A, buzz-A-B, wig-A-B, tug-A, plus drill, toggle and bar) and **learn.css is written by `node scripts/learn/css.mjs`** from the classes the scenes use: run it after changing a scene, write every class out in full, never edit learn.css by hand. New sharing stills hang-heavy-things-on-a-wall-13cfbf7e.jpg and french-cleat-350b0ccd.jpg (the old ones deleted). Tested on a production build (scratchpad learn/test.cjs, 166 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1280 and with reduced motion: index cards, headings, the player's buttons, keys, swipe, Play all moving on after 7 s, See all steps, #step-3 fresh and as a change, drawings moving on screen and paused off it, none moving with reduced motion and finished, table, three Amazon links each with the Associate line, JSON-LD, back links, WhatsApp words, search, sitemap, sharing pictures, no sideways scroll, no page errors).

It straightens, strips all metadata (GPS), resizes to 1600 px, lightly corrects, saves public/images/services/<slug>/<hash>.jpg and rebuilds that service's "photos" list in services.json from the folder (empty folder = no photos; removed photos are deleted). On the service page the photos show as "Photos" (no jobs yet, replacing the "no photos yet" block) or "More photos", and photos[0] becomes the card and sharing photo instead of the drawing (coverFor in src/lib/services.ts). ## Blog Posts Written (content/blog/)

4-9. Project posts (written 3 Oct 2026, dated Feb to Sep 2026 to match the jobs), written from the job photos only, each linked both ways with its job page (blogSlug): curved-storage-seat-kennington, birch-ply-desk-stands-wood-green, birch-ply-room-chiswick, reception-desk-brentwood, loft-wardrobes-ikea-pax, wardrobes-around-a-chimney-breast. Photos in public/images/blog/<post>/ (hero.jpg 16:10 plus 1.jpg... at 900 px). Post layout tricks (globals.css): two images on one markdown line sit side by side, three make a row of three; add #narrow (62% wide) or #slim (40%, for 9:16 video stills) to an image address for an upright single photo; an italic line on its own right after photos becomes a caption. Post images lazy-load. Posts are deliberately different in length and shape, and dated a little after each job was finished. "Tools used" only lists tools seen in that job's photos, by their Products-tab names so links-from-sheet.py keeps them in sync.
10-19. ## Project pages (added October 2026)
- **Source photos**: Raf drops each job into a folder on his Mac: Documents/Raf Carpentry Projects/<YYYY-MM what area>/ with subfolders 1 Finished, 2 Build steps, 3 Drawings, 4 Free plan, plus notes.txt (template folder inside; area only, never client names).
- **Processing**: photos are rotated, stripped of all EXIF/GPS, resized to 1600 px and lightly corrected (white balance, levels). Short iPhone clips give stills; longer clips become muted web videos (720 px wide, H.264) in public/videos/.
- **Data**: one JSON per job in content/projects/<slug>.json (title, area, finished YYYY-MM, type, tags (search words and filter buttons), summary, intro, cover, facts, steps, gallery, optional compare / plan (image, real, imageLabel, realLabel, views) / video / planSale (previews, includes, price, buyUrl Payhip link or freeDownload) / blogSlug / tools). Loader: src/lib/projects.ts.
- **Pages**: /portfolio lists jobs (newest first) plus an "Earlier work" strip from content/pages/portfolio.json; /portfolio/[slug] shows main photo with a title-block of facts, one interactive piece (before/after slider, plan vs real, or video), numbered build steps, photo grid with a full-screen viewer, optional free SketchUp plan / blog link / tools, quote band and next job.
- **Search**: src/lib/search.ts (stem + matches) shared by the My Work page and its quick-filter buttons.
- **Components**: src/components/project/CompareSlider.tsx, ProjectVideo.tsx, ProjectGallery.tsx, Lightbox.tsx (+ lightbox.css, shared full-screen viewer; since 9 Oct 2026 it sets the picture width from the photo's shape (--lb-ratio) because browsers sized it from the srcset guess, which showed 780 px screenshots at 159 px on an iPhone and 1600 px job photos at about 600 px on retina desktops; pinch zoom works and swipes pause while zoomed; optional `fill` mode and per-photo `caption`), PlanCard.tsx (free preview + buy/download), PlanDrawings.tsx (plan next to the real thing, plus a 'More drawings' row from plan.views; all drawings open full size), ScreenGrid.tsx (SketchUp screenshots); styles in src/app/portfolio/project.css.
- **Selling plans**: Payhip (London, free plan 5% + Stripe 1.5% + 20p). Each plan £4.99, bundle planned at £12.99. Add `planSale` to the job JSON with preview images (no dimensions) and the Payhip link; the job page and /sketchup pick it up.
- **Live jobs** (21, October 2026): the 4 from 2026-09 plus 17 added from Raf's folders (window seats, desks, PAX wardrobes, birch ply room, reception desk, panelling, glass doors, decking, fence, porch storage, curved seat...). All notes.txt were empty, so text is written from the photos only; no sizes, timings or prices claimed. People other than Raf are cropped out of photos. The archive "Pantry cupboard" was removed because it is now a full job page.
- **Batch 3 (3 Oct 2026, 31 jobs live)**: ten folders dated 2025-08 to 2025-12 (Haringey TV unit + bookcase, Haringey picture-frame panelling (same room), Kings Park L-shaped bench (has a SketchUp drawing: 3000 x 1215 mm, 400 high), Highbury floating walnut desk, Bowes Park vanity, Newington Green alcove shelves, East Finchley loft cylinder cupboard, East Finchley alcove wardrobes with boiler, East Finchley bookcase with radiator cover, Chiswick four-door birch ply wardrobe). notes.txt were blank again. The 2025-11 East Finchley loft and the 2025-12 Chiswick wardrobe are different rooms from the jobs already on the site (checked by photo). The walnut desk hangs on a wall batten and the side walls; the black parts under it are drawer runners, not brackets. Videos (16 MOV) not used yet. New quick filter: Bookshelves.
- **Materials and tools per job ("What I used")**: Raf ticks them in the "Project kit" tab of his Google Sheet "Raf Carpentry Amazon links" (ID 1iBZ48RoMbq_KiumV-VfNlcM3fH8ugde8Kwia6DIuLZU): one column per job (job page address in row 2), one row per item, column A "Checked" = allowed on the site, column F = product key on the Products tab. To update the site: read 'Project kit'!A1:AC300 and Products!A1:M300 with the Sheets connector, save as {"kit": [...], "products": [...]} and run `python3 scripts/kit-from-sheet.py that.json` -> content/project-kit.json (kept apart from the job JSONs). Amazon links only appear for products marked Confirmed; the Amazon Associate sentence shows whenever a page has one. Shown by src/components/project/ProjectKit.tsx after Photos; kit words are also searchable on My Work. The first draft (2 Oct 2026) was spotted from the photos; nothing was checked yet.
- **Product links (Tools page + blog "Tools used")**: Products tab of the same sheet. Raf pastes Amazon short links (link.amazon/... or amzn.to, used as pasted) and sets Status to Confirmed. Read Products!A1:M300, save the raw values as a JSON file and run `python3 scripts/links-from-sheet.py that.json`: it matches by name ("Name on the site" = tool name on the Tools page / in a post's tools list) and only links Confirmed rows; everything else shows without a link. New tools need a description, so add them to content/pages/tools.json by hand. src/lib/affiliates.ts mirrors the Products tab and has isAmazonLink() (rel="sponsored nofollow noopener" and the Amazon Associate sentence wherever an Amazon link shows).
- **Tool photos on the Tools page**: small square photos of Raf's own tools, cropped from his job photos (never Amazon's images: the Associates rules forbid copying them). Files are public/images/tools/<product key>.jpg (320x320), set as `image` in content/pages/tools.json; tools without a photo show their brand name in a grey square. Still missing (Oct 2026): Domino, multi-tool, Irwin chisels, Bahco saw, Stabila 70-2-60 level, Bosch laser measure, Shinwa square, Incra rule.
- **Raf's kit**: mostly DeWalt power tools and Festool (TSC 55 KEB track saw, FS 1400/2-LR 32 rail), plus a Makita DRT50 trim router, Stabila TECH 196 electronic level, Henry vacuum. The old Makita track saw/drill/jigsaw guesses were replaced on the Tools page, the cut-list post and the Products tab.
- **Curved seat (Kennington)**: has the SketchUp model (front, left, right views from "3 Drawings") beside the built seat, plus both hand sketches. Its planSale previews are filled in (no dimensions); it shows once a Payhip buyUrl is added. When replacing a job image, give it a new file name so cached copies are not served.

Steps can now run past five: on a computer they wrap into rows of 3 or 4 (stepCols in the page). New step text is written from the photos only (no sizes, materials or claims beyond what shows). New files are named step-/b-/g-<hash>.jpg so nothing old is served from cache. How it was done: stage the folder's photos and clips, run `python3 scripts/job-contact-sheets.py OUTDIR "slug=Folder name"` (numbered sheets in time order, with sharpness and an ON mark for photos already on the page), pick by number, then process like the other photos (upright, no metadata, 1600 px, light correction). Part names reach the page as node extras `part` (su-to-glb.mjs), so name parts in plain English when building. Poster scripts must hide `.m3d-tools` (the zoom buttons) before the screenshot. Restart the local server with scratchpad restart.sh (pkill patterns anchored with ^ so the shell isn't killed). Framing (9 Oct 2026): the camera fits the corners of every part where it is now (open, apart) and aims at the middle of where they land on screen, settled at once on the first frame, so every model sits centred; all stills were remade after this change (scratchpad poster.js + poster-fit.py). Room context: materials 'Wall' or starting 'Room' (e.g. 'Room: cast iron' fireplace) stay put when taken apart and can't be tapped; against a wall, taken-apart pieces only come forward. **More models (9 Oct 2026, Raf: 'If you don't know use standard size and do all points')**: (1) Palmers Green alcove units (standard sizes): 2600 high to the cornice, 350 deep, 18 mm MDF with 18 mm backs, 80 plinth; left alcove 850 wide full-height shelving with six shelves; right alcove 1000 wide, three shelves over a two-door cupboard (top at 888, one shelf inside), inset doors with round wooden knobs where they meet; chimney breast, back wall and a plain cast iron fireplace for context. Bay walls with three windows, back wall, side wall, chimney breast for context. About 290 KB because of the lattice holes. Taking apart: the room fades out while the job is apart (room parts get their own edge material) and comes back when it goes together; a model holding two jobs lists them in `apart` (e.g. ["Window seat", "Alcove cabinet"], also ["Left alcove", "Right alcove"]) so parts named '<job>: ...' come apart round their own job's middle. (18) Newington Green alcove shelves: 850 x 340 alcove lined to 2100 under a 2700 ceiling, cupboard 685 high (recessed plinth, shelf inside, two flat inset doors), four open shelves, a warm LED strip under the first shelf (emissive); chimney breast and walls as room context. **Batch 5 (10 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Next'), all standard sizes, the last 7 jobs:** (19) Chiswick birch ply room: back wall 2600, ceiling 2500, 18 mm birch ply; wardrobe 1000 x 600 x 2100 (left: top shelf, rail, two low shelves; right: four shelves over four drawers) with a top box to the ceiling; overhead cupboards 350 deep from 1650 to the ceiling, four doors in pairs, round finger holes (prism holes); walls under them lined in ply; a 200 deep shelf on three black straps; wall-hung desk 800 x 500 at 740 with a drawer; a birch ply pegboard (54 holes) on the side wall; window, radiator and lamp left out; about 280 KB. (20) Haringey glass sliding doors: 2800 opening, four 720 panels (40 mm black frame, two bars, glass with `opacity`), outer pair fixed in the back channel, middle pair slide out in front; above the track, timber between the steel ceiling joists (room context) and a plasterboard strip; 'Slide the doors open'. (21) Ealing fence and gate: 3850 close-board fence 1800 high (posts, three rails a bay, gravel board, 98 mm boards, capping), seen from the patio side as in the photos; gate post, head, ledged and braced gate (nine boards, three ledges, two braces, T-hinges) hung by a brick pier, opens towards the patio. (22) Haringey dark panelled wall: 2800 wide, four dark panels floor to ceiling, two brass strips (emissive) set into slots (prism holes) in the middle pair; bookcase as room context; no moving parts (no open button). (23) Haringey picture-frame panelling: one 4200 wall of the room (the others are not drawn), pine moulding 20 x 12 mitred (four trapezoid pieces a frame), three tall frames above a dado rail at 880 to 950, a short frame over the door, two shorter frames below the dado (none behind the radiator), 100 mm gaps; door, architrave, skirting, cornice, radiator as room context; no moving parts. (24) Crouch End kitchen: V-groove MDF made as prisms with 6 x 3 mm V notches every 125 mm (helper `vgroove` in scratchpad m3d build, faces -y, +x or -x), on battens over a 3000 run of worktop; wall cupboard with panelled side and door; cooker hood (ledge, tapered canopy, chimney); 600 x 600 tall unit with panelled sides and door; dado-height panelling with capping and skirting on the dining side; base units, worktop and hob as room context. (25) Camden Town decking: courtyard 3600 x 5000, membrane, 47 x 100 joists across at about 400 on packing blocks, 25 boards 139 wide running to the far fence, a border board each end; brick walls cut down to 900 so the deck shows; 'Slide the boards off' slides them towards the doors end to show the frame (lifting them only hid it). 31 jobs now have models, every job on the site; the SketchUp page gallery lists them all. **Links to the models (10 Oct 2026, Raf: 'What other connections we need to make', then left it to me overnight)**: every job page's model has id `in-3d` (the section, or the model block inside PlanDrawings for the 3 drawing jobs) and a 'See all 31 jobs in 3D' link to /sketchup#in-3d (src/components/project/AllIn3DLink.tsx; the number is countModels() in src/lib/projects.ts, with modelOf()); each service page has 'These N jobs in 3D' after its job list: the same ModelGallery with that service's jobs (just the viewer, no picker, when there is one job), then the same link; the home SketchUp section has a third link 'See all 31 jobs in 3D'; the Instagram page (/ms, ms.json) has a My Work button after Plan Your Project. 'See the job' in the gallery still opens the top of the job page (the real photos). Still needs Raf: which job each of the 5 reviews is about (reviews.json `job`), a Payhip link for the Kennington plan, ticks in the Project kit tab, Google Search Console; the /ms button 'CabinetOS App Waitlist' is out of date (the app has a free plan). Build tools: scripts/su-readback.py turns the padded read-back file plus a meta JSON (name, source, materials, moves, view, actions, secs, apart, omit) into the .su.json; the SketchUp helper code is saved in scripts/su-helpers.py (paste the whole file at the top of each build_model call, no imports allowed there): mm helpers set_frame (turn and move a piece), material, assembly, box, prism (an outline pushed along a vector, with holes), cyl, and export_model (walks the groups, naming 'Assembly: Part', world coordinates, faces wound outward, visible edges only). Hinge origins in the meta are the door's hinge-side front corner (axis 0,0,-1 for hinged left, 0,0,1 for hinged right); doors next to a side wall or hinge-to-hinge with a neighbour open 90. The model is built in the Trimble SketchUp connector (mcp__Trimble_SketchUp__build_model, inches inside the API) to the sizes on Raf's drawing, as named parts (18 mm boards, lids as their own parts), then read back face by face (outer + inner loops, visible edges) into scripts/models/<slug>.su.json, which also holds the materials, what moves (`moves`: hinge {origin, axis, angle} or slide {offset}, each with its slot `at` in the open animation and `with` for knobs that ride along), the dimension lines, the opening view and turn limits (`view`), the button words (`actions`) and `secs` (SketchUp axes, mm; the script's header explains the format). Getting the read-back out: return it from build_model with a ~120,000-character padding field so the connector's answer is too long and gets saved as a file in the tool-results folder; parse that file (no copying by hand). Shared shapes (a door, a knob, a frame part) are kept once under `geoms` with a 3x4 transform per copy, as SketchUp components are. `node scripts/su-to-glb.mjs scripts/models/<slug>.su.json public/models/<slug>.glb` makes the web file (checks every part is closed; about 46 KB for the bench; gltf-validator: no errors). The connector has no glTF/COLLADA export and this workspace cannot download its .skp links (Raf can, in his browser); it also cannot open Raf's own .skp files, so each job is rebuilt from its drawing's sizes. Job JSON: `plan.model3d` {src, poster (a still of the opening view, shown until the 3D is ready and if there is no WebGL: 1600x800 for wide pieces, 1600x1200 with `shape: "tall"` for wardrobes), title, text, shape}. Shown by src/components/project/Model3D.tsx inside PlanDrawings (under the drawing and photo, above 'More drawings'); three.js + the model load only when it is near the screen (src/lib/model3d-scene.ts). Drag to turn (sideways only on a phone so the page scrolls; turning is limited to the room side), slow swing until touched, 'Lift the lids' (each lid turns on its hinge, one after another), 'Show the sizes' (blue dimension lines like the drawings), SketchUp-style dark edges on MDF-coloured faces. The text says "modelled in SketchUp to the sizes on the drawing": it is a rebuild from the drawing, not Raf's original file. Make the poster from the page with reduced motion on (no swing) so it matches the opening view.
- **Job Kit app (10 Oct 2026, Raf: 'make of it pwa where I fill all the pictures and videos and measurements and later send to you ... doesn't take much space on phone')**: rafcarpentry.com/job-kit, a home-screen web app for Raf only (not linked, noindex, robots disallow, no menu/footer/floats via LayoutShell). It follows the 'Job photo and video checklist' doc (Claude Docs): tabs Before (3 fixed spots + problems), Build (8 stages + labels + clever bit), Finished (main upright and sideways, the 3 spots again with the before photo shown as a guide, 45 degrees both sides, open, close-ups, in the room, lights), Clips (reveal, open and close, satisfying moment, before/after clip, time-lapse from Photos), Drawings, Notes (sizes W x H x D rows, materials, hardware, tool chips, tricky, what the client wanted, OK to show photos, Google review) and Send. Slots and wording: src/app/job-kit/plan.ts. Take photo / Film use the file input with `capture`, so on an iPhone nothing goes into the camera roll; photos are redrawn on the phone at 2400 px JPEG 0.86 (about 0.5 MB, EXIF and GPS gone) with a 240 px thumbnail (media.ts); clips go as filmed. Everything waits in IndexedDB 'raf-job-kit' (store.ts) and is sent one file at a time, clips in 3 MiB pieces (uploader.ts); the phone's copy is deleted once sent; with no signal it waits and retries (online event, app opened, every 20 s). job.json + notes.txt (template shape) follow when the queue is empty and edits have stopped for 15 s, or at once on 'Send to Claude'. PIN in localStorage (raf_jobkit_pin). Service worker public/job-kit-sw.js (scope /job-kit) keeps the page, scripts and fonts so it opens with no signal; manifest public/job-kit.webmanifest; icons public/images/job-kit/ (white R on blue, made from src/lib/r-logo.ts with sharp). On iPhone the home-screen app and Safari keep separate storage, so Raf must add it to the home screen first and use it from there (the app says so). **Server**: src/app/api/job-kit/{check,upload,job}/route.ts + src/lib/job-kit-server.ts write each file through the GitHub Contents API into a PRIVATE repo, rafmoney503/raf-job-inbox (JOBKIT_REPO), on a branch per job `job/<id>` (made from main, so the repo needs a README commit) under a folder `<id>/` (before/ build/ finished/ clips/ drawings/, names like finished/014-after-1.jpg, clip pieces name.mov.part02of05, job.json, notes.txt); 5xx and 'already there with the same size' are handled on the server, 429 tells the phone to wait. Vercel env: JOBKIT_GITHUB_TOKEN (fine-grained token, only raf-job-inbox, Contents read and write) and JOBKIT_PIN; without them the routes answer 503 not-set-up and the phone keeps everything. The app can only send in, never read out. Why GitHub: this workspace can reach github.com (and git) but not Vercel Blob, Google Cloud or rafcarpentry.com, and Claude Code sessions can't create repos or releases, so Raf makes the repo and token once. **Processing a sent job** (Raf: 'process job kit ...'): attach rafmoney503/raf-job-inbox with add_repo, clone it next to this repo (../raf-job-inbox), then `python3 scripts/job-kit-fetch.py --list` and `python3 scripts/job-kit-fetch.py [words]` (default: newest job marked sent) -> /mnt/user-data/uploads/Documents/Raf Carpentry Projects/<YYYY-MM What Area>/ with 0 Before, 1 Finished (+ reveal clip), 2 Build steps (+ other clips), 3 Drawings, notes.txt, photos.txt (file: slot label, caption), job.json; clip pieces joined, file times set to when each was taken (job-contact-sheets.py falls back to that time for these folders). Then the usual steps. Real sizes from notes.txt mean the 3D model is drawn to them (no 'standard sizes' note). Put `"jobKit": "<job id>"` in the new job JSON: the /job-kit page reads it at build (getAllProjects) and the job's Send screen and card in the app then say 'Done: it's on your website' with a link to the page (before that: 'Received in your inbox' with the time, and the line to tell Claude). Raf asked for that confirmation on 10 Oct 2026. Tested locally against a mock GitHub (scratchpad jk/mock-github.mjs + jk/test.js, Playwright as an iPhone 13: PIN, new job, photos with an EXIF rotate tag come out upright, a 8.6 MB clip in 3 pieces rejoins byte for byte, offline then back online, notes, Send, reload on a tab).
- **Posts for Instagram, TikTok and YouTube Shorts (10 Oct 2026, Raf: 'create something similar for sending to instagram and TikTok or you tube', then 'Nice let's do it')**: a Posts screen in the Job Kit (home card 'Posts for Instagram, TikTok and YouTube' -> list -> one pack). Automatic posting was advised against for now: TikTok keeps API posts private until it audits the app, Instagram needs a Meta developer app, YouTube a Google project, and trending sounds can only be added inside the apps. So each pack is shared by hand through the phone's share sheet: per platform (Instagram Reel, Instagram photos, TikTok, YouTube Short) a button copies the caption (sync execCommand copy + clipboard API, so the share sheet still opens from the same tap) and calls navigator.share({files}) with the video or photos, which the page downloads into memory as soon as the pack opens (the share sheet only opens straight from a tap); numbered steps under each button say where to tap in that app, including where the sound button is and the business-safe sound lists (TikTok Commercial sounds, Instagram's cleared list for business accounts, YouTube Shorts list, clips under 60 s); fallback on a computer: the files download. 'Posted' ticks per platform are kept in localStorage raf_jobkit_posted (this phone only); the list puts packs with everything posted last and marks the first one 'Next up'. A job page made from the Job Kit shows 'Post it' in the app's Done box when a pack exists for it. Packs: `python3 scripts/social-pack.py scripts/social/<slug>.json` (recipe: reel pieces in order, carousel, captions per platform; see the script's header) -> public/social/<slug>/ reel-<hash>.mp4 (1080 x 1920, 30 fps, H.264 CRF 21, about 15 to 20 s), slide-NN-<hash>.jpg (1080 x 1350, up to 10), poster-<hash>.jpg, plus content/social/<slug>.json, which /job-kit reads at build. Look: the site's drafting paper: a paper card with the title (Bricolage) and place (Geist Mono, blue) on the first piece, numbered paper step tags (01 Drawer boxes first...), landscape photos shown on a white mount on graph paper, an end card with the ink R (scripts/social/r-ink.png), 'Raf Carpentry', 'Fitted furniture in London', rafcarpentry.com; text kept in the top left, clear of the apps' buttons and captions. Videos are deliberately silent (a silent AAC track): Raf adds a licensed sound in each app (his question 'how do we solve the problem with sounds'); the original clip sound is left out because these clips have background sound (talking, maybe a radio) that could not be checked here. Clips come from the original MOVs staged from his Mac (/mnt/user-data/uploads/Documents/Raf Carpentry Projects/...), photos from public/images/projects/<slug>/; a photo can also be 'src:name.jpg', a crop made for the post in scripts/social/src/<slug>/ (the Kennington kitchen photo was cropped from the original to cut out a person, EXIF stripped). First 3 packs: floating walnut desk Highbury (19 s, 12 MB), curved storage seat Kennington (19 s, 17 MB), office reception desk Brentwood (16 s, 4.5 MB; its before/after wipe is labelled 'Bare MDF' / 'Finished' because the before photo is the desk before the finish, not the empty office). Captions follow the site rules (facts from the job JSON only, no prices or timescales, never that Raf painted anything, UK English, no dashes), 5 to 10 hashtags on Instagram, 5 on TikTok, YouTube title + description with the job page link and #shorts. Next: packs for the other 28 jobs (Raf to say yes after trying these), and a pack made as part of processing each new Job Kit job.
- **Private 3D designs for customers (10 Oct 2026, Raf: 'generate that files with new jobs and send client to pan and open and close door and so on interior own phone')**: rafcarpentry.com/d/<code>, one page per design before a job is agreed, the link Raf sends on WhatsApp. src/app/d/[code]/ (page.tsx, design-view.tsx, opengraph-image.tsx: the WhatsApp preview is the model's still on the drafting-paper card), src/lib/designs.ts, content/designs/<code>.json {code, title, area, made, intro?, options[{name, text?, model: Model3DInfo, sizes[{label, value}]}], notes[], example?}. Each option is its own tab and its own model in the same viewer as the job pages (turn, zoom, full screen, open the doors, take it apart, tap a part). Under it: the sizes, then 'What do you think?' with two WhatsApp buttons with the message typed in ('I'm happy with the design: ... <link>' / 'I'd like to change: ...') and the phone number. No prices. Not linked anywhere, not in the sitemap, robots disallow /d/, noindex, no floating buttons; the code is random (8 letters and numbers, e.g. k7m2q9xd), area only, never the customer's name or street; a design updated after feedback keeps its code so the same link shows the new version. Unknown codes 404 (dynamicParams false). Making one: build the piece in the SketchUp connector at the customer's sizes (same pipeline as the job models: su-helpers.py, su-readback.py, su-to-glb.mjs, poster), put the .glb and still in public/designs/<code>/, write the JSON, push. Example live at /d/example: two options made from the East Finchley alcove wardrobes and the Palmers Green chimney-breast wardrobes models, labelled as an example (different rooms).
- **Design requests from the Job Kit (10 Oct 2026, Raf: 'can we have a special section in job kit where this chat can be present and generate the design', then 'Yes')**: home card 'Designs for customers' -> Designs list -> New design (what, area) -> the request: photos of the space (DESIGN_SPACE, section id 'before') and of sketches or pictures they like (DESIGN_IDEAS, 'drawings'), sizes, what they want, options to show, the customer's mobile (kept on the phone only, for Send on WhatsApp; never in job.json or notes.txt), Send for design. It is a Job Kit job with `kind: 'design'` and `design: {brief, options, mobile, changes[]}` (src/app/job-kit/designs.tsx, store.ts), id `design-<month>-<what>-<area>-<rand>`, so it travels like a job (branch job/design-... in the inbox; notes.txt is the brief). States: not sent, sending, sent (waiting), Design ready (link, Send on WhatsApp with the message typed in, Copy link, Open), Changes sent (the same link updates). 'Ready' comes from content/designs/<code>.json `request` (the request id) and `updatedFor` (the request's readyAt it answers, ISO, copied exactly): the app asks POST /api/job-kit/designs behind the PIN (the codes are what keep the pages private, so they are never put in the /job-kit page itself), on open and whenever the app comes back to the screen, and keeps the answer in localStorage raf_jobkit_designs. next.config traces content/designs/*.json into that route. Making them: scripts/DESIGNS.md (runbook), `python3 scripts/design-requests.py` (what needs doing; --fetch ID copies the brief and photos and prints the code: existing for an update, new random 8 characters otherwise), scripts/model-poster.mjs + scripts/poster-fit.py (stills, also for /d/<code>?tab via a tab number). job-kit-fetch.py leaves design requests out of its default pick. The Home jobs list leaves them out too. The back button in the top bar says where it goes (Jobs, Designs, Posts). **Talk it through (10 Oct 2026, Raf asked how to add a voice memo)**: Claude can't listen to audio and this workspace can't fetch a speech-to-text model (Hugging Face and OpenAI's model host are blocked), so voice becomes text on the phone: a big 'Talk it through' box (TalkBox in parts.tsx, notes.talk) at the top of a job's Notes tab and at the top of a design request, for dictating with the iPhone keyboard microphone (Settings, General, Keyboard, Enable Dictation). It goes into notes.txt as 'Talked through (dictated, not sorted yet):' and job.json notes.talk. When processing, sort it into the right fields and read it for meaning (dictation mishears words; typed sizes win over spoken ones). **Tidying (10 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Is there a way to delete finished design, job or send posts', then 'Yes please')**: removing only ever clears the phone (inbox and website keep everything). Home shows a 'Clear finished (N)' box under the jobs list when jobs are on the website (done map) with nothing left to send; tap, then 'Tap again to clear' within 5 s. One job: Send tab, bottom (only once sent). One design: bottom of the request. Posts: packs ticked on all four platforms fold into 'Posted everywhere (N)' (Show / Hide) at the bottom of the Posts list; 'All posted' when none are left; the home Posts card says 'N to post, M posted everywhere' (postedEverywhere in posts.tsx). **Scheduled task 'Job Kit designs'** (trig_01Qw5s1VqbQBN75fXbkqdFvQ, created 10 Oct 2026, cloud, auto approval, no notifications): `CRON_TZ=Europe/London 46 6-20/2 * * *` (6:46 to 20:46 every 2 hours); each run is a fresh session that runs design-requests.py, stops at 'Nothing to do.', otherwise follows scripts/DESIGNS.md (at most 2 a run) and pushes to main. Tested 10 Oct 2026 with a one-off run: in a scheduled session both repos are already cloned at /home/claude/raf-carpentry and /home/claude/raf-job-inbox (no add_repo tool there), git push works, the SketchUp connector builds (its Python API, not Ruby) and the Vercel connector reads deployments, with no approval prompts. The test left a branch trigger-check (this session's proxy refused to delete it) and a doc 'Job Kit test run (delete me)'. If runs fail, Raf can say 'make the new design' in a chat and the same runbook is followed there.
- **Wardrobe planner and template (11 Oct 2026, Raf: 'use the designs you have created ... just update sizes and internal parts', then the double = 1 / single = 0.5 idea and his rules; agreed in a 'Wardrobe builder' spreadsheet first)**: in a Job Kit design request, 'Plan a wardrobe' (src/app/job-kit/wardrobe-planner.tsx) saves `design.wardrobe` (sent in job.json with `wardrobePlan`, and as lines in notes.txt). Choices: build MDF (floor to ceiling on an 80 plinth, all doors the same width) or IKEA PAX (100 / 50 cm frames, 2010 high with a small top box above, or 2364 for taller ceilings with a taller top box; under 300 above = infill strip; what's left of the width = fillers, split both ends); wall to wall (different every job), ceiling (Raf's usual 2400), depth 600; layout chips 1, 1 + 0.5, 0.5 + 1, 1 + 1, 1 + 1 + 0.5, 0.5 + 1 + 0.5, 1 + 1 + 1, plus + Double / + Single / Remove last (up to 6); left and right end (end panel is Raf's usual, wall = 20 scribe, nothing); per unit the inside as a stack, bottom to top (11 Oct, Raf: 'if ikea we add basket then it usually go at bottom then drawers above, shelf, short hanging, shelf'): pieces IKEA baskets (count, IKEA only), drawers (count), shelf, shelves (count), short hanging, long hanging, shoe shelf, added with + buttons (baskets go to the bottom, drawers above them, others under a top shelf), moved with up/down, removed with x; one-tap presets Hanging + top shelf, Drawers + hanging, Baskets + drawers + hanging (IKEA: 2 baskets, 1 drawer, shelf, short hanging, shelf: 2 + 2 is 142 mm too much for a 2010 frame with the suggested heights), Two rails, Shelves; baskets, drawers, shoe shelf and shelves take their own height (a shelf that is the last piece also gets 300 above it), hanging and groups of shelves share the rest in proportion to the least each needs (long 1400, short 1000, shelves 250 a gap), and the unit says 'N mm to spare' or 'N mm too much inside'; old requests (bottom/above/topShelf) are converted by normalise(); the drawing is a front elevation to scale on graph paper with Doors / Inside views (doors with dashed opening lines pointing to the hinge, handles, panel lines; inside shows baskets dashed, drawers, shelves, rails with hangers), blue width and ceiling dimension lines, unit sizes under the floor line; once a design is ready, 'Update the 3D design' under the planner sends it again (change 'Wardrobe planner changed') and the same link updates; doors flat / shaker / panelled; handles knobs / bars / push. Live sums and warnings and a front view drawn to scale. Raf's rules (11 Oct): ceiling 2400 usually, ends usually end panels, MDF floor to ceiling, IKEA 2000 + small unit above or 2300 tall + taller top box. Still Claude's suggested numbers (Raf hasn't changed them): plinth 80, scribe 20, door gap 3, MDF doors 300 to 600, IKEA filler warning over 250, top box from 300, drawer 200, basket 160, shoe shelf 250, top shelf space 300, long hanging 1400, short 1000. The sums live in src/lib/wardrobe.ts and, the same, in scripts/wardrobe/plan.py (checked equal on 400 random specs, old and new shapes; Python rounds halves like Math.round). The 3D model: `python3 scripts/wardrobe-model.py <wardrobe.json or job.json> scripts/models/design-<code>-a.su.json public/designs/<code>/a-<hash>.glb --name ...` (about a second; prints the option text, sizes and warnings) runs scripts/wardrobe/build.py (the template, written with the su-helpers.py functions, no imports) through scripts/wardrobe/local.py (su-helpers.py with solid/material/assembly/export_model swapped for plain Python giving the same read-back), then su-readback.py and su-to-glb.mjs. Parts are 'Unit N: ...' (apart by unit), doors hinge at a double's outer edges and a single's nearer end, open 90; drawers and baskets slide out after; top box doors open too; rails are named 'Hanging rail N (short/long)'. `--sketchup code.py` writes the same build as one block for the SketchUp connector (not tried there yet). design-requests.py --fetch saves wardrobe.json; scripts/DESIGNS.md step 3b. Tested: an MDF 1 + 1 + 0.5 shaker and an IKEA 1 + 1 + 0.5 panelled with baskets, drawers, shoe shelf and top boxes (closed, open, apart), and the planner at iPhone 13 size.

Saved as design.alcove / design.bookcase / design.chest (job.json also has alcovePlan etc., notes.txt has the lines). Sums in src/lib/planners/{alcove,bookcase,chest}.ts (no runtime imports, so scripts/planner-plan.mjs runs them with node --experimental-strip-types). 3D: `python3 scripts/planner-model.py <kind>.json OUT.su.json OUT.glb --name ...` (wardrobe goes to wardrobe-model.py) builds with scripts/planners/build.py (build_alcove: room wall, chimney breast, cast iron fireplace and hearth as room context, side walls, base carcass with doors, shelves wall to wall; build_bookcase: dividers, cupboards deeper than the shelves, top, back, shelves per bay, doors; build_chest: carcass, top, plinth or legs, dividers between columns, fronts with drawer boxes that slide out top first, knobs (two on fronts over 500) or bars). Tested 11 Oct: an alcove pair with shaker base cupboards, a 3-bay bookcase with panelled cupboards and bars, a 4-row chest with two small top drawers on legs (closed and open), and the planners at iPhone 13 size. design-requests.py --fetch saves <kind>.json; DESIGNS.md step 3b covers all four.
- **Planner changes (11 Oct 2026, Raf: 'in every planner add next to its name open close section ... if i want to use different planner everything from previous resets', 'in Pax there is 1000 unit which in real is 998, also can we add another unit 750 which is 748 and 500 which is 498', 'in alcove planner sometimes customers want have same things on both sides and sometimes different on each side and sometimes just left or right side'; he called the drawings amazing and the bookcase and chest planners great and perfect)**: every planner has an Open / Close button beside its name (PlannerHeader in planner-parts.tsx); closed it shows one line of what is picked and keeps everything; 'Use a different planner' (also in the wardrobe now) clears it and the next planner starts fresh. Wardrobe build chips are 'MDF' and 'IKEA PAX' with a line under them; PAX units are 1000, 750 and 500 (unit type 'double', 'mid', 'single'; really 998, 748 and 498: RULES.paxDouble/paxMid/paxSingle in wardrobe.ts and plan.py); a 750 has two doors (Claude's choice, about 372 each; not confirmed by Raf); IKEA layouts read in PAX sizes ('1000 + 750 + 500') with + 1000 / + 750 / + 500 buttons and extra presets 1000 + 750, 750 + 1000, 1000 + 1000 + 750; MDF stays '1 + 0.5' with + Double / + Single, and a 750 becomes a double if the build is switched to MDF (normalise); the drawing shows the PAX size and the real width under each unit; the unit cards say 'PAX 1000' etc. Alcoves: 'Which alcoves' is Both, the same / Both, different / Left only / Right only; the spec now has `same` and a `left` and `right` fit each ({base, baseH, baseD, shelves, shelfD}); the same choice edits both at once; different shows a Left alcove and a Right alcove box; old flat requests are converted by normaliseAlcove(). Checked: TS and Python wardrobe sums equal on 400 random specs with 750 units; a PAX 1000 + 750 + 500 at 2300 (filler 20, top box 390) and alcoves with a cupboard on the left and shelves only on the right built and opened in 3D; at iPhone 13 size no sideways scroll, closing keeps the sizes, a different planner starts empty.
- **Several planners per request (11 Oct 2026, Raf: 'when filling one planner and close or save it i want to have access to other planners or have access to copy of existing', then 'yes' to one tab each with Copy for options)**: a design request holds a list `design.plans` [{id, name, kind: wardrobe|alcove|bookcase|chest, spec}] (store.ts PlanItem; plansOf() turns an older request's single design.wardrobe/alcove/bookcase/chest into a one-item list; saving writes plans and clears the old fields). Up to 4 (MAX_PLANS). Planners.tsx shows them in order, each with Copy and Open / Close beside its name and 'Remove this planner' (tap twice) at the bottom; '+ Add a planner' opens the four choices (with Cancel); Copy puts an exact copy straight after it named '<name>, option B' (C, D), a second piece of the same kind is '<name> 2'; a line says the customer's page shows them as N tabs in this order; one 'Update the 3D design' under the list once the design is ready. job.json sends `design.plans` [{id, name, kind, spec, plan}]; notes.txt lists 'Planner a: <name> (<kind>)' with its lines. design-requests.py --fetch writes a-<kind>.json, b-<kind>.json... and prints 'option a: <name>'; DESIGNS.md 3b: each planner is one option (tab) on the page, same order, named as Raf named it. Tested at iPhone 13 size: wardrobe, a copy changed to shaker, a chest of drawers, all closed; saved plans read back from the phone's storage; no sideways scroll.

It opens on the inside (shelves, rails with hangers, drawers, baskets; IKEA top box doors stay on), with a light underlined link under the drawing, 'Show with the doors on' / 'Show the inside', instead of the dark Doors / Inside switch. Under each unit only its real width (e.g. 1182, 998), as the alcove shows 900. Keep all planners on this one look.
- **Today screen and Current jobs from ServiceM8 (11 Oct 2026, Raf: 'jobs or current jobs where type the person name, general enquiry info, date created, status quote, in progress, finished ... it will be my primary morning routine'; he invoices and keeps customers in ServiceM8, so it is connected rather than copied)**: Raf upgraded ServiceM8 to Starter (Free has no API access) and made a Full access API key, in Vercel as SERVICEM8_API_KEY (production, sensitive). src/lib/servicem8.ts + POST /api/job-kit/sm8 (behind the PIN; actions list / enquiry / status; SERVICEM8_API overrides the address for tests): reads Quote, Work Order and Completed (last 30 days) jobs with `$filter=active eq 1 and status eq '...'`, all active clients, job contacts and client contacts (best contact: the job's own, else billing/primary, empty ones skipped); a new enquiry reuses a client with the same name or makes client + BILLING contact, then a Quote job (job_address, job_description) + JOB contact, sending our own uuids; status changes POST job/<uuid>.json {status}. Nothing deletes anything in ServiceM8. Customer details go phone <-> server <-> ServiceM8 only. App (src/app/job-kit/current.tsx): Home is now 'Today' (date, a list of what needs doing: new enquiries to quote, quotes to follow up after 5 days, designs ready to send, designs being made, jobs going ahead, Job Kit jobs still open or waiting for the website page, posts to post; each line opens its screen), then cards Current jobs / Designs / Posts, then 'Job Kit jobs' (New job and the list). Current jobs: Enquiries and quotes / Going ahead / Finished, last 14 days (hide per job); a job: WhatsApp, Call, Email, Where it's up to (Quote, Going ahead, Finished; 'I've sent the quote' is a mark on the phone because Raf sends quotes by WhatsApp, ServiceM8's own quote_sent counts too; going ahead / not going ahead / finished take two taps and change ServiceM8), Follow up on WhatsApp, Make a 3D design for them (design request filled in: what from the description, area from ServiceM8's town or postcode, the mobile, linked by `sm8`), Start the Job Kit (linked the same way); linked items show as chips. New enquiry: name, mobile, email, address, Talk it through; the draft stays on the phone until ServiceM8 has it. The list, the marks and the draft are kept in localStorage (raf_jobkit_sm8, raf_jobkit_sm8_marks, raf_jobkit_enquiry); refreshed on open and when the app comes back, at most once a minute (Refresh forces it). Designs record when Send on WhatsApp was tapped (`sharedAt`) so 'ready to send' clears. **Unsuccessful (11 Oct 2026, Raf: 'can we also have unsuccessful after quote ... hide the job for some time and later delete', advised not to delete in ServiceM8)**: the steps in a job are buttons (tap, then 'Tap again' within 5 s) with a full-width 'Unsuccessful (didn't go ahead)' under them; the server also reads Unsuccessful jobs marked in the last 30 days; they leave the main list and sit folded in 'Unsuccessful, last 30 days (N)' at the bottom of Current jobs (Show / Hide), each saying when it drops off, with 'They came back: back to Quote'; after 30 days they drop off the Job Kit by themselves while ServiceM8 keeps them under Unsuccessful. **ServiceM8's own status names and money (11 Oct 2026, Raf: 'take from servicem8 status of the job like quote, work order, completed, unsuccessful ... and also maybe payments if there were made any and what is left')**: the screens use ServiceM8's words (groups 'Quote: enquiries and quotes', 'Work Order: going ahead', 'Completed (last 14 days, or still owing)', 'Unsuccessful, last 30 days'; steps Quote / Work Order / Completed + Unsuccessful; 'They said yes: move to Work Order', 'Job finished: move to Completed'); statuses always come from ServiceM8 on each refresh, so a change made in ServiceM8 shows here too. The server also reads jobpayment.json (active, grouped by job; if ServiceM8 refuses it the jobs still come, payments null) and the job's total_invoice_amount: each CurrentJob has `total` and `payments` [{amount, date, method, deposit}]; a card shows 'Quote £4,800' or '£12,000 · paid £3,000 · £9,000 left' / 'paid in full' (payment_received counts as paid in full); a job has a Money box (Total / Paid / Left, each payment with date, method, deposit); completed jobs stay listed while money is owed (within the 30 days ServiceM8 sends); Today adds '£1,350 left to collect on 1 completed job'. Payments are only read, never added, from the Job Kit. Tested against a pretend ServiceM8 (scratchpad jk/sm8mock.mjs + sm8-test.js, money-test.js) only: the real API was not reachable from here, so the first real use is the test; errors show ServiceM8's own message on the screen. **Diary from ServiceM8's schedule (12 Oct 2026, Raf: 'in job kit can i have also shown meeting with customers, work on given day for what customer, future meetings ... do we need to connect apple calendar'; he picked ServiceM8 as the one place for visits and work days)**: no new connector: the same SERVICEM8_API_KEY reads ServiceM8's bookings ('job activities': jobactivity.json, scheduled ones only, from yesterday on; asked with `start_date gt '<date> 00:00:00'`, and if ServiceM8 turns that filter down, all active ones picked on the server) and staff.json, in the same 'list' call; a booking on a job outside the list (finished or lost long ago) fetches that job too. Each Booking {uuid, jobUuid, start, end, staffUuid} (London time 'YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM'); if ServiceM8 won't send them the jobs still come (bookings null). Kind comes from the job: a booking on a Quote job is a 'Visit', on Work Order or Completed 'Work'. New actions on /api/job-kit/sm8: book {job, start, end, staff?} (POST jobactivity.json with activity_was_scheduled '1'; staff = the first active staff member unless picked), move {uuid, start, end}, cancel {uuid} (DELETE, which ServiceM8 treats as archive: active 0, restorable there; the only removal anywhere in the Job Kit). App: Today lists the rest of today's bookings first with their time in blue ('21:00 Work: Priya Patel, Crouch End', up to 4, then 'N more today'), then 'Tomorrow 10:00, Visit: ...' or 'Tomorrow: 3 bookings, first at 8:00'; a 'Diary' card under the Today box ('3 today · 7 in the next 7 days', or 'Nothing this week; next Thu 16 Oct, 9:00'); the Diary screen (current.tsx Diary) lists Today and Tomorrow always and every later day with a booking, each booking with its time, Visit/Work, name, area and what, and on today's ones Directions (Apple Maps), On my way (WhatsApp) and Call; a booking over several days shows on each day ('8:00, until Sun 18 Oct 16:00', 'From Sat 17 Oct to 16:00'); tapping opens the job (back says Diary). On a job, 'In the diary' (BookingsBox) under Where it's up to: its bookings still to come, each with Confirm on WhatsApp (message typed in: 'just to confirm I'll come round tomorrow at 10:00 to have a look and measure up' for a visit, 'I'll be with you on ...' for work), Move (day, from, to; a multi-day booking also has Last day and keeps its length when the day changes) and Cancel (tap twice); 'Book a visit' (presets 1 hour, 30 min, 2 hours from the start time; default tomorrow 9:00 to 10:00) or 'Book work' (All day 8 to 16, Morning 8 to 12, Afternoon 12 to 16; default tomorrow 8:00 to 16:00); a Who choice only if ServiceM8 has more than one staff member. Saved in ServiceM8 first, then shown; the list and bookings stay on the phone with the jobs (raf_jobkit_sm8). Apple Calendar: not linked directly (would need an Apple app-specific password on the server and two diaries to keep in step); ServiceM8's External Calendars add-on shows its schedule in the iPhone Calendar if Raf wants it there. Tested only against the pretend ServiceM8 (jk/sm8mock.mjs now has staff, jobactivity, gt/lt filters, DELETE, and NO_GT=1 to refuse date filters; jk/diary-test.js and diary-test2.js as an iPhone 13: today's lines, the diary days, multi-day spans, book, move, a bad time, cancel, moving a two-day booking a day later, the fallback): whether ServiceM8 takes the date filter and which staff member a booking goes to are first checked on Raf's real account. **Icons (12 Oct 2026, Raf: 'add some miniature icons next to corresponding sections like diary and so on')**: src/app/job-kit/icons.tsx draws six line icons in the sketch style (diary page, job card on a clipboard, a box seen from the corner for designs, a phone playing a clip for posts, a camera for Job Kit jobs, a pound coin for money owed): each Today card has its icon on a small square of graph paper (IconTile), each Today line shows the icon of the section it opens (blue when it needs doing, grey otherwise, instead of the old dots; booking lines keep their time), and the Diary, Current jobs, Designs and Posts screen titles and the Job Kit jobs heading carry the same icon. **Paste a brief into a new enquiry (12 Oct 2026, Raf, with a customer's website brief that came by WhatsApp: 'is it possible to copy this input and put to something in job kit that will send all details to servicem8 create new contact, save it and create new job')**: New enquiry (Current jobs) has a 'Got their brief or message on WhatsApp?' box at the top: 'Paste and fill in' reads the clipboard (navigator.clipboard.readText; if the phone won't allow it, the box below takes a press-and-hold Paste, and pasting there fills in at once; typed text has 'Fill in from this'). src/app/job-kit/brief-paste.ts parseBrief() reads the website brief's own format (brief-builder.tsx: 'Hi Raf, this is <name>. Here is my brief from your website.', the What / Room(s) / For / Material / Finish / Rough size / When / Budget / Mobile / Address / Parking / Parking and access lines, the 'Something like your job' line and link, their own words, 'I will send photos of the space next.' or the email's 'Photos of the space attached.') into name, mobile, address and a description for ServiceM8: the What list as the first line (so the card and whatOf() read well), the other lines, their words, then 'From the website brief.'; any other message becomes the description as it is, with a name ('this is / I'm / my name is ...'), a UK mobile and an email picked out; several WhatsApp messages copied together lose their '[date, time] Name: ' starts, and an unsaved sender's number becomes the mobile. A line under the box says what was filled in and, with no mobile, how to copy it from the WhatsApp chat. Nothing is sent until Add to ServiceM8 (client + billing contact, Quote job with the address and description, job contact, as before); the draft stays on the phone. The website brief (step 07) now also has an optional 'Mobile' ('So I can call you back'), written into the message as 'Mobile: ...' just before the address, so a pasted brief usually fills the mobile too. Tested with made-up briefs (scratchpad jk/brief-test.mts with node --experimental-strip-types; jk/paste-test.js as an iPhone 13 against the pretend ServiceM8: clipboard paste fills name and address and says to add the mobile, the POSTs carry the right client, contact and job; a plain message fills name and mobile; the brief shows the Mobile line). Customers' real briefs are never put in tests or the repo. **'Character Set Not Supported' (12 Oct 2026)**: the first real enquiry (a pasted website brief) came back from ServiceM8 as 400 {"message":"Character Set Not Supported"}. ServiceM8's docs say nothing about it; text copied from WhatsApp carries invisible marks (direction marks round a copied number, joiners inside emoji) and emoji, and the brief's budget has the pound sign. Now every text sent to ServiceM8 goes through tidy() in src/lib/servicem8.ts (invisible marks, emoji and the older emoji symbols out; curly quotes, long dashes, ... and odd spaces made plain; accents and the pound sign kept), the mobile becomes digits, a leading + and spaces, and the JSON body is sent as ASCII bytes (other letters as \u escapes). If ServiceM8 still answers 'character set', call() sends the same request again with plain English letters only (accents off, pound sign as GBP): first as a form (as ServiceM8's own add-on examples post), then as JSON marked charset=UTF-8. The phone also strips invisible marks when a brief is pasted and from what's typed in New enquiry. A new enquiry is now safe to send again after a try that stopped half way: a client is found by name in any case (all active clients are read and matched when the name filter finds none), a client with no contact gets its billing contact, and a Quote job of that client with the same description is reused (with its job contact added if missing) instead of a second job. Which of these ServiceM8 actually needed is not known (not reachable from here): tested against the pretend ServiceM8 with CHARSET=raw / astral / ascii / noform refusal modes (scratchpad jk/charset-test.mjs, charset-ui.js).
- **Design library (12 Oct 2026, Raf: 'where do i find all designs you have created with trimble sketchup, can we make like a library in job kit of those designs so i can share with clients whenever they interested in something similar')**: Job Kit screen 'Design library' (src/app/job-kit/library.tsx; Home card under Designs for customers with a books-on-a-shelf icon 'library' in icons.tsx; a link under New design on the Designs screen; 'Send them a design from the library' on a current job). It lists every 3D design, newest first: the customer designs (content/designs, not the example; sent by POST /api/job-kit/designs behind the PIN as `library` [{code, title, area, made, options, poster, words}] beside `ready`, kept on the phone in localStorage raf_jobkit_library; fetchDesigns() in designs.tsx) and every job with a 3D model (built into the /job-kit page from getAllProjects + modelOf as LibraryJob, public anyway). Search box (src/lib/search.ts matches over title, area, type, tags, summary, option names and still captions), quick words (Wardrobe, Alcove, Bookcase, Desk, Seat, Doors, Panelling, Kitchen), All / Customers / Jobs. Each card: still (next/image at 104 px), title, area and date, 'Customer design' or 'Finished job' (· N options / · standard sizes), WhatsApp (message typed in; opened from a current job it goes to that customer's chat and starts 'Hi <first name>'), Copy link, Open. A customer design is sent as /d/<code>/like (src/app/d/[code]/like/page.tsx + opengraph-image.tsx): the same 3D and sizes ('The sizes of this one'), kicker 'A design I made · <month>', 'I drew this in 3D for a home in <area>. Yours would be made to your own room and sizes.', without the first customer's intro, option text and notes (written to them), and 'Something like this for you?' with one WhatsApp button 'I'd like something like this' (message ends 'My room: ') instead of approve / change; noindex, not linked, /d/ disallowed; /d/example/like is a 404. A finished job is sent as /portfolio/<slug>#in-3d (model plus the real photos). New designs appear in the library as soon as their JSON is pushed (the API reads content/designs at run time). Tested as an iPhone 13 (scratchpad jk/library-test.js): 37 items (6 customer, 31 jobs), search, copy, a current job's chat link, back labels (Job, Designs, Today), the like page, no sideways scroll at 320.

See it in 3D first' with the first 5 kinds' short names and 'All 31' (home.css .hero-plan*, a sideways row on phones); the brief (brief-builder.tsx, designKinds prop from how-it-works/page.tsx) shows 'See 9 wardrobes in 3D' links under What is it? for what is ticked (new tab, so the brief stays filled in); job pages' 3D link (AllIn3DLink now takes href + label; PlanDrawings takes allLink); service pages 'Browse all 31 designs in 3D, by type'; home SketchUp section 'See all 31 designs in 3D' (was /sketchup#in-3d); SketchUp page gallery 'Find one by type: wardrobes, alcoves, desks and more'; menu '3D designs' after My Work (menu link padding px-2 below xl so 8 links fit at 1024 with no sideways scroll); footer 'Designs in 3D'; /ms card 'Designs in 3D' after My Work (ms.json). Job Kit library: above the list a card sends a whole kind (from the word typed or tapped, e.g. 'All 9 wardrobes as one link', rafcarpentry.com/designs/wardrobes) or, with nothing typed, the whole page, with WhatsApp ('Here are 9 wardrobes I've built, all in 3D...'; 'Hi <first>' from a current job), Copy link and Open; not on the Customers tab or for words that aren't a kind. - **Menu panels: each page's sections as # tags (13 Oct 2026, Raf: 'main menu buttons like services, my work and show names of hashtags or section from given page', mockup on the 'Hover previews' canvas, then 'yes')**: on a computer (lg+, mouse only: pointerType 'mouse') resting on a menu item for 180 ms drops a panel under the header (src/components/Navbar.tsx + MenuPanel.tsx): the page's kicker, title, one line, a link to it; its sections as # tags (Link chips with a blue mono #, a count where there is one); sometimes a short list; a picture from the page. Moving to another item swaps at once; leaving the menu and panel closes after 250 ms; Escape, a click or a new page closes it; keyboard focus (focus-visible) opens it; the open item gets a blue underline. Touch screens never get it (a tap goes to the page). The phone menu has a + beside each item that opens the same tags (and lists) under it; the menu scrolls (max-h 100dvh - 64). Data: src/lib/menu-panels.ts getMenuPanels(), built in src/app/layout.tsx and passed through LayoutShell (menu prop): Services (10 services with job counts, newest job's photo), My Work (the quick filters, shared with the My Work page through src/lib/portfolio-chips.ts PORTFOLIO_CHIPS / portfolioChips / jobSearchText; tags go to /portfolio?q=), 3D designs (the kinds with counts), SketchUp (#in-3d, #benefits, #compare, #plans only once a plan is for sale, #learn, #get-a-design), CabinetOS (#screens, #features, #compare, #sizes, #faq, #try), Blog (#jobs 'Jobs, step by step' and #guides 'SketchUp and CabinetOS' with counts, the 3 newest posts; the blog page is now grouped into those two sections, isGuide() = category SketchUp or Cabinetos), Tools (each kit category, ids from toolsSectionId(name) e.g. power-tools, measuring-layout; plus calculator, tips, Timber Stack), About (#story, #different, #follow). The section ids were added to those pages; no scroll-mt on them because html already has scroll-padding-top 88px (globals.css; the old scroll-mt-24 on the SketchUp page's #in-3d/#learn/#plans and scroll-mt-20 on CabinetOS #sizes were removed too, they doubled it): every jump lands 88 px down, under the sticky menu. No fs checks on public/ in menu-panels.ts: the home page is regenerated hourly on Vercel, where public/ isn't traced, so a check would drop the pictures (the home page's .nft.json does include content/pages, projects, blog, designs, project-kit). Tested (scratchpad jk/menu-test.js, anchor-test.js): opens after the pause, swaps, stays open in the panel, a tag click goes to /cabinetos#sizes and closes, leaving and Escape close, Tab opens, 1024 no sideways scroll, phone + shows 10 service tags and a tag closes the menu, a tablet tap goes straight to the page, all 19 jump points land at 88 px.
- **Save, search, jobs near you, what happens next (13 Oct 2026, Raf: 'create 1-4' from the 'Hover previews' canvas ideas)**: (1) **Save for my brief**: a heart on every 3D design (/designs viewer button 'Save for my brief' and a round heart on each still), every job page (beside Get a quote and WhatsApp) and every My Work card (round heart on the photo, outside the card link). src/lib/saved.ts (useSaved over localStorage `raf_saved`, newest first, up to 12, other tabs follow through the storage event; memory only if the browser blocks storage; nothing leaves the visitor's browser) and src/components/SaveButton.tsx (Heart, 'button' / 'icon'). A job saved from the designs page and from its job page is the same item (`job:<slug>`, link /portfolio/<slug>); a public customer design would be `design:<code>` (/d/<code>/like). The floating Plan your project pill shows a blue heart with the count and then goes to /how-it-works#brief (the list icon hides under 380 px so it never reaches the WhatsApp button; the count pops on each save in that tab). The brief shows 'Things you saved (N)' at the top of the form (still, title, area, remove) and the message gets a 'Things I liked:' block (title, area, then the link on its own line) after 'Something like your job' (the job from ?job= isn't listed twice); a line under the brief's 'See N wardrobes in 3D' links says to tap Save. The message never has two empty lines in a row. The Job Kit's pasted-brief reader turns that block into one 'Liked: A + B' line in the ServiceM8 description. (2) **Search everything**: a magnifier in the header on a computer (also / or Ctrl+K / Cmd+K; Escape, a click on the dimmed page, a result or a new page close it; menu panels don't open while it is open) drops a panel with Jobs (photo, area, month, 'the write-up mentions ...' when only the text matches, 'All N jobs' to /portfolio?q=), Blog and Also (services with the matching tag as '# Boiler cupboard', 3D kinds, pages, materials, questions, workshop tips, tools, and 'Start a brief: <choice>' which opens /how-it-works?what= / ?for= / ?room=...#brief with that choice ticked); Enter opens the best match (a page, service or 3D kind named by the words first). On a phone it is a box at the top of the menu; while something is typed the menu links hide. The list is /search-index.json (src/app/search-index.json/route.ts, force-static, built from src/lib/site-search.ts: jobs, post bodies, services, kinds, a hand list of pages with their words, tips, tools, materials, FAQ, brief choices; each entry's words once, about 76 KB), loaded the first time search opens and searched in the browser with src/lib/search.ts (src/components/SiteSearch.tsx). Nothing found: 'ask me on WhatsApp' with the words typed in. The header gaps shrink between lg and xl so the magnifier fits at 1024. (3) **Jobs near you** (home page, under Latest jobs, src/components/JobsNear.tsx): a postcode (full or the first half) or Use my location, looked up from the visitor's browser on api.postcodes.io (full postcode, falls back to its first half; reverse lookup for the location), not saved; then 'N jobs within 3 miles of N22, the nearest in Wood Green' and one row per area (its newest job, 'and 4 more there' to /portfolio?q=<area>, miles; 'here' under 0.3), 6 rows then Show all; 'Miles are to the middle of each area, not the house.'
