- **Send to Raf: the brief straight into ServiceM8 with photos (13 Oct 2026, Raf: 'go' to it after the area pages)**: Plan your project's brief has a blue 'Send to Raf' button above Send on WhatsApp / Email it / Copy text ('Straight to me, photos and all. Needs your mobile or email.'; it reads 'Send to Raf, with 3 photos' and 'Sending photo 2 of 3...'). Step 07 now has Mobile and an optional Email (the message gets an 'Email:' line; the Job Kit's pasted-brief reader takes it as the email); Send to Raf needs the name and a mobile (10+ digits) or an email, and points at the box if not. Step 10 'Your photos' has Add photos (up to 10, file input `image/*` multiple; each redrawn on the phone by shrinkPhoto from src/app/job-kit/media.ts: 2400 px JPEG, upright, no EXIF or GPS; thumbnails with remove). Flow (brief-builder.tsx sendDirect): POST /api/brief {name, mobile, email, address, message, photos, company, ms} -> createEnquiry (client, billing contact, Quote job, job contact, as the Job Kit's New enquiry; description = parseBrief(message).description plus 'Photos: N sent with the brief (in the job's diary).' and 'Sent with Send to Raf.'), then an email to info@rafcarpentry.com through ServiceM8's Messaging API (POST https://api.servicem8.com/platform_service_email {to, subject 'New brief from the website: <name>', textBody, regardingJobUUID}; best effort, waited on for at most 4 s: whether the account's API key may send email is not known yet, it may need an OAuth scope; the job is there and on Today either way) -> a token (src/lib/brief-token.ts: HMAC with BRIEF_SECRET or a key derived from SERVICEM8_API_KEY; job uuid, number of photos, 30 minutes) -> each photo POST /api/brief/photo multipart {token, i, file} -> attachPhoto (ServiceM8's one-request multipart POST attachment.json with file, related_object job, related_object_uuid, attachment_name brief-photo-01.jpg, and our own uuid from photoUuid(job, i), so a retry gets 409 and counts as done, and no more than N can ever be added; JPEG only, first bytes FF D8 FF, up to 3 MB) -> POST /api/brief/done (touchJob: saves the job's status again, ServiceM8 support's fix for API attachments not showing in the diary). Each request retries once after 1.5 s. Bots: a hidden 'company' box and under 4 s on the page get an ordinary 200 and nothing is made; more than 4 sends from one address in 10 minutes get 429 (per server instance only); field lengths capped. After sending, the card says 'Brief sent / Your brief is with me, with N photos', 01 'By phone or WhatsApp on <mobile>' (or 'By email to ...'), and either 'More photos help', 'N photos didn't go through' or (no photos) the photo tips, each with 'Send photos on WhatsApp' (message typed in with their name); the other send buttons hide so it can't arrive twice. The privacy line under the name is now 'Only sent to me. Nothing is kept on this website.'; the brief intro (how-it-works.json briefIntro) says 'Then send it straight to me with your photos, or on WhatsApp or by email.' Tested only against the pretend ServiceM8 (scratchpad jk/sm8mock.mjs now takes multipart attachment.json with 409 on a repeated uuid, ATTACH_FAIL=2,3 to fail uploads, platform_service_email with EMAIL403=1 to refuse; brief/ui.js as an iPhone 13: no-contact prompt, 4 files with a text file left out, remove and re-add, send, the POSTs in order, 3 attachments on the job, the saved photo upright with no EXIF; brief/api.js: hidden box, too fast, no contact, no name, email only, a repeated photo, photo number over the limit, PNG bytes, forged and made-up tokens, 6 sends from one address = 200 x4 then 429; a failed photo and a refused email still finish with the card saying 1 photo didn't go through). Confirmed on Raf's real ServiceM8 (13 Oct 2026, his test brief from his phone: 'works perfect'): the Quote job, the photos in its diary and the email all arrive, so ServiceM8's Messaging API (platform_service_email) accepts the account's API key.
- **Designs like yours and the automatic thank-you email (13 Oct 2026, Raf: 'is it possible to send automatically the similar answer or design from this enquiry ... the closest to the one in enquiry', then 'go')**: src/lib/design-match.ts closestDesigns(items, {what, room, uses, material, liked, address}) picks up to 3 job models closest to the brief (no fs; the same function runs in the browser and on the server): +10 for each of a design's kinds that a What choice is about (kindsIn), +4 for For/Room choices, +6 for kinds shared with what they saved with a heart or the ?job= job, +3 for each word of their For/Material/Room/What choices in its title, type, tags or summary (stems, a few stop words, small extra word lists for Hallway, Home office, Bathroom, TV and media, Hanging/Folded clothes; Finish is ignored), +2 when its area is in their address, a little for newer. Only designs scoring 3+ are shown (so sometimes 1 or 2); saved ones are left out; with no match at all the 3 newest show as 'Recent designs in 3D'. Each links to /designs/<kind>#<slug> (opens the 3D on it), plus 'All 9 wardrobes in 3D' (the kind asked for) or 'All 31 designs in 3D'. The list is matchItems() in src/lib/design-library.ts (every design on /designs with kinds, still and its words; about 10 KB, passed to the brief as `designs`). Shown on the Brief sent / What happens next card after any send (Send to Raf, WhatsApp, Email, Copy) as 'Designs like yours, in 3D' with stills, new tab. After Send to Raf, /api/brief (body now also has `choices` {what, room, uses, material} and `liked` hrefs) works out the same three and, when the customer gave an email, sends 'Thanks for your brief, <first name>' through ServiceM8 (sendEmail in src/lib/servicem8.ts: platform_service_email with regardingJobUUID so it is filed on the job, and replyTo info@rafcarpentry.com so replies reach Raf; if ServiceM8 refuses replyTo with 400 it is sent again without it): it's with me, reply within 24 hours by phone or WhatsApp on <mobile> (or by email), the designs with links, the 'All N' link, the three next steps in the site's own words, more photos by reply or WhatsApp, signed Raf, Raf Carpentry, phone, website (src/lib/brief-email.ts). Raf's own email lists the designs they were sent (or, with no email given, the ones the website showed them). Both emails go together, waited on for at most 5 s. next.config.ts traces content/projects, content/pages and content/designs into /api/brief. Not automated, on purpose (advised): WhatsApp or text replies (need Meta's WhatsApp Business API with templates, or ServiceM8 SMS credits) and a custom 3D design from the brief's rough sizes (the site promises the drawing after the visit; Raf uses Make a 3D design for them in the Job Kit). Tested (scratchpad match/cases.ts on 11 sample briefs; brief/match-ui.js as an iPhone 13 against the pretend ServiceM8: a saved Haringey PAX left out, wardrobe + hanging + PAX gives Finchley PAX and the two Palmers Green wardrobes with All 9 wardrobes, a WhatsApp send of a desk brief shows three birch ply desks, both email bodies; REPLYTO400=1 in sm8mock.mjs for the fallback; no sideways scroll at 320).

Kept on the phone: the last conversation per job (raf_jobkit_mail), what's being written until it's sent (raf_jobkit_mail_drafts), and emails sent from here until ServiceM8's list shows them (raf_jobkit_mail_sent). Server: /api/job-kit/sm8 actions `mail` {job, email} and `send-mail` {job, to, subject, text} -> src/lib/servicem8-mail.ts. Sending: POST /platform_service_email {to, subject, textBody, regardingJobUUID}, no replyTo, so it is filed on the job and the reply comes back to the job in ServiceM8 (two-way email); failures are shown (ServiceM8's 429 'Daily message quota exceeded' in plain words) and nothing is lost. Reading: GET /api_1.0/email.json (ServiceM8's May 2026 read-only endpoint, merged inbound and outbound history; came out account by account, fields not published): $filter related_object_uuid with the uuid in quotes, then without, then all and picked on the server; each field read from the likely names (text_body/body/message_text..., html_body/message_html..., subject, from/from_email..., timestamp/sent_date/... incl. ISO with Z, direction inbound/outbound), the text from email/<uuid>.json when the list hasn't got it, and an email whose words still can't be found shows its field names ('Tell Claude these names') so the reading can be fixed. If ServiceM8 won't let the account read emails ('email is not an authorised object type', 403, 404): each email sent from the Job Kit is also saved as a note on the job ('Email sent from the Job Kit', To, Subject, the text) and the conversation shows those copies, with a line that their replies are on the job in ServiceM8. 'list' also brings `replies` (job uuid -> their newest email in the last 14 days, from email.json filtered direction inbound; null if not readable): Today shows 'Email from <name>' (up to 3, envelope icon 'mail' in icons.tsx), the job card a blue 'New email' chip, until the job's Emails has been opened (marks mailSeen). A job with no email in ServiceM8 (13 Oct 2026, Raf's first try: 'nothing happens when press the button'; the only sign was small text under the empty To box): the To box now says 'There's no email on this job in ServiceM8. Type theirs here: it's saved on the job when you send.'; Send with something missing shows the reason above the button and puts the cursor in that box; the typed address is saved on the job contact without an email (else the client's contact), never over one already there (send-mail `save`); a brief whose What is 'Something else' / 'Not sure' gets the subject 'Your enquiry with Raf Carpentry'. Not yet tried on Raf's real account: whether email.json is switched on for it, and its real field names, are found out on first use (if it isn't, ServiceM8 Support can switch it on: Help menu in the Online Dashboard). The website emails changed with it: the customer's thank-you after Send to Raf no longer has replyTo info@ (their reply now comes back to the job in ServiceM8, so it shows in Emails), and Raf's own 'New brief' email has replyTo the customer (if they gave an email) plus 'To email them, use Emails on the job in the Job Kit'. Text: To = the job's mobile (else the number in the conversation), 'Hi <first>, ', signed 'Raf, Raf Carpentry' (SMS_SIGN_OFF in src/lib/mail-text.ts), a counter 'N characters · 1 text credit (one per 153 characters)' (textCredits), 'Send text through ServiceM8'. Reading: GET /api_1.0/sms.json the same way as email.json (quoted / unquoted / all; text from the likely names incl. message, numbers from from_number/to_number/from/to..., direction, own sms/<uuid>.json), merged with the emails into one conversation, oldest first, each bubble marked 'Email' or 'Text'; Raf's texts shown without the sign-off or ServiceM8's 'Reply here' link (textOnly). If you're happy with the work, a Google review would really help: www.rafcarpentry.com/review' + sign-off, one credit for first names up to about 13 letters; Email: subject 'Thank you from Raf Carpentry', the words of the website's Ask for a review tool, link https://www.rafcarpentry.com/review, the thank-you page with the Google button and the 'something not quite right? tell me' line); Raf checks it and sends. Not sent by itself, on purpose (a job can be Completed before the customer is happy or has paid). Sending a message with that link marks the job (marks reviewAskedAt; also seen from the conversation) and the box then says 'You asked <first> for a review on <day>. Write it again'. Today: 'Ask <name> for a review' (or 'Ask N finished jobs for a review') for jobs Completed in the last 14 days and not asked yet, with a new star-in-a-speech-bubble icon 'review' in icons.tsx. - **Find a customer in ServiceM8 (15 Oct 2026, Raf: 'in jobkit when type in person first name and last name can it search through service m8 database'; his second idea, automatic research of new enquiries on social media, was advised against: LinkedIn/Facebook/Instagram don't allow name lookups, scraping breaks their rules, common names pick the wrong person, and profiling every homeowner who fills in the form is a UK GDPR risk; offered instead: work email domain, EPC and planning data for the property, a 'Look them up' button that opens a Google/LinkedIn search for Raf to do himself, not built yet)**: ServiceM8's $filter only finds an exact name, so /api/job-kit/sm8 has `clients` (allClients() in src/lib/servicem8.ts: every active company + its companycontacts as {uuid, name, address, people [{first, last, mobile, phone, email}], edited}, most recently changed first) and the phone searches that list as Raf types (src/app/job-kit/customers.tsx: searchClients over indexClients; any part of a first or last name in any order, accents folded so lukasz finds Łukasz, O'Brien also as obrien, Anne-Marie as anne marie; people on a company count (Sarah O'Brien finds Smith Legal Ltd and shows her under it); digits find mobiles and phones with +44 / 0044 / 07 made the same; an @ finds emails; postcode or street words match too, below name matches; ties go to the most recently changed). Lists are read page by page: listAll() asks with cursor=-1 and follows ServiceM8's x-next-cursor header (pages of 1,000, up to 25 pages; an account that doesn't page sends everything at once). The list is kept on the phone (localStorage raf_jobkit_clients), fetched when the app opens or comes back and the search box is used, at most every 10 minutes (CLIENTS_FRESH; Refresh forces it); customers on a current job but not in the list yet are added from the jobs (withCurrent), so a new enquiry is findable at once. The box ('Find a customer: name, mobile, postcode') sits on Today under the Today list and at the top of Current jobs above New enquiry; results show the name, the person if different, postcode or first address line, mobile, and chips for their open Quote / Work Order; 25 shown, then Show all; nobody found offers 'New enquiry for <what was typed>'; what was typed is kept for the visit (sessionStorage raf_jobkit_find_today / _current). **A customer** (screen 'customer', src/app/job-kit/customer-view.tsx; `client` action = clientJobs(): company/<uuid>.json (archived or missing = 'sm8-gone'), its contacts, every active job of that company (job.json company_uuid filter, any status or age) shaped like CurrentJob with best contact and payments, newest first): name, address, each person with numbers, email, WhatsApp and Call; '4 jobs since Mar 2024 · £18,400 of work · £2,000 left to pay · 1 open quote' (Work Order and Completed totals; 'all paid' when nothing is left; left out when payments couldn't be read); 'New enquiry for <first name>'; the jobs (status and date, number, description, a different address, money). The last 12 customers opened stay on the phone (raf_jobkit_customers) so the page opens with no signal. A job there opens in the usual job screen (sm8 screen with from 'customer'; the app looks a job up in the Current jobs, then in customers opened: findJob), so an old Unsuccessful quote can go back to Quote, a finished one be asked for a review, emails and texts read; a status change on an older job updates it there and fetches the Current jobs again. CurrentJob now carries `client` (the company uuid; missing in lists kept from before) and a current job's From here starts with 'All jobs for <name>'. **New enquiry knows who's already a customer**: under Email, 'Already a customer?' (SameCustomer; sameCustomers(): same mobile (last 10 digits), same email, same full name, or two or more typed words starting their name words; strongest first, up to 3) with 'Yes, it's them' (puts it on that customer: enquiry `client`, fills the still-empty mobile, email and address; then 'Going on <name>'s record ... Not them: make a new customer') and 'See their jobs'; a line says what happens if nothing is picked (a same-name match: ServiceM8 puts it on them, as createEnquiry always did; else a new customer) and 'No, a different person' (enquiry `newClient`, a uuid made on the phone: createEnquiry makes a new company with that uuid even when the name matches, and a second try finds that same company via company/<uuid>.json instead of making another). 'New enquiry for <first>' on a customer's page starts filled in with them picked; an unsent draft for someone else is kept and offers 'Clear it and start one for <name>'. The enquiry action takes `client` / `newClient` (uuids checked); a picked customer archived meanwhile gives 'sm8-gone' ('ServiceM8 doesn't have that customer any more'). New icons 'search' (magnifier) and 'person' in icons.tsx. Tested against a pretend ServiceM8 only (scratchpad jk/sm8mock.mjs, made-up people: paging with PAGE=4 so the 15 customers come over 4 pages, NOPAGE=1 to ignore the cursor, an archived company, accents, two John Smiths, a company with a person, 404 on unknown uuids; jk/find-test.mjs as an iPhone 13, 36 checks: partial, reversed, accented and unaccented names, double-barrelled, a person on a company, mobile typed 07 against one saved +44, part of a mobile, postcode start and full, email, archived left out, nobody found, the customer page and summary, an old unsuccessful job brought back to Quote in ServiceM8, back labels, New enquiry for a customer (no new company), same name + same mobile ordering, a different person (new company with the phone's uuid), Yes it's them, the Current jobs box with the Work Order chip, All jobs for, no sideways scroll at 320). Confirmed on Raf's real ServiceM8 (15 Oct 2026, from his phone: 'works very good').
- **The property and Look them up on each job (15 Oct 2026, Raf: 'yes' to the safer version of his 'search automatically everything about this person' idea: facts about the building from public records, plus a button for him to search the person himself; nothing about the person is fetched or saved)**: on every job screen in the Job Kit, under Where it's up to, src/app/job-kit/property-box.tsx shows 'The property' and 'Look them up'. Server: /api/job-kit/sm8 action `property` {address} (behind the PIN, works without ServiceM8) -> lookupProperty() in src/lib/property.ts: readAddress() takes the postcode, flat ('Flat 3', 'Apartment 12B' -> 'Flat 12B'), house number ('12A', '22-24') or house name and street from the job address (tested on 9 shapes: scratchpad jk/address-test.mts); postcodes.io (lat/lng, admin_ward, admin_district); PlanIt (www.planit.org.uk/api/applics/json, lat/lng or pcode, krad 0.08, pg_sz 200; documented fields address, description, app_type, app_state (Permitted -> Approved, Conditions -> Approved with conditions, Rejected -> Refused...), start_date, decided_date, url, link, distance; sends a User-Agent because PlanIt answers 403 without a proper one): 'Planning at this address' = records whose address holds '<number> <first street word>' (so 120 Hall Lane isn't number 12) or the house name, any age, up to 10; 'Nearby, last 5 years' = up to 5 others, nearest first, with metres; HM Land Registry price paid by SPARQL (landregistry.data.gov.uk/landregistry/query, Accept application/sparql-results+json; lrcommon:postcode/paon/saon/street, lrppi:propertyAddress/transactionDate and, as OPTIONALs, lrppi:propertyType/estateType/newBuild with lrcommon URIs detached / semi-detached / terraced / flat-maisonette, freehold / leasehold): the latest sale of that house (that flat when the address has one; without a flat number the house itself, else a flat in the building, and the box says 'Going by a sale of Flat 2, 5 Weston Park'); shows type, tenure, 'new build when sold', 'last sold <year>'. The price is deliberately not fetched or shown (Raf can open the sold-prices link himself). Each source fails on its own (null -> 'didn't answer this time'); undefined sale = 'No sale on record for <house> since 1995'; no postcode = 'Add it in ServiceM8'. Leasehold adds 'building work may need the freeholder's or managing agent's permission'. Links: Energy certificate (find-energy-certificate.service.gov.uk/find-a-certificate/search-by-postcode?postcode=), Street View (Google Maps URLs map_action=pano at the postcode's lat/lng; Map search without it), 'Sold prices and old floor plans' (rightmove.co.uk/house-prices/<postcode-with-dash>.html); 'Look again' forces a new lookup. Kept on the phone 30 days per address (localStorage raf_jobkit_property, 40 addresses); a lookup where a source didn't answer is asked again after 10 minutes. 'Look them up': a work email's domain (anything not in the personal-mailbox list in property-box.tsx: gmail, hotmail, outlook, icloud, btinternet, sky, wp.pl, onet.pl...) shows 'Their email is at <domain>, not a personal mailbox' with See the website; Search Google ('"<name>" <borough>') and Search LinkedIn (people search) open in a new tab; 'Common names find other people too. Nothing is saved.' **Energy certificate figures (15 Oct 2026, Raf: 'yes')**: the government moved the EPC register to get-energy-performance-data.communities.gov.uk (free; GOV.UK One Login; the bearer token is on its /api/my-account page as 'Bearer token (for developers)'). Its docs pages can't be read from here (robots), so the API was built from the service's own public code: github.com/communitiesuk/epb-data-frontend lib/views/api_tech_docs (making_request, search_certificates, fetch_certificate_data) and epb-data-warehouse spec/fixtures/json_samples (field names across RdSAP 17 to 21 and SAP 16 to 19) and spec/fixtures/look_up_data.csv (age bands). epcOf() in src/lib/property.ts: GET https://api.get-energy-performance-data.communities.gov.uk/api/domestic/search?postcode=&page_size=500 with Authorization Bearer EPC_API_TOKEN and Accept application/json (404 = no certificates; 401/403 = token turned down, epcError 'token'), matches the house like the planning ('<number> <first street word>' in addressLine1-4, the flat when the address has one, else the house without a flat, else a flat in the building with a note), the latest registrationDate; then GET /api/certificate?certificate_number= for total_floor_area (rounded m²), habitable_room_count, dwelling_type (string, or {value} in older schemas), sap_building_parts[].construction_age_band (A before 1900 ... K 2007 to 2011, L '2012 or later' or '2012 to 2021' in RdSAP 21+, M 2022 or later) or .construction_year, current_energy_efficiency_band, registration_date; if that second call fails the search's rating and date still show. The box shows 'Energy certificate' first when EPC_API_TOKEN is set (epcReady): '93 m² · 5 rooms' large, 'Mid-terrace house · built 1930 to 1949 · rated D (2021)', a note on what floor area and rooms mean, and the link 'The energy certificate' to find-energy-certificate.service.gov.uk/energy-certificate/<number> (else 'No energy certificate on record for ...' and the postcode search link). Without the token nothing changes; a phone copy made without it is looked up again after a day, so adding the token shows within a day (or tap Look again). Tested against pretend data shaped like the official samples (scratchpad jk/property-mock.mjs /api/domestic/search and /api/certificate with Bearer test, EPC_DOWN, CERT_DOWN; jk/epc-test.mts running lookupProperty straight with node --experimental-strip-types, 9 checks: latest certificate for 12 not 120, area, rooms, type, age band, rating, {value} type and L in an older schema, the flat itself, a flat with none, a 404 postcode; no token gives nothing; a wrong token gives the token message; property-test.mjs now 27 checks). Raf to add EPC_API_TOKEN in Vercel (Production, Sensitive); the first real job opened after that is the real test. The website brief now says under the address: 'To prepare your quote I may check public records for the property, like its energy certificate and planning history.' Also fixed while testing at 320 px: the job screen's From here buttons wrap their long labels, and the Emails and texts form no longer pushes the page 2 px sideways (grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)] in mail.tsx). Tested against pretend sources only (scratchpad jk/property-mock.mjs: postcodes, PlanIt records with distance, SPARQL bindings, PC_DOWN / PLANIT_DOWN / LR_DOWN; jk/property-test.mjs as an iPhone 13, 20 checks: ward and borough, the Land Registry line with no price, planning here and nearby with the 5-year cut and 120 vs 12, the three links, the Google search words, no company for a personal email, the phone's copy used on a second open, a flat in a leasehold building, no planning, a work email with its website, no sale on record, no sideways scroll at 320; every source down gives nulls). PlanIt, postcodes.io and Land Registry can't be reached from this workspace, so the first real job opened is the real test: if the Land Registry line always says it didn't answer, the SPARQL endpoint or property names need checking. **One folded section (23 Oct 2026, Raf: 'in job card the property section should have drop down and maybe put together in one section with look them up section, combine this in a smart way, compact it'; live since 23 Oct 2026, Raf: 'merge')**: the two sections are now one, 'Property and customer', below Notes and the Job diary, folded to a row like the Job diary whose line under the title is the facts that matter ('86 m² · 4 rooms · Mid-terrace house · Freehold · 2 planning applications'; 'Looking up the property…', or 'No address on this job · search for them'). Open: postcode · ward, borough; the facts in two columns (Size, Building, Energy (EPC), Land Registry; a cell spans both when it's a sentence), the small notes (floor area, flat in the building, the leasehold warning in ink), Planning at this address (N) with each application's reference as its link on the date line, and the nearby ones folded behind '3 nearby in the last 5 years'; then every link in one place: the house (Energy certificate, Street View, Sold prices, floor plans) and 'Look up Priya' (Google, LinkedIn, the work email's website with its line); one footer line with the sources, 'Searches open for you to read; nothing is saved.' and Look again. Open or folded is remembered on the phone (localStorage raf_jobkit_property_open). The property is still looked up when the job opens, so the folded line is ready. Tested (scratchpad prop/test.js, seeded properties so nothing is fetched: one section where the two were, folded with the facts, nothing looked up again, open with the two columns, planning and nearby, the links, Look up Priya with the name and borough, remembered open and folded, a leasehold flat with no certificate, no sideways scroll and nothing past the card at iPhone 13, 320 and 1440, no page errors).

- **Draft a reply with Claude (15 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Can I forward email to you so you can analize add to service m8 database and create response email so I can approve and send', then 'Go')**: Claude has no email address and can't reach ServiceM8, and ServiceM8's own forwarding address (the Email Inbox add-on) needs the Growing plan (Raf is on Starter), so the flow is copy and paste: copy the client's email in Mail, Job Kit, Current jobs, New enquiry, Paste and fill in (picks name, mobile, email), Add to ServiceM8; the job opens and its Emails and texts box has 'Draft a reply with Claude' (open by itself when it's their turn: nothing sent to them yet, or they wrote last): an optional 'Anything it should say?' line (e.g. 'I can come round Tuesday at 10'), 'Write the email' / 'Write the text' (or 'Write a new ... draft' over what's there, with 'Put back what you had'), and the draft lands in the box with 'Claude's draft is in the box: check it, change anything, then send.' Nothing is sent until Raf presses Send. Server: POST /api/job-kit/draft (behind the PIN) -> src/lib/draft-reply.ts draftReply(): Anthropic Messages API (fetch, x-api-key ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, anthropic-version 2023-06-01, model ANTHROPIC_MODEL or claude-sonnet-5-5, falling back to claude-sonnet-5 if the account answers 404 / model; no temperature: Sonnet 5 refuses non-default ones; max_tokens 800; ANTHROPIC_API overrides the address for tests). The system prompt keeps it to the site's promises (reply within 24 hours, free visit to measure up, free quote, 3D drawing to approve before anything is cut), never prices, costs, deposits, dates, timescales or guarantees (say it's for the visit or the quote), never that Raf paints or stains, nothing made up about their room, no days or times unless Raf's note gives them (else ask what suits them), UK English, no dashes or emoji, no sign-off (added when sending); email 'Hi <first>,' and 2 to 4 short paragraphs; text one paragraph aiming under 130 characters (one credit with the sign-off). What goes to Claude: first name, what the job is (not when the description starts with a pasted email's greeting), area, status, ServiceM8's description, the last 8 messages, Raf's note, all through redact(): Address/Mobile/Phone/Email/Postcode lines dropped, emails, UK phone numbers (0 / +44 / 0044, 10+ digits), postcodes, house-number-and-street ('14 Oak Lane') and links other than rafcarpentry.com replaced with [email] [phone] [postcode] [address] [link], and the surname (from the client's name, sent only for this) taken out. tidyDraft(): quotes off, dashes to commas ('10 to 12' for number ranges), trailing Raf / Raf Carpentry / phone / website lines off, a text on one line. Errors in plain words (sm8-post.ts): ai-not-set-up (no ANTHROPIC_API_KEY), ai-key, ai-limit (spend limit or no credit: Claude Console, Billing), ai-busy, ai-error. A pasted email's first line ('Hello Raf,') no longer becomes the subject: 'Your enquiry with Raf Carpentry' instead. ANTHROPIC_API_KEY set in Vercel by Raf on 16 Oct 2026 (Production, Sensitive; a personal key from the Claude Console, platform.claude.com, scoped to the Default workspace only (a key for several workspaces would need an anthropic-workspace-id header, which draft-reply.ts doesn't send), expiry Never, prepaid credit with auto-reload off), live from the redeploy that evening. Tested against a pretend Claude (scratchpad mail/claude-mock.mjs, AI=401|limit|busy|model404; mail/start-ai.sh starts it with the pretend ServiceM8) and the pretend ServiceM8: mail/draft-api.mjs (both modes, nothing personal in what Claude gets, every failure, no key), mail/draft-ui.js as an iPhone 13 (a pasted email to a new job, panel open, email draft, note used, put back, send, Sarah's text draft, no sideways scroll at 320); sms-ui.js and ui.js still pass. Not yet tried with the real Claude API.
- **Quotes: rough prices for new enquiries, read by Claude (2 Nov 2026, Raf: 'In jobkit ... if new job come through from email to service m8 with pictures I'd like you to be in jobkit analyse it and create answer with proposed quote price ... I'd like to be able to respond with 24h ... various options to copy and paste if wardrobes make plain design with quote, ikea pax with mdf outside, custom mdf shaker and match colour from Cworkshop with their prices. I will approve or change prices once your quotes are ready'; previewed on the `quote-drafts` branch, live since 2 Nov 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: **What Claude does** (src/lib/quote-maker.ts): reads a Quote job in ServiceM8 (oneJob in servicem8.ts), its description, Raf's notes on it (jobNotes) and up to 8 photos from its diary (jobPhotos in servicem8-diary.ts, oldest first, each made 1568 px JPEG with sharp) through the Claude API with ANTHROPIC_API_KEY (the same prepaid key as Draft a reply, not Raf's Claude plan; QUOTE_MODEL, else ANTHROPIC_MODEL, else claude-sonnet-5-5, claude-sonnet-5 if the account hasn't got it), and answers through one forced tool, quote_read: the sizes of each separate space (and whether each came from the brief, a photo or a guess), what the photos show, what to check on the visit, up to 4 questions, the reply's opening sentence, their budget as they said it, for a wardrobe the units, drawers, top cupboards, what's inside and whether PAX suits it, up to 3 Cworkshop boards that suit the room (with why) and the inside board, its pick; for any other job up to 3 ways to do it, each by one of Raf's rates (kitchen, laminate m², loft m², decking m², doors, media wall) or days and materials. **Claude never sets a price.** It gets the first name only: contact details, the surname and street are taken out (redact() from draft-reply.ts). cleanRead() trims every field, turns dashes into commas, keeps only board codes from the list. **The prices** (src/lib/quote-sums.ts, no imports; checked by `node scripts/quotes/check.mjs`, 771 checks with Raf's prices written into it): worked out on the phone from Raf's price list, rounded up to £50, never under £330. Wardrobe options: 1 MDF flat doors £1,200 a metre; 2 IKEA PAX built in with MDF £1,050 a metre (from his Wandsworth quote: his work and the MDF; they buy the IKEA parts, said as roughly £400 a metre; the PAX frames named from pax-brief.ts fillWall, e.g. 'PAX 100 + 75 + 50'; not offered under 2,030 high, too narrow, or when Claude says PAX doesn't suit; a depth note under 600); 3 MDF shaker £1,600 a metre; 4 finished Egger board from Cworkshop in a colour matched to the room, nothing to paint (sheets worked out from the parts: doors, plinth, infill, fillers outside; sides, dividers, top, bottom, two shelves a unit, 18 mm backs inside; 72% of each 2800 x 2070 sheet used; plus Cworkshop's cutting £47.30 a sheet, edging £15 a sheet, hinges and rails £50 a unit, runners £25 a drawer, delivery £60, labour 0.5 days + 1 day a metre + 0.15 a drawer + 0.3 a metre for top cupboards at £650 a day). Drawers £80 each and top cupboards £250 a metre go on the flat and shaker prices. Marked 'my guess' on the prices screen (Raf to confirm): PAX parts £400/m, drawer £80, top cupboards £250/m, edging £15, hardware £50, delivery £60, the days, smallest job £330. His prices are kept in the store (row '_prices') and changed on Quotes, Your prices (each Cworkshop board can take his own price too). **Cworkshop's boards** (src/lib/cworkshop-boards.ts, BOARDS_CHECKED 2 Nov 2026): 30 Egger boards whose 18 mm price could be read on cworkshop.co.uk (whites, greys, beiges, sage, fjord, blues, concrete, light, warm and dark oaks, walnuts; Feelwood oaks are 18.5 mm; U250 and H3325 on offer to 30 Nov), each with its link and a swatch colour picked by eye. A board page without its script shows the 8 mm price first, so W980 SM Platinum White and eight others whose 18 mm price couldn't be read are left out (Premium White W1000 ST9 is the inside). Cworkshop publishes no edging, delivery or spray prices (their cutting list app prices a job live: app.cworkshop.co.uk/cutlist); the £47.30 a sheet is the difference between a board and the same board 'added to cutting list'. **The store**: Supabase cabinetos project, migrations 'jobkit_quotes' and 'jobkit_quotes_grants' (live since 2 Nov 2026): jobkit.quotes (id = the ServiceM8 job uuid, state working / ready / failed / none, data = what Claude read with the job's number, first name, area, photo uuids, model, tokens, edits = Raf's changes, edits_at), RLS on, no policies; public.jobkit_quotes(p_secret, p_action list | claim | save | edit, p) with JOBKIT_SYNC_SECRET (anon and service_role only): claim lets one reading run at a time (4 minutes), never again once ready unless Raf asks, nor within 30 minutes of a failure; a failed re-reading keeps the earlier reading; Raf's later change wins. **Route**: POST /api/job-kit/quote behind the PIN: list, make {job, force, note} (claims, answers 'working' at once and reads after the answer with Next's after(); maxDuration 120), edit {id, edits, at}, prices {prices, at}. **When it reads**: the Job Kit starts one by itself for each Quote job of the last 14 days with the quote not sent and nothing read, once it's 15 minutes old (photos from WhatsApp follow a new job), one at a time (toRead in quotes.tsx), never retrying a failure; the website brief starts one after it has answered the customer (/api/brief when there are no photos, /api/brief/done once the photos are in; both maxDuration 120), so a quote is often ready before Raf opens the job; Raf can Work it out, Try again, or Work it out again with a note for Claude ('the ceiling is 2,600'), keeping his prices and ticks. **Screens** (src/app/job-kit/quotes.tsx, in the Customers section, a price-tag icon 'quote'): Today 'Rough quote ready to check: <name>' (two at most, else 'N rough quotes ready to check'); the Customers box a 'Quotes' row ('2 ready to check · 1 being worked out · 1 not worked out'); the computer's sidebar Quotes (with the ready count), the list beside an open quote; a ServiceM8 Quote job has a 'Rough quote' box after Where it's up to (Open the quote, Work it out or Try again). Quotes: Ready to check, Being worked out, Couldn't work it out, Not worked out yet (Work it out), Sent (5, then Show more); Your prices; Refresh. A quote: the name, #number, area, Open the job, what it is and Claude's summary, 'Read 4 min ago from 3 photos', the photos (tap for full size), What Claude saw, their budget, Check on the visit, Sizes in mm (wide, high, deep boxes for each space, where each came from), drawers and top cupboards, then each option: the price + VAT and with VAT, 'Claude's pick' (with why), Your price box (and Back to the worked-out one), a tick for 'in the message' (an option not offered says why), what the customer reads, How it's worked out (every line, the total and the rounding); the board option also has the colour (Claude's picks with why, All 30 colours by family), Inside, sheets outside and inside, days, its Cworkshop page and their cutting list; Questions in the message (ticks); The message (written from the ticked options, grows to fit, can be changed by hand, 'Use the written-out words again'); Copy, WhatsApp <first> (their mobile, message typed in) and Email it (puts it in the job's Emails and texts box with the subject 'Rough prices for your <what>', to send through ServiceM8, which adds the sign-off); I've sent it (the same quoteSentAt mark as the Clients board, so the job moves to Quote sent and Follow up after 5 days, and sentAt on the quote; tap again if it wasn't). **The message**: 'Hi Sarah,' Claude's opening, 'Here are my rough prices for a space about 2,400 mm wide and 2,400 mm high (from your brief and photos; I'll measure properly on the visit):' (the bracket only when a size isn't from the brief), then '1. Made to measure in MDF with flat doors: £2,900 + VAT (£3,480). Floor to ceiling in 18 mm MDF with full backs, scribed to your walls and ready for your decorator to paint. Inside: ...', the other options numbered, 'A few questions to firm it up:' (or 'One question to firm it up: ...'), 'The next step is a free visit to measure up, then your written quote. I draw it in 3D for you to approve, and nothing is cut until you sign it off.', 'Thanks,' 'Raf'. No timescales or guarantees, and it never says Raf paints. Kept on the device: raf_jobkit_quotes (the list) and raf_jobkit_quote_pend (changes made with no signal, sent when it's back); the list is asked for on open, when the app comes back (at most once a minute) and every 6 s while one is being read. Kept as row '_rules' {text} beside '_prices' (Supabase migration 'jobkit_quotes_rules', applied 2 Nov 2026 before the merge, as the app already live ignores what's new: list leaves out every '_' row and returns `rules`; 'get' reads '_prices' or '_rules'; claim and save refuse '_' ids; edit gives '_rules' the state 'rules'); route action 'rules' {rules: {text}, at}; on the device in the same pending list as the prices. **Claude**: runQuote reads them (getRules in quote-maker.ts) and systemFor() adds them after the defaults, saying they win (what to assume, offer, recommend, ask and how the words read; e.g. pax_ok false for no PAX), that a paragraph for every message goes in the tool's new `extra` and a changed closing in `closing` (both optional; quoteMessage puts extra after the questions and closing in place of the usual 'The next step is a free visit...' (CLOSING)), that a fact he gives can be used but none made up, and never a price in words. Each reading keeps `rulesAt` (the version used, 0 = none). **Redo**: ready quotes not sent and read before the latest instructions (readBeforeRules) show 'Read before your latest instructions' on their card, and the card offers 'N ready quotes were worked out before these instructions' with Redo all N with them (or 'before you cleared your instructions'); they go one at a time (job-kit-app.tsx redo queue, raf_jobkit_quote_redo on the device, so a reload carries on; it waits for the store's own list after a reload, for his instructions to reach the store, and a minute after no signal), 'Redoing with your instructions: 2 to go'. The Cworkshop screen shows 30 decors at a time (Show more) and a quote's All N colours is a search with family chips (ColourFinder in quotes.tsx). **Services, at Cworkshop's own prices before Raf's discount** (`source` on each: website, basket, raf or guess; `published` true for website and basket): cutting £47.30 a sheet (their pages); from his basket of 6 Nov (3+ sheets of Egger H1732 ST9 Sand Birch): 1 mm ABS edging 22 mm £3.26 a metre, hinge cup holes £2.66 each, edging a panel under 65 mm wide £3.47 an edge (narrowEdge, new), tape to take away £1.50 a metre, sold in 5 m at least (tapeM, new; not used in quotes); delivery his figure, about £60, more for a heavy order (source 'raf'); 2 mm edging and Fast Flow still guesses. **Discount**: content/cworkshop.json `discount` {percent 10, on, from, checked} (his, 10% off the whole order; the basket showed 10% on edging and drilling and 5% on tape) -> PriceKey cwDiscount (PRICE_BASE from the file, kept 0 to 50, a box 'Your account discount' at the top of the Cworkshop screen; 0 = charge Cworkshop's full prices and keep the 10%). A quote's finished-board option has one line 'Your Cworkshop discount, 10% off the boards, cutting, edging and drilling' (discountOf in quote-sums.ts: on Cworkshop's prices for the sheets before his markup, the cutting, the edging and the drilling; not Fast Flow or delivery), money / money2 now write negatives as −£115.15; labour as times the materials counts the materials after it. **Edging**: 1 mm everywhere by default, one line 'Edging 1 mm, 54 m at £3.26' (edgingOf / edgingWords); a tick '2 mm edging on the doors and frame' (edits.thick) splits it into 2 mm and 1 mm as before. **Delivery tick** says 'About £60, your figure. 7 sheets is about 490 kg: a heavy order costs more' (SHEET_KG 70 in quote-sums.ts). Fast Flow is the only price still marked my guess on a quote (GUESSED). Example, 2.4 m wall, oak doors and frame, white inside: 2 sheets oak £209.00, 5 white £435.35, cutting 7 £331.10, edging 54 m £176.04, discount −£115.15, fittings £150, delivery £60, labour 3 days £1,950: £3,200 before VAT. **Weekly check** (scripts/CWORKSHOP.md): reads the 80 boards with the oldest `checked` and adds up to 40 new decors a week, one fetch at a time (helpers at most three at once), never touches `discount` or the basket and raf prices. scripts/quotes/check.mjs: 2595 checks (every board, the basket's totals to the penny after its discount, his 10% and delivery, the hand-worked wall with 1 mm edging and the discount, 2 mm when ticked, 0%). Tested on a production build (scratchpad cw/test2.cjs, 60 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1440: the services and where each comes from, the 10% box, 180 decors with Show more, search, families, a 19 mm board, a Kronospan decor, an offer, his 2 mm, decor and 12% saved and only those kept, 1 mm everywhere with 'your 12% off', the discount line as −£, the delivery weight line, the 2 mm tick with his price, hinge holes, no delivery, choices kept on the quote, Your prices' Cworkshop card, labour as twice the materials, no sideways scroll, no page errors). Waiting for Raf: the boards and cutting part of a basket (to see the 10% on the sheets and cutting at £47.30), and whether he uses Fast Flow.

Every button, handler and text kept; the IconTile import is no longer used in Home (still in icons.tsx). Install and PIN boxes and the 'On this phone' line unchanged.
