- **Job Kit section colours, colour key and Cancel in the Diary (18 Oct 2026, Raf: 'add to jobkit in diary in a visit option to cancel so I don't have to go to service m8 and do it . Also everything looks same can we distinguish the different sections with slightly different colors ... and maybe make a pop up legend to learn now and then the main sections colours , nothing too crazy but noticeable')**: src/app/job-kit/sections.tsx: five sections, each a colour set in job-kit.css (`.jk-sec-<id>` sets --sec / --sec-soft; hex kept there on purpose, the Job Kit only): To do blue (the accent), Customers teal #17736e, Designs in 3D violet #6a45b8, Website amber #a35a00, Photos and posts rose #b0345a. `secOfScreen(name)` says which section a screen is in (diary, current, sm8, enquiry, customer, inbox, email = Customers; designs, design, new-design, library = Designs; stats, stats-page = Website; posts, post, job, new = Photos and posts; Today and Settings none). The screen's colour shows as a 3 px line under the top bar (`.jk-secline`), the back label and the screen title's icon (`.jk-sec-ink`, Diary, Current jobs, Inbox, Designs, Library, Posts, Stats); on Today each box has a 4 px edge, a washed label bar and its icon, label and arrows in its colour (HomeBand `sec`; To do keeps its blue bar), and each To do line's icon takes the colour of where it goes (`secOfLine`). Colour key: five dots in the top bar (KeyDots, 'What the colours mean') open a sheet 'Where things are' (ColourKey: each section's name and what's in it, 'Got it', Escape or the backdrop closes); it opens by itself on the first open and twice more a week apart, then only from the dots (localStorage raf_jobkit_legend, the times it showed). Diary (current.tsx Diary): every booking not yet over has Cancel at the right of its bottom row (with Directions / On my way / Call on today's): tap, then 'Tap again to cancel it' within 5 s ('... all 3 days' for a booking over several days), then cancel {uuid} as before (ServiceM8 archives it). A dashed note stays where it was: 'Cancelled in ServiceM8: Visit, 10:00 to 11:00, Tom Baker' with Tell Tom on WhatsApp (cancelText: 'sorry, I need to cancel my visit tomorrow at 10:00. When else would suit you?' / for work 'sorry, I can't make it ... I'll be in touch to rearrange.'), Undo (books the same start, end and staff again: a new booking in ServiceM8) and Hide; the note lives only while the Diary is open. ServiceM8's error shows under the card. Also: the Diary's lists are `grid-cols-1`, so a long description no longer widens the cards past the screen (it did at 390 px). Tested as an iPhone 13 and at 320 against the pretend ServiceM8 (scratchpad jk/colours-test.js, 27 checks: the key's three showings, dots, backdrop, band and line colours on Diary / Designs / Stats / Posts / Inbox, cancel two taps and the 5 s disarm, the DELETE, the WhatsApp texts and numbers, Undo's POST with the same times, no Cancel on one already over, no sideways scroll, no page errors).
- **Coloured backgrounds, delete emails, back without a reload (18 Oct 2026, Raf: 'I'd like the background to be same color as section and also would like to the option to remove the email from list here and on server same time . Like in todo section there are now just icons with different colors and I'd like to have back grounds as well . Maybe this way can you make a switch so I can turn on and off the background in whole app if I get used to')**: **Backgrounds** (on by default): the root gets `jk-tint`; a screen in a section is washed in its colour (11% of --sec into paper, the sticky top bar too; cards and boxes stay white on it), each Today box 7% into mount, and each To do line is its own strip in the colour of where it goes (12%, `ul.jk-todo`, the li carries secClass(secOfLine(l.go)); booking times and arrows in that colour too). Switch 'Coloured backgrounds' (TintSwitch, role switch, a square track) in the colour key (one line) and in Settings under Colours (with 'Each screen and To do line in its colour.' / 'Off: colour on the lines and icons only.'); useTint() reads localStorage raf_jobkit_tint ('0' = off) through useSyncExternalStore, so both switches and other tabs follow. **Delete an email** (Inbox): src/lib/outlook.ts binMail(id) = Graph POST /messages/<id>/move {destinationId 'deleteditems'} (what Outlook's own Delete does, so it can be got back from Deleted Items; Mail.ReadWrite covers it), restoreMail(id) moves it back to 'inbox'; both answer the new id Microsoft gives a moved email. Route actions delete {id} / restore {id}. App: a bin at the right of every Inbox row (tap, 'Delete?' in an ink box within 5 s, then gone) and 'Delete' on the email screen (tap, 'Tap again to delete it'; then back to where it was opened from); removeMail in job-kit-app.tsx moves it on the server first, then drops it from the list here (dropHere: unread count too; an email Outlook no longer has is just dropped), and an ink bar at the bottom says 'Deleted the email from <name>. It's in Deleted Items in Outlook.' with Undo (restore, putBackHere with its new id, then the list is fetched again) and × for 12 s; the To do email line follows the list. ServiceM8's 'Message from' lines are the job's record in ServiceM8 and aren't deleted (they clear once the job's Emails and texts is opened). **Back without a reload**: the Job Kit's history entries were {jk: screen} only, because the app's first replaceState ran before Next's router had copied its own fields in, so Next took every back (button or swipe) as a page it didn't make and reloaded the whole page (losing anything only in memory, e.g. the Undo bar). pushState / replaceState now keep ...history.state, so back is instant and in place. Tested as an iPhone 13 and at 320 against a pretend Microsoft sign-in + Graph with made-up emails (scratchpad bin/graph-mock.mjs, MOVEFAIL=1; bin/start.sh starts it with the pretend ServiceM8; bin/ui.js, 20 checks: on by default, To do strips and box colours, the Inbox washed teal with its top bar, bin two taps and the 5 s disarm, the move to deleteditems, Undo's move back, delete from the email screen and back to the Inbox, the To do line gone, switch off in the key and still off after reload, on again in Settings, no sideways scroll at 320 with the bin armed and the bar showing, no page errors); jk/colours-test.js still passes (27). Not tried on Raf's real mailbox yet: the first delete is the test.
- **Job Kit opens at the top (18 Oct 2026, Raf: 'When I open jobkit through shortcut on iPhone first thing it does goes to search bar in customers I'd like it to be at the top of the app')**: nothing in the code focused or scrolled to the Find a customer box (no autoFocus; not reproducible in Chromium), so the likely causes on an iPhone were dealt with in job-kit-app.tsx's mount effect: `history.scrollRestoration = 'manual'` and a scroll to the top on open and once the screens are drawn (`ready`), so iOS can't put back an old scroll position when it reloads the home-screen app; when the app goes into the background a focused search box (input type search) is let go (blur), so coming back doesn't jump down to it with the keyboard up (what was typed stays); back on Today after more than 30 s away (visibilitychange, screenNow ref) or when iOS puts the page back from its cache (pageshow persisted) it goes to the top; other screens keep their place (mid-task: an email, a job). Tested (scratchpad bin/top.js as an iPhone 13 against the pretend services: manual restoration, opens at the top, search let go on hide, back after 40 s at the top with no keyboard and the typed text kept, back within 30 s keeps the place, pageshow from the cache, the Diary opens at the top and keeps its place after a long break, no page errors); bin/ui.js still passes (20). The real test is Raf's iPhone.
- **Job timeline: a Gantt chart in the Job Kit (19 Oct 2026, Raf: 'add Gant chart to jobkit as a separate section where we decide on job start and finish time and progress in a smart compact way')**: a sixth section, olive green (`.jk-sec-timeline` #587a14 in job-kit.css; SECTIONS, secOfScreen 'timeline', secOfLine, the colour key's lines shortened so six fit an iPhone 13; new 'gantt' icon). **Where the dates live**: a job's start and finish are its work bookings in ServiceM8 (the Diary's bookings), so they show in ServiceM8 and the Diary too; bookings scheduled before the job went to Work Order are the quote visit and don't count, and a completed job's count up to its completion. How far along a job is (0 / 25 / 50 / 75 / Done) is kept on the phone in the marks (raf_jobkit_sm8_marks `progress`, `progressAt`): ServiceM8 has no place for it. Work days are Monday to Saturday (Sundays shaded, never counted). The sums are in src/lib/job-timeline.ts (types only, so Today, the job screen and the chart read the same rows): rowOf / timelineRows (rows: Work Order jobs with work booked, plus completed ones that ended in the last two weeks or so, by start; undated: Work Order jobs with nothing booked), state upcoming / on / late (finish passed, not done) / finished / completed, expected % by the booked days gone (today counts half), behind when 15+ under it and ahead when 25+ over (only once a booked day has gone by), rowWords ('Day 2 of 4 · 50%, on track', 'Starts tomorrow · 5 days', 'Finish was Wed 14 Oct'), rangeOf (Monday of last week, or the earliest job, to 8 weeks ahead, whole weeks), planChanges (nothing booked: one booking 8:00 on the first day to 16:00 on the last; one booking: moved, its times of day kept; several: the first starts on the new start, the last ends on the new finish, the ones between stay, and a start past the first booked day or a finish before the last is turned down rather than cancelling days; Move the whole job shifts every booking). **Screen** (src/app/job-kit/job-timeline.tsx, screen 'timeline' with optional `job` to open its sheet; back says Timeline): kicker 'Gantt chart · jobs on Work Order', ServiceM8's time and Refresh, a summary ('2 on now · 1 past its finish · 1 needs dates'), Days / Weeks (30 or 11 px a day, kept in raf_jobkit_timeline_zoom) and Today; the chart runs edge to edge on a phone and scrolls sideways (opens two days before today, or earlier to show a job that's been going, keeping today on screen): months and days (today in blue) or weeks across the top, week lines, Saturdays and Sundays shaded, a blue today line; each job a row with its name and words sticking to the left and a bar from its first booked day to its last, striped where a day in between isn't booked, filled dark as far as it's got with the % after it, dashed when behind, a dashed overrun up to today when past its finish, grey when completed. Under it 'Going ahead, no dates' with Set dates. Tapping a job opens a sheet: the customer, the job, its words, what's booked, Start and Finish (‹ date ›), work days, Move the whole job ‹ week / ‹ day / day › / week ›, Save to ServiceM8 (or Book it in ServiceM8; Put back; each booking moved or booked through the same book / move actions as the Diary, ServiceM8 first), How far along (the step nearest the expected % dotted; at Done, 'Job finished: move to Completed', two taps), Open the job, Close (Escape and the backdrop close too); a completed job's dates can't be changed there. **Today**: a 'Job timeline' box after Customers ('4 going ahead'): this week and next as a 14-day strip (today in blue), up to four jobs (past their finish first, then on, then later ones as 'Starts Mon 3 Nov'), the summary and Open; To do lines (only when ServiceM8 sent the bookings): '<name>: past the finish date' (or 'N jobs past their finish date'), '<name>: done, move it to Completed' (opens the job), '<name> is behind: 25% done, about 50% by now', 'N jobs going ahead need dates', and 'N jobs on Work Order' now opens the timeline. **Job screen**: a Work Order job's Where it's up to has a line with its dates and words (or 'Set its start and finish'), opening the timeline on it. **Server**: currentJobs() asks ServiceM8 for bookings from 60 days back (BOOKINGS_BACK; it was yesterday on), so a job's days already gone show; the Diary, Today and a job's In the diary still start at today; jobs outside the list are only fetched for bookings from yesterday on. Tested against the pretend ServiceM8 with GANTT=1 (scratchpad jk/sm8mock.mjs: a job on now over a Sunday with its quote visit before Work Order, one with no dates, one past its finish, a completed one, a booking 80 days back that doesn't come): tl/sums.mts (29 checks on the sums) and tl/ui.js as an iPhone 13 and at 320 (Today box and To do lines, five box colours, rows and words, opened near today, progress kept, finish a day later and the whole job a week later as ServiceM8 moves, several bookings refused, Set dates books 8:00 to 16:00, Completed, back says Timeline, Weeks kept, the job screen line, the colour key fits, the name sticks while the days scroll, no sideways scroll, no page errors); bin/ui.js and jk/colours-test.js updated for five boxes and six key lines and still pass. Not tried on Raf's real ServiceM8 yet: the 60-day lookback and moving a multi-day booking are the first real test.
- **Clients board (22 Oct 2026, Raf: 'in jobkit in section timeline i'd like to have a separate summary for current clients showing name, job required, status - new enquiry, quote, quote send, in progress (accepted quote no payment yet) work order, deposit paid, remaining amount, completed (where we can close the client off the board)')**: a second screen in the Job timeline section (olive), beside the Gantt chart: tabs 'Gantt chart' / 'Clients board' at the top of both (TimelineTabs in src/app/job-kit/client-board.tsx), a 'Clients board' row under the Job timeline box on Today ('2 new · 3 quoting · 2 sent · 1 accepted · 1 deposit paid · 2 completed · £1,350 to collect'), and 'Clients board' in the computer's sidebar under Gantt chart (new 'board' icon: three columns of cards). Screen 'board' (ROOMY; back label 'Board'; a job opened from it is sm8 with from 'board', shown beside the board on a computer). **The six steps** (src/lib/client-board.ts, types only, so it runs in tests): New enquiry = Quote in ServiceM8 with no visit booked or marked done and no price; Quote = a visit booked or done (Diary's Visit done counts) or a price in ServiceM8, quote not sent; Quote sent = ServiceM8's quote_sent or the Job Kit's 'I've sent the quote' (WhatsApp) mark; Accepted = Work Order with nothing paid; Deposit paid = Work Order with any payment (or ServiceM8 says paid); Completed = Completed (ServiceM8 sends the last 30 days); Unsuccessful ones aren't on it. Each card: name, #number, the job (whatOf the description), area, a six-part step strip, where it's up to ('Enquiry 3 days ago', 'Visit tomorrow, 10:00', 'Visited Sat 17 Oct, quote to send', 'Quote sent 7 days ago', 'Accepted Mon 19 Oct · Starts Tue 27 Oct · 3 days' / 'Day 2 of 3' from the Gantt's own sums), the money ('Quote £950 written, not sent', 'Quote £4,800', '£5,200 · nothing paid yet', 'Paid £3,000 · £9,000 left', '£1,350 to collect of £3,000', '£1,800, paid in full') and a flag for what waits on Raf (Waiting N days, Quote to send after a visit 3+ days ago, Follow up after 5 days, Past its finish date, Behind, £X to collect). Totals on top: Quotes out (quotes sent), To come (left on jobs going ahead), To collect (left on finished jobs). Phone: the steps one under another with chips All / each step (counts); computer: six columns in a row where there's room (container 1060 px+, e.g. 1440), else two rows of three (1024, 1280); in the list pane beside a job it's the phone layout. **Close**: a completed card has 'Close it off the board' (tap, then 'Tap again to close' or 'Tap again: close with £1,350 unpaid' within 5 s), with a note and Undo; closed ones fold into 'Closed off the board (N)' with Put back. It's the same `hidden` mark as Current jobs' 'Done with it: hide from the list' (so they leave Current jobs too, and it syncs to the computer); nothing changes in ServiceM8, and a closed job reopened there shows again. **I've sent the quote on the card** (22 Oct 2026, Raf: 'how can i change quote to quote send'): New enquiry and Quote cards have 'I’ve sent the quote' in their footer (one tap, the same quoteSentAt mark as the job screen's button, so it syncs and ServiceM8 isn't changed); the card moves to Quote sent at once with a note 'Moved to Quote sent: <name>' and Undo (onQuoteSent in client-board.tsx; scratchpad board/sent.mjs, 27 checks at 390 and 320). Days are counted by calendar day now on the board and in Current jobs alike ('sent on the 15th is 7 days ago on the 22nd'; Follow up from 5 calendar days; before, Current jobs counted whole 24 hours). Tested (scratchpad board/sums.mts, 41 checks with tsx; board/sm8mock.mjs, a pretend ServiceM8 with a made-up client at every step dated around today, board/start.sh; board/ui.mjs as an iPhone 13, at 320, 1024 and 1440: Today row, totals, chips, every card's words, money and flags, a step on its own, open a job and back, 'I've sent the quote' moves Isla to Quote sent, close, Undo, the 5 s second tap, closed fold, off Current jobs, Put back, the two tabs, the colour key still fits, columns, a job beside the board with the same day count, no sideways scroll, no page errors). Not tried on Raf's real ServiceM8 yet: his own jobs are the first real test.
- **Payments from the Job Kit (22 Oct 2026, Raf: 'is it possible to add option for payments in client card similar like in service m8 deposit everything with various methods and date to choose')**: until now payments were only read. Now a payment can be added (src/app/job-kit/payments.tsx): on the Clients board each card for a quote sent ('Add a deposit'), an accepted job, a deposit-paid job with money left and a completed job still owing ('Add a payment') opens a sheet over the board (PaySheet: the client, the job, '£12,000 · paid £3,000 · £9,000 left', then the form); a job's Money box (current.tsx MoneyBox, id money) has 'Add a payment' too, shown even with no total yet (not on Unsuccessful). The form (PaymentForm): Deposit / Payment (Deposit to start with when nothing is paid and the job isn't Completed), Amount in pounds (typed '1,350' or '£1350.50' is read; quick chips 'All of it £4,800' / 'What's left £9,000' and 'Half £2,400' when nothing is paid; a note when it's more than what's left), When (Today, Yesterday, or a date, never after today), How they paid (Bank transfer, Cash, Card, Cheque, in the spelling Raf's ServiceM8 payments already use, then up to three other methods he has used, most used first, and Other to type one; the last one used is picked next time, localStorage raf_jobkit_pay_method), Note (optional, e.g. a reference). 'Save in ServiceM8', then 'Tap again: £2,400 deposit, bank transfer, today' within 5 s; nothing is sent on the first tap. Then 'Saved in ServiceM8: ...' (PaidNote); on a job still on Quote it adds 'A deposit usually means they've said yes' with 'Move it to Work Order too' (two taps, the usual status change). A failed save says why and keeps what was typed. Payments are only added: one typed wrong is changed or removed in ServiceM8 (the form says so). Server: /api/job-kit/sm8 action `pay` {job, uuid, amount, date 'YYYY-MM-DD', method, deposit, note} (bad-amount, bad-date after today or before 2015, missing-method) -> addPayment() in src/lib/servicem8.ts: POST jobpayment.json {uuid made on the phone, job_uuid, amount '2400.00', timestamp (today: the time now; an earlier day: 12:00:00), method, is_deposit '1'|'0', note, actioned_by_uuid = the first active staff member}; if ServiceM8 turns a POST down but has that uuid, it went in the first time, so a second try never adds it twice. The app (job-kit-app.tsx pay) puts the payment on the job at once (board, Money box, Today's money lines) and fetches the list again 1.5 s later for ServiceM8's own sums. Payment now carries `note` and `uuid` (shape()). The board: a deposit on a job still on Quote counts as Deposit paid ('Deposit paid · still a Quote in ServiceM8', flag 'Move to Work Order'; in To come, not Quotes out). Fields from ServiceM8's Create Job Payment reference (developer.servicem8.com) and is_deposit as listed by integrations; not tried on Raf's real account yet: the first real payment is the test (check it in ServiceM8, and that a deposit shows as a deposit there). Tested (scratchpad board/pay.mjs, 48 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1440 against the pretend ServiceM8: which cards offer it, the sheet, defaults, the methods in ServiceM8's spelling, no amount, Half, the two taps, nothing sent on the first, the record in ServiceM8 (amount, deposit, method, today's time, staff), Work Order after it, the card moved, Amelia's rest by cash yesterday with a note at 12:00, paid in full with no more button, a 502 keeping the amount then one PayPal payment, To collect £0, the job's Money box with the cursor in the amount, PayPal offered and picked, a card deposit 3 days ago shown in the box, the same payment sent twice kept once, negative, tomorrow, no method, a made-up uuid, wrong PIN, the sheet fits at 320 and 1440, Escape, no sideways scroll, no page errors); board/sums.mts 43 checks and board/ui.mjs still pass.
- **What each job made: receipts, costs and days on site (11 Nov 2026, Raf: 'Yes' to a mockup on the 'Job Kit, what each job made' design canvas (artifact TkRhggndAk2psykx96Jdeb), then 'Go' without answering the two questions, so: days come from the work bookings in the Diary, and helpers and parking count as job costs; previewed on the `job-money` branch, live since 11 Nov 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: what's left of a job's price after its costs, a day on site, against Raf's day rate (Quotes, Your prices, dayRate £650). **Sums** (src/lib/job-costs.ts, type imports only): price before VAT = ServiceM8's total_invoice_amount / 1.2 (assumed to include VAT), else what's been paid / 1.2; costs = this job's share of each cost before VAT; days = siteDays(workBookingsOf(job, bookings)): bookings from the day it went to Work Order (earlier ones are the quote visit) to the day it was completed, hours per day (one-day bookings by their times; several-day ones 8 to 4, from the start / to the end on the first and last day), 5 hours or more a day, less half a day, Sundays left out unless that's all; Raf's own days win (Change on the job). (price − costs) ÷ days, '£46 a day over your £650 rate' / 'under' / 'Right on your rate' (vsWords). A finished job with no costs and not ticked 'That's all the costs on it' shows ≈. kindOfWork() from the description (IKEA PAX, wardrobes, kitchens, alcoves, panelling, doors, flooring, seats, desks, cupboards, outdoor, stud walls, other). **Kept in step** (src/app/job-kit/costs-store.ts, localStorage raf_jobkit_costs and _pend, rev raf_jobkit_costs_rev): Supabase cabinetos project, migrations 'jobkit_costs' and 'jobkit_costs_grants' (applied 11 Nov 2026): jobkit.costs (id, data jsonb, at, device, rev from jobkit.costs_rev), RLS on, no policies; public.jobkit_costs(p_secret, p_device, p_since, p_docs) with JOBKIT_SYNC_SECRET (anon and service_role only): the later `at` of a doc wins, deletions kept 120 days, a round brings back only what changed after the device's rev (400 at a time, `more`). Docs: k<...> a cost {date, shop, what (materials, hardware, parking, helper, tools, other), gross, vat, net, lines, note, where job | van | company, jobs [{job, amount before VAT}] (one, or a split), from photo | pdf | email | typed, mail, mailAtt, photo {job, uuid}, photoWaiting, read}; j-<job> Raf's days and allCosts; s-<job> a snapshot of each Completed job (written by the phone when it changes, so a job stays counted after ServiceM8's 30 days; keepSnaps in job-money.tsx); m-<hash> an email that isn't a receipt. Route POST /api/job-kit/costs (pinGuard, so it works on previews). **Receipts** (src/lib/receipt-reader.ts, POST /api/job-kit/receipt, pinGuard): Claude reads a photo (2400 px JPEG from the phone), a PDF, or a supplier's email in info@ (its PDF or photos of 30 KB+, else its words; the email isn't marked read) with one forced tool receipt_read (shop, day, total paid, VAT as printed, lines, kind, sure); cleanReceipt() keeps the sums right (VAT never over the total, no future days); never a price decided by Claude, never the job: Raf picks. RECEIPT_MODEL, else ANTHROPIC_MODEL, else claude-sonnet-5-5 (claude-sonnet-5 if missing), the Job Kit's ANTHROPIC_API_KEY, noted as 'receipts' in Claude credit. Saving puts the photo or the email's PDF on the first job in ServiceM8 as 'Receipt - <shop>, <day>' (uuid stableUuid(job:receipt:<cost id>), so never twice; then touchJob); with no signal the photo waits on the phone (IndexedDB raf-job-kit-receipts) and goes when it's back; van stock and company costs (tools) go on no job ('Keep the paper receipt'). **Screens** (src/app/job-kit/job-money.tsx): screen 'receipt' {id?, job?, mail?, typed?} (Customers, teal): Take a photo of it / Choose a photo or PDF / No receipt: type it in; Read by Claude, shop, Paid, Day, VAT on it (a 20% button), Before VAT / VAT / Paid, the lines as printed, What it was; What is it? chips (Tools makes it a company cost); Which job? (Work Order jobs and ones finished in the last 21 days, the one booked today first and tagged, Split it between jobs with even shares that must add up, Van stock, not one job); the button says where ('Put £41.60 on Tom's job'); then a done card with Undo, Snap another, Open the job. An email: 'Not a receipt'. A cost tapped on a job opens it to change or take off (two taps). **On a job** (MadeBox via CurrentView's new `made` prop, after Money; Work Order 'What it's making', Completed 'What it made'; job menu chip 'Made', new icons 'made' and 'receipt'): the day figure, a costs-and-yours bar, Price / Costs / Days on site, the sum written out, the costs (picture or initial, shop, what, day, how it came, its share), Costs before VAT, VAT on the receipts to claim back, Snap a receipt, Add a cost by hand (Parking to start with, no VAT), 'That's all the costs on it' (Completed), Days on site with the days as chips (gone ones blue), Change (± half days, Use the Diary's), check-in hours when the phone has the job's diary. **What jobs made** (screen 'made', the third tab of the Job timeline beside Gantt chart and Clients board, olive; ROOMY; sidebar 'What jobs made'): 30 days / 90 days / This year (raf_jobkit_made_period), the hero (a day after costs, against the rate, jobs, costs, days, ≈ and jobs not counted), By kind of work (bars in blue with the rate as a dashed line), What it tells you (insights(): the kind that pays best, a job well under the rate with what its days and costs come to at the rate, helpers, jobs with no costs, van stock and company costs in the days), Every job (8 then Show all; a row opens the job, or the customer when ServiceM8 no longer sends it). **Today**: 'Screwfix email in info@: which job is it for?' or 'N receipts in info@ to put on jobs' (receiptLike(): a supplier's address or subject and order, receipt or invoice words, not offers; last 14 days; not on a job or skipped), '<name>'s job made about £667 a day: any receipts still to add?' for jobs completed in the last 7 days until 'That's all the costs' (two at most), and a 'What jobs made' row in the Job timeline box ('≈ £507 a day on 3 jobs in 90 days, your rate £650'). The Inbox's email screen has a box 'Looks like a receipt from Screwfix' (Put it on a job, Not a receipt), or 'On Emma Clarke's job: £96.18 before VAT'. Not built yet (stage 2, once there's data): 'Against your quote' (planned days and materials from Quotes beside what it took) and suggestions for Your prices from the jobs. Tested on a production build against stand-ins for ServiceM8, Microsoft, Claude and the real jobkit_costs on a throwaway Postgres 16 (scratchpad jm/: mocks.mjs, start.sh, sql-test.sh, sums.mts 55 checks; test.cjs 103 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1440: the To do lines and Today row, Tom's job booked today, a photo read and put on his job with the JPEG on the ServiceM8 job and the job saved again, the costs and VAT on the job and every amount on screen, parking typed, days changed, the Screwfix email read without marking it read and its PDF on Emma's job, Not a receipt, tools as a company cost, a split that must add up, Emma's figure and the sum, That's all the costs clearing To do, What jobs made (tabs, three jobs, kinds, rate line, insights, ≈, 30 days, a row to the job and back), three snapshots and five costs in the store with no pictures, Claude credit noted as receipts, the computer getting it all, a change and a take-off on the computer reaching the phone, no signal then the photo going once back online, out of credit in plain words, 320 with three tabs fitting, no sideways scroll, no page errors). Not tried with Raf's real receipts, ServiceM8 and info@ yet: the first real receipt is the test. **Snap a receipt from Today (12 Nov 2026, Raf, asking where Snap a receipt was (it was only on a job's Made box), then 'Yes' to it on Today and in the sidebar; previewed on the `snap-receipt` branch, live since 12 Nov 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: SnapButton in job-money.tsx: one tap opens the phone's camera straight away (a hidden file input with capture environment, image/*; on a computer no capture and PDFs too), the photo is handed to the receipt screen (holdSnap / takeSnap, kept in memory only, so a reload lands on the screen's first step) and Claude reads it at once, with no Take a photo step. On Today it's a big row at the top above To do ('Snap a receipt / For Tom Baker’s job, booked today', or 'N jobs booked today: pick one after', or 'Claude reads it, then you pick the job': snapLine from jobChoices; a teal edge like the boxes; up to 560 px wide on a computer) with 'A PDF, a photo from the phone, or no receipt' under it (the receipt screen); the job booked today is picked for it. A job's Made box uses the same button (the camera straight away, that job picked) with 'A PDF or a photo from the phone' under it. The computer's sidebar has 'Snap a receipt' under Customers after Current jobs (SideId 'receipt'). Screen 'receipt' takes `snap`. Tested on a production build (scratchpad jm/snap.cjs, 33 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1440: the row on Today above To do, its line naming Tom's job, 66 px, capture on the phone, one tap to the camera, straight to reading with no second step, Tom picked, saved on his job with the photo in ServiceM8, Done back to Today, the other ways, a reload not stuck reading, the job's button opening the camera with Sarah picked, 320 fitting, the sidebar item and its highlight, a PDF on the computer, no sideways scroll, no page errors; jm/test.cjs 103 still pass).
- **Notes and the Job diary on a job (23 Oct 2026, Raf: 'in jobkit in job card ... notes card where i can add notes, saved automatically in servicem8, if i wanted to see a diary ... minimalised section when i press and read whole diary', then 'go'; live since 23 Oct 2026, Raf: 'merge')**: two sections on every Current jobs job screen, straight under Where it's up to (src/app/job-kit/job-notes.tsx JobNotes, server side src/lib/servicem8-diary.ts, route /api/job-kit/sm8 actions `notes`, `note-save`, `diary`, `file`). **Notes**: a box to type or dictate (keyboard microphone); 2 s after the last change the note is saved in the job's diary in ServiceM8 as ServiceM8's own note (note.json, related_object job). The phone makes the note's uuid, so the first save creates it with that uuid (a retry's 409 becomes an update) and every later save updates the same record (POST note/<uuid>.json): one diary entry, not one per pause. Status line: 'Saves when you stop typing', 'Saving in ServiceM8…', '✓ Saved in ServiceM8 09:31', 'No signal: kept on this phone. It goes to ServiceM8 when you're back online.', or ServiceM8's error plus 'Kept on this phone.' with Try again. + New note finishes the note (it still goes if waiting) and empties the box; a saved note left alone 30 minutes is finished by itself. Earlier notes under the box (ServiceM8's notes newest first, with this phone's newer or waiting copies; 3 then Show all N; More for long ones; Edit puts any note back in the box, 'Changing the note from Wed 21 Oct, 9:15', Done). Notes waiting on the phone (localStorage raf_jobkit_notes, per job) go when the box saves, when the job screen closes, when the app comes back online or on screen and when it opens (flushNotes in job-kit-app.tsx); the save uses fetch keepalive, so a note typed just before the app is closed still reaches the server, and one whose answer never came back is sent again as an update (never twice). The copies of emails and texts kept as notes ('Email sent from the Job Kit') are left out of notes. **Job diary from ServiceM8**: folded to one row ('22 entries · last: photo yesterday 16:40', or what's in it before the first load); tapped, it loads (once a phone has it, again after 2 minutes, or Refresh) the job's notes, attachments (attachment.json on the job; photos as a grid of thumbnails, tap for the full photo with Newer / Older and Escape, files like PDFs with Open / Save to the phone), emails and texts (jobMail), and the job activities: bookings (activity_was_scheduled; ones still to come under 'Coming up' at the top, soonest first) and check-ins (activity_was_recorded: 'On site, Checked in 8:12, out 15:40 (7 h 28 min)'); plus, from the job the phone already has, the payments and the job's dates (Enquiry came in, Quote sent, Moved to Work Order, Completed, Marked unsuccessful). Newest first, by day (Today, Yesterday, Wed 21 Oct), photos of one day together, 30 then Show 30 more; filters All / Notes / Photos and files / Messages / Bookings / Money with counts; its notes are the freshest (the Notes card's, waiting ones marked). Kept on the phone for offline (localStorage raf_jobkit_diary, last 10 jobs; thumbnails in the browser's Cache Storage `raf-jobkit-thumbs-v1`, up to 400). Says 'ServiceM8 keeps no history of status changes; the job's dates show when it moved on.' and, when one part couldn't be read, which (missing). Files: GET /attachment/<uuid>.file (redirect followed); thumb = 400 px JPEG made with sharp, full = photos over 2.5 MB or HEIC made 2000 px, other files up to 4 MB (Vercel's answer limit). ServiceM8 doesn't publish every field of notes, attachments and activities, so dates are read from create_date / timestamp / edit_date and names from attachment_name / file_type: check them on the first real job (if a date or a photo is missing there, that's the place to look). Tested (scratchpad notes/test.js with notes/sm8mock.mjs, 65 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1440: Notes under Where it's up to, earlier notes without the email copy or archived ones, created then updated as one note, still in the box after a reload, New note, offline kept and sent by itself back online, ServiceM8 error with Try again, Show all, Edit updates ServiceM8's own note and adds none, sent after the app's Back and after a reload straight after typing (once), the folded row, the whole diary with Coming up, notes, photos, PDF, emails, bookings, check-in, payment and the job's dates, 400 px thumbnails, the row's count and last entry, every filter, the photo viewer and PDF, offline from the phone with its thumbnails, no sideways scroll, no page errors).
- **Customers' photos in the diary, and a menu on each job (3 Nov 2026, Raf: 'when getting info from service m8 diary i will be able to see the pictures sent by customers right now says just img something ... can we make like a mini menu for the job sections so its easy to navigate and also distinguish the sections better'; previewed on the `diary-photos-and-job-menu` branch, live since 3 Nov 2026, Raf: 'merge')**: **Photos**: iPhone photos arrive in ServiceM8 as HEIC, which sharp's prebuilt library can't read (no HEVC), so every thumbnail failed and the tile showed its name. src/lib/photo-jpeg.ts jpegOf(bytes, edge, quality): sharp first (turned by EXIF), and for a HEIF file sharp can't read, heic-decode 2.1.0 (libheif in WebAssembly, libheif-js; it applies the camera's rotation) to raw RGBA, then sharp makes the JPEG; sniff() by first bytes (JPEG, PNG, GIF, WebP, TIFF, AVIF, HEIC, PDF). Both packages are in next.config.ts serverExternalPackages (loaded from node_modules, about 2 MB, traced into the routes; checked in the .nft.json of /api/job-kit/sm8, inbox and quote). Used by attachmentFile() in src/lib/servicem8-diary.ts (thumb 400 px q70; full 2000 px q80 for photos over 2.5 MB and every HEIC or TIFF; at most two photos made at a time per server, twoAtATime), quote-maker.ts photoFor (Quotes now read iPhone photos too) and outlook.ts attachment() (the inbox). fileOf() counts as a photo a file_type like '.jpg', 'jpg' or 'image/jpeg', a name ending in a photo type (heic, avif, tiff too), photo_width, or a name with no ending and no type ('IMG_4824'); a tile that turns out not to be a photo shows a file icon, the name and Open, and the viewer shows an image only when the file it got is one. **Photos named in emails**: photoMarks() in src/lib/mail-text.ts turns Gmail's '[image: IMG_4821.jpeg]', Outlook's '[cid:image001.jpg@...]', Apple's '<IMG_4821.jpeg>' and an HTML email's own cid images (htmlToText keeps them as marks; web images like logos and pixels are dropped) into '[photo: name]'; photoKey() matches a name whatever its ending (an iPhone sends IMG_4821.HEIC as IMG_4821.jpeg). In the Job diary an email shows the attachments it names under it (data-email-photos) and the lines that only named them go; the diary shows emails with no blank lines between paragraphs; Emails and texts shows '[photo: IMG_4821.jpeg]'. **The menu** (src/app/job-kit/job-menu.tsx JobMenu, under the job's contact buttons): a row of chips that sticks under the top bar (its height measured, so an iPhone's notch is allowed for), one per box on the job screen in screen order, found by data-jsec on each box (status, quote, whatsapp, notes, diary, goodnotes, property, bookings, emails, money, more; labels and icons in JOB_SECS), with a word from data-jsec-note (Notes 2, Diary 16 once loaded, Goodnotes count, Bookings 'tmrw 10:00' / 'today 10:00' / 'on now' for work already going, Messages count, Money '£9,000 left' or the quote, Quote 'ready' / 'sent') and data-jsec-alert='1' for what wants Raf (a quote ready, work today or on now, their message the last one, money owed on a job going ahead or done), shown pale blue; the box being read is lit up (the last one whose top has gone under the menu, More at the page's end) and slid into view; once stuck a first chip '↑ Amelia' goes back to the top. A tap jumps there, 10 px below the menu, opening a folded box on the way (a button with data-jsec-fold: the Job diary's and Property's). Every box's scroll-margin allows for the menu (job-kit.css .jk-job [data-jsec], --jk-stick), so the screen's own buttons (Email or text, Ask for a Google review) land below it too. Phone: one row that slides, the ends faded with ‹ › to slide and a mouse wheel sliding it sideways; computer (.jk-desk): the chips wrap onto two rows so all show. Each box's title has the same icon on a small square (SecIcon, .jk-secicon); new icons in icons.tsx: steps (Where it's up to), history (Job diary), tablet (Goodnotes), house (Property), more (From here); Bookings uses diary, Messages mail, Money money, Notes note. The Bookings box got id bookings, Where it's up to id status, From here id more. Tested on a production build (scratchpad jobmenu/test.cjs, 129 checks against jobmenu/sm8mock.mjs with made-up photos in jobmenu/files (two HEIC from pillow-heif, one turned by the camera; a JPEG typed image/jpeg; one with no ending; an 8.8 MB JPEG; a PNG; a PDF and a PDF with no ending) and an email naming three of them, as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1440: thumbnails and full size as JPEGs at the right sizes and upright, the big one made 2000 px, a small JPEG sent as it is, PDFs as files, the diary's photo flags, the menu's chips in order with their words and highlights, the icon by each title, sticking under the top bar, the name chip, Money and the Diary landing just below it (the Diary opened), every photo shown and none as 'IMG_...', the PDF with no ending as a file tile and file in the viewer, the email's 3 photos without its placeholders, '[photo: ...]' in Emails and texts, the lit chip following the scroll, Email or text landing below the menu, back to the top, the chips wrapping on a computer, Sarah's Quote chip and visit tomorrow, less motion jumping at once, no sideways scroll, no page errors); board/ui, pay and sent still pass (ui's 'colour key fits the iPhone 13 screen' fails since the eighth section was added on 2 Nov, not touched here). Not tried on Raf's real ServiceM8 yet: the first job with photos from an iPhone is the real test.
- **Photos that came with a message (4 Nov 2026, Raf: 'i still don't see some pictures being shown in service m8 and not in jobkit like becki ponting message not showing in diary pictures'; previewed on the `diary-email-photos` branch, live since 4 Nov 2026, Raf: 'merge')**: the diary only asked ServiceM8 for attachments filed on the job (attachment.json, related_object_uuid = the job), but ServiceM8 doesn't publish where it keeps a customer's emailed or texted photos, and they can be filed on the message instead. Now jobDiary (src/lib/servicem8-diary.ts messageFiles) also asks, for the newest 12 messages (theirs first, then Raf's), for the attachments filed on each message (related_object_uuid = the email's or text's uuid, whatever kind ServiceM8 names), three at a time, and looks up one by one (up to 12, GET attachment/<uuid>.json) any attachment a message's record points to: fileRefs() in src/lib/servicem8-mail.ts searches every field named like attach / photo / image / media / file for record uuids (lists, objects, JSON strings), leaving out the record's own, job, client and staff uuids. Each file found carries `of` = its message's entry id ('msg-<uuid>'); one also filed on the job is marked, not doubled. mailOf also adds '[image: name]' for photos only the HTML version shows (cid images the plain text leaves out). Archived attachments are left out. Quotes (jobPhotos) gets the emailed and texted photos too. **Phone** (job-notes.tsx `under`): under each email or text its own files (`of`), the photos it names found by name, and, for one of theirs that names photos still not found, the job's photos added within 3 minutes of it that no other message has; photos as thumbnails, PDFs and other files as a row with Open (data-email-file); those aren't shown again on their own in All (Photos and files still lists every one, Messages keeps them under the message); a named photo the Job Kit couldn't get reads '(photo IMG_x.jpeg: open it in ServiceM8)'. **Log** (until the real layout is known): when a customer's message names photos the diary couldn't find, or its record has a field like attachments, one line '[diary-photos] {...}' goes to Vercel's runtime logs: counts, the record's field names, the shapes of its attachment-like and related fields, and where the job's own attachments are filed (related_object, attachment_source, class_name counts); never a value, a name or an address (read them with the Vercel connector's get_runtime_logs, query 'diary-photos'; take it out once settled). Tested on a production build against a pretend ServiceM8 (scratchpad dphoto/mock.mjs, made-up customer; photos filed on two emails and a text, one only in the HTML, a PDF named in the record but filed on another object, a photo the email calls image0.jpeg filed on the job a minute later as Photo 1.jpg, one named that ServiceM8 hasn't got, an archived one): dphoto/lib.mts 18 checks (every file and its message, the log line with no names or words, Quotes' 6 photos oldest first); dphoto/ui.cjs 57 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1440 (each message's photos and the PDF under it, no '[photo:' words, the missing one's line, the site photo alone, thumbnails drawn, Photos and files 7, Messages, a photo and the PDF opening, no sideways scroll, no page errors). Not tried on Raf's real ServiceM8 yet: Becki's job is the test, and the log line says where ServiceM8 really keeps them if they still don't show.
- **Email signature icons aren't pictures (8 Nov 2026, Raf: 'in jobkit there is a new job from Ana cayana showing signs of links as pictures but no pictures fix it', with screenshots of job #180)**: the job had been made with New enquiry from an email, and Outlook's plain text writes a picture with no words as its address in brackets ('[https://signaturehound.com/api/v1/png/mobile/default/....png]': the phone, map, website and Instagram icons of an email signature) and a link as its words then the address ('cayana.co.uk<http://www.cayana.co.uk>', '0770...<tel:0770...>'), so the description showed a column of image links. cleanLinks() in src/lib/mail-text.ts (no imports) takes out web pictures in brackets, drops a link address that only repeats its words (phone numbers matched by their last digits), keeps any other address as plain text after its words ('Like this one https://pinterest.com/...'), turns a lone '<https://...>' into the address and drops the same address twice running. Photos in the email itself ('[cid:...]', '[image: ...]', photoMarks) stay. Used in servicem8.ts shape() (every job description, so job #180 reads clean without changing ServiceM8, where it still shows the brackets), servicem8-mail.ts mailOf (emails in the diary and Emails and texts), outlook.ts tidy() and the list preview (Inbox), and inbox.tsx enquiryFromMail / conceptBriefFromMail (new enquiries and Design with Claude). The 7 files ServiceM8 has on that job were never the problem (the diary's thumbnails didn't fail). Two log lines added while looking stay: '[diary-file]' when a diary file can't be shown (why, sniffed type, first bytes, no names) and the extra file-type counts in '[diary-photos]'. Tested (scratchpad links/test.mts, 17 checks on a made-up signature email: no bracketed web picture or <address> left, phone and websites once, the cid photo kept, a Pinterest link and 'Click<https://click.com>' keeping their addresses, a spaced phone, Gmail's image mark and ordinary '<-' text untouched). Live from 6f277a4. **Photos pasted into an email (13 Nov 2026, Raf, a screenshot of a job made with New enquiry from this email, its description full of '[cid:f5f95524-...@GBRP123.PROD.OUTLOOK.COM]' lines)**: Outlook's plain text writes a photo pasted into the email as its content id, with no name. cleanLinks() now turns '[cid:image001.jpg@...]' into '[photo: image001.jpg]' and a run of nameless ones into one '[3 photos in the email]' (also on job descriptions already in ServiceM8, through shape(), and in the Inbox, the assistant's jobs and Design with Claude); photoMarks() counts those as `unnamed` (also htmlToText's cid images with no alt), so the Job diary still looks for the job's photos added within 3 minutes of that email and hides the line once they show under it. - **Remove a Quote job made by mistake (3 Nov 2026, Raf: 'if i want to delete current job in jobkit which was created by mistake how do i delete it', then 'yes please' to a button; previewed on the `remove-mistake-job` branch, live since 3 Nov 2026, Raf: 'merge')**: the one place the Job Kit takes something out of ServiceM8, and it only archives. **Server** (src/lib/servicem8.ts removeJob / restoreJob; /api/job-kit/sm8 actions `remove` {job, client?} and `restore` {job, bookings, client?}): reads the job even if archived (anyOne); refuses anything not on Quote (sm8-not-quote 409, detail the status) or with an active payment (sm8-paid 409); unknown job sm8-gone with detail 'job'. Archives (ServiceM8's DELETE, active 0, restorable; developer.servicem8.com 'Delete a Job': set active back to 1 to restore) its scheduled bookings still to come (not automated ones), then the job; with client true it archives the customer too, but only when listAll job.json for that company finds no other active job (any status, any age; if that read fails the customer stays). Answers Removed {job, bookings, client, clientName, others}. restoreJob POSTs active '1' to the customer, then the job, then each booking. **App** (current.tsx RemoveJob, at the bottom of Where it's up to on a Quote job with no payments, when onRemove is given): 'Created by mistake? Remove it' (quiet underlined link) opens a dashed box 'Remove this job?' saying it goes off the Job Kit and is archived in ServiceM8 where it can be brought back, 'Its visit tomorrow at 10:00 is cancelled too.' when one is booked, a tick (on to start with) 'Remove <name> from your customers too / Only if this is their only job in ServiceM8. If they have older ones, they stay.', or, when the customer has another job on this device (otherJobs from Current jobs and customers opened), '<first> stays as a customer: there's another job for them.' and no tick; Remove it (ink) and Keep it. ServiceM8 refusing leaves the box open with the reason. On success (job-kit-app.tsx removeJob) the job leaves sm8.jobs (kept on the device), its bookings and customerJobs, a removed customer leaves the search list (fetched again next time), the job stays findable as `leaving` while the screen goes back (so the sm8 screen doesn't jump to its parent as well), and goneJobs keeps it off a list already on its way. A bar at the bottom (data-removed-bar, same look as the deleted-email bar, stacked with it when both show): 'Removed Sarah Jones’s job #103, its booking and the customer. Archived in ServiceM8.' (plus '<first> stays as a customer: there is another job.' when asked but kept), Undo for 12 s (sendRestore, the job and its bookings back in the list, Current jobs fetched again; a refused Undo says it's still archived in ServiceM8, where it can be restored) and ×. Footer line on a Quote job: 'Nothing is ever deleted there: a job removed is only archived.' Tested on a production build (scratchpad rmjob/test.cjs, 57 checks against rmjob/sm8mock.mjs with made-up customers, /__fail?delete=1&restore=1 to make it refuse: refused with a payment, on Work Order, unknown and bad ids, wrong PIN; Kasia removed with her customer, off the list, archived, Undo back; Sarah's visit tomorrow named, cancelled with the job, her customer kept when unticked, gone from Today and the Diary and after a reload; Tom's duplicate with no tick and his other job kept; Ava's old Completed job keeping her though ticked; no button on Noah's deposit or Harry's Work Order; a refused delete keeping the box and the job, then removed; a refused Undo, then Undo; the bar going after 12 s; the box and bar at 320; the job closing beside the list on a computer; no page errors; restore bringing a job's visit back). Not tried on Raf's real ServiceM8 yet: the first real removal is the test (check the job shows under archived jobs in ServiceM8).

Claude's instructions say 'in a row' / 'side by side' = drawers 1, across 3, 'in a column' / 'stacked' = drawers 3, across 1, a low piece (bench, window seat, TV unit, under 600 high) has them side by side unless told otherwise, fronts 250 to 1,000 wide and 120+ high. drawerGrid(section, bay width) gives rows and columns: `across` when set, else an older plan's drawers go side by side in a bay over 1,000 wide when stacking would make each under 200 high and each would be 300+ wide (so designs made before this draw right without a redraw); asDrawn() writes that out for a change sent to Claude; drawerCount() prices rows × columns at the drawer rate. ConceptDrawing draws the grid of fronts with a handle each, and inside the uprights between columns and a box per drawer. The only way is Meta's WhatsApp Business Platform with 'coexistence' (the Business app number also on the Cloud API), onboarded through a Solution Partner or Tech Provider, needing a verified Meta Business portfolio; from 1 Oct 2026 service replies sent through it cost £0.0159 each after 1,000 free a month per number, messages sent from the app itself stay free; history sync is up to 180 days, no groups, media only for the first 14 days (Meta's 'Onboarding WhatsApp Business app users' page and UK rate pages, checked 19 Oct 2026). Not set up; offered as a later stage. So a chat comes in through WhatsApp's own **Export Chat** (the chat, their name, Export Chat, Attach Media, Save to Files), picked in the Job Kit. **Reading** (src/lib/whatsapp-chat.ts, no imports): parseChat(text, zipFileNames) reads iPhone ('[19/10/2026, 10:21:05] Name: text', 12-hour US dates with the narrow space, Polish dots) and Android ('19/10/2026, 10:21 - Name: text') lines, messages over several lines, the direction marks; the date order is worked out from the dates (day first unless only month first fits); an attachment is a file in the zip named in the text whatever the words round it ('<attached: ...>', '(file attached)', Polish), its caption kept, an iPhone document's 'Plan.pdf • 3 pages' dropped; 'image omitted' / '<Media omitted>' mark media not in the export; the encryption notice, deleted messages and missed calls are system lines; stable ids by time, sender and words, so mergeMsgs adds only what's new when the same chat comes in again; nameFromFile ('WhatsApp Chat - Kasia Nowak.zip', 'WhatsApp Chat with ...', a number for an unsaved contact), guessMe (Raf is the writer who isn't the chat's name, or matches Raf/Rafal/Rafał/Janczy/Raf Carpentry; else the chat asks 'Which one is you?'), lastStretch (after the last gap of a week), withoutGreeting ('Hi Raf, it's Sarah again. We'd like...' -> 'We'd like...', so jobs are named by what they want). **On the phone** (src/app/job-kit/wa-store.ts): fflate (now a direct dependency, 0.8.2; posthog keeps its own 0.4) unzips only the chat text, photos and PDFs (videos, voice notes and stickers stay in WhatsApp); chats live in their own IndexedDB 'raf-job-kit-wa' (chats + files, nothing leaves the phone until sent); bringing the same chat in again (same name or number) adds the new messages, and if it already went to a job only the new ones are ticked. To start with, ticked = in the last stretch, their words and every photo and PDF. **Sending** runs outside the screens (carries on while Raf looks at the job; written down after each file, so no signal or a reload carries on): each ticked photo is redrawn as a 2400 px JPEG (no GPS) and each PDF sent as it is to /api/job-kit/attach (new, behind the PIN: multipart job, file, name, key; JPEG or %PDF only, up to 4 MB; ServiceM8 uuid = stableUuid(job:wa:key) in brief-token.ts, so a retry is a 409, never a second copy), then /api/job-kit/sm8 gets two new actions: note {job, text, key} (addJobNote in servicem8.ts, a note on the job, keyed so it isn't added twice) and touch {job} (touchJob, so the files show in the diary). Too big / wrong type have plain words in sm8-post.ts. **Screens** (src/app/job-kit/whatsapp.tsx): a seventh section 'WhatsApp' (sections.tsx; its dot, Today box edge and top line in WhatsApp's own green #25d366 via --sec-mark, text in a darker #0f7b3e, so it can't be taken for the teal Customers or the olive timeline; every other section sets --sec-mark to its own colour; new 'chat' icon, two speech bubbles); Today box after Customers ('WhatsApp chats', 'N chats · M not in ServiceM8 yet · newest ...'); To do lines 'WhatsApp from <name>: not in ServiceM8 yet' (brought in during the last 14 days, nothing sent) and 'WhatsApp files for <name> didn't all go'. WhatsApp screen: the three Export Chat steps, Choose the exported chat (a zip without a chat or messages is turned down in plain words), the chats on this phone with their last line, counts and where they went. A chat: name, counts, 'Bring it in again for newer messages'; In ServiceM8 (each trip: 'New job #102: We'd like ...' or 'Added to #101', 'Sending 2 of 4…' / '3 of 3 files in the job's diary' / 'N didn't go' with Send again, messages added as a note, Open the job); 'Who is it in ServiceM8?' (sameCustomers from customers.tsx on the chat's name and the number when the contact isn't saved or one in their messages: 'It's them'; or a search over the same customer list as Today's box; 'See their jobs', 'Not them'); the conversation in bubbles (theirs white, Raf's light green, days as headings, earlier messages folded under 'Show N earlier messages', 'Ticked to start with: from <day>, after a gap of a week or more'), a tick box on each message and photo (tap the bubble too), photos shown from the phone with a full-screen view, PDFs as chips, voice notes and videos as 'stays in WhatsApp', 'Tick all from <first>' / None; a bar fixed at the bottom ('3 messages · 2 photos · 1 PDF', 'For Sarah Jones', Send to ServiceM8) opens a sheet: **New job** (New enquiry filled in by enquiryFromChat: a website brief pasted in the chat is read as one (parseBrief), else the ticked words without the greeting, 'From a WhatsApp chat, <day>. 2 photos and 1 PDF in the job's diary.'; the name, the mobile (their number, one in their words, or the customer's), email, an address with a postcode cut down to '12 Park Road, N8 8JD'; on the picked customer's record; NewEnquiry takes seedFrom 'whatsapp' for its words; after Add to ServiceM8 the files follow and the job opens with a 'From WhatsApp' box, back says Chat), **Add to #101: <their open job>** for each of the picked customer's open Quote and Work Order jobs (the words as a note 'From WhatsApp, <day>: ...', the files in its diary), and **A 3D design request from the photos** (New design with what and mobile filled in, linked to the job if one; the ticked photos become 'The space', the ticked words the brief). Take this chat off this phone (two taps; WhatsApp and ServiceM8 keep everything). Job screen: CurrentView `extra` shows WaJobBox under Where it's up to when a chat went to that job. The colour key's intro and rows were tightened so seven fit an iPhone 13 (the 'Open this again' line folded into the intro); the dots get smaller under 360 px. (2) Designs in 3D, on the website: 'Show it on the website?' with Yes, they're happy / Not this one (only once the customer is happy; area only), then 'Going on at the next design check (every 2 hours, 7am to 9pm)' with Not yet, keep it off; once live 'On the website, area only' with its address and Take it off (two taps), then 'Coming off ...' with Keep it on; 'Not on the website' with Show it after all; built: 'The finished job shows there now'. The answer is design.publish {ok, at} on the request (DesignBrief in store.ts), sent in job.json (design.publish) and notes.txt ('On the website (Designs in 3D): yes, the customer agreed'); readyAt doesn't change, so nothing is rebuilt. (3) My Work: 'Built: <title>' with Open the job page; the Job Kit job on its way; or 'Start the Job Kit for it', which opens New job filled in from the design ('Built from the 3D design ...') and saves `fromDesign` (the request id) on the Job Kit job. (4) Design library: always (as the finished job once built). **Links by themselves**: a Job Kit job started from a ServiceM8 job that has a design gets fromDesign too (kitJobFor / designFor in designs.tsx also match by the same ServiceM8 job); the job's screen shows 'Built from the 3D design <what>' (opens it); job.json carries fromDesign and notes.txt a 'Built from the 3D design request' line; scripts/job-kit-fetch.py prints 'built from the 3D design <code> ...: put "design": "<code>" in the job JSON' and the design's model files. **Already made?** New design checks before starting (similarDesigns: same ServiceM8 job, or the same area and a shared word in what it is, on this phone and in the library's customer designs) and lists them with Open it / Open; Start becomes 'Start a new one anyway'; from a ServiceM8 job that already has one it shows at once. **To do**: '<what>: show it on the website?' once a design was sent to the customer a day ago and not answered (opens it; 'N designs: show them on the website?'); the Designs list adds '· on the website', '· built' or '· website?'. **The scheduled design run** (trig_01Qw5s1VqbQBN75fXbkqdFvQ, prompt updated 19 Oct 2026): scripts/design-requests.py also prints `[publish]` / `[unpublish]` when a request's design.publish differs from its design's `public` (and 'built: <slug>'), and the run follows scripts/DESIGNS.md step 8: add `"public": true` or remove it, after checking the words show nothing that identifies the customer, build, push, check READY. Those don't count towards the 2 builds a run. - **Phone and computer in step (19 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Do it' to stage 2 of the desktop Job Kit)**: what the Job Kit kept on one device now goes through a small private store, so the phone and the computer show the same. **Kept in step** (src/app/job-kit/sync.ts): `mark` (a ServiceM8 job's marks: quote sent, progress, visits done, review asked, hidden, emails seen), `posted` (a post pack's ticks), `job` (Job Kit jobs and design requests, without design.mobile: the customer's mobile stays on the device it was typed on, and is put back when a copy comes in) and `item` (a photo or clip's details with its 240 px thumbnail, without parts/sentParts/sentAt); since the same evening also `wa` and `wafile` (see 'WhatsApp chats in step' below). **Not**: drafts, and what each device fetches itself (ServiceM8 lists, the inbox, website stats, designs ready). **Files**: a photo's file stays on the device that took it, which sends it to the inbox; on the other device the item is `remote: true` (uploader.ts skips it, never marks it lost; the photo panel says 'on your other device, sent from there'; the Send tab '1 still on your phone: they go from there when the Job Kit is open on it'). Only the owner says whether it's sent; the other device can change its caption or delete it. A job's details (job.json) wait until every photo of it is in the inbox, and go from the device that made the latest change, or the one that sent its last photos after it (mayDetail in uploader.ts; editedElsewhere = job.syncedIn === job.updatedAt, syncedIn being the version last taken in, kept on the device). **Rounds**: on open, when the app comes back, going into the background, every 30 s while open, 2.5 s after a change (reload(), mark(), posted ticks), 3 s after the uploader changes something; each round hashes everything here (canonical JSON, keys sorted, cyrb53), compares with what both sides last had (localStorage raf_jobkit_sync_seen), sends what changed (raf_jobkit_sync_pend: hash and when, about 1.4 MB a request, marks first, photos last) and takes in what the other device changed since its place (raf_jobkit_sync_rev). The later change wins (by when it changed on its device); a change made here while a round is out, or a newer one not sent yet, wins; the first time a device ever syncs, what it has counts as the oldest (at 1), so a store copy wins where both have something. Taking a job in keeps seq at the higher of the two and sets syncedIn; a design request or the Notes tab open on screen is keyed by syncedIn, so the other device's change shows at once instead of being written over. Clearing or removing a job, design request or photo removes it on both (the store keeps a 'deleted' row), except a job the device still has photos of to send: kept, and put back. Device id raf_jobkit_device, label from the browser (iPhone, Mac...). **Server**: POST /api/job-kit/sync behind the PIN {device, label, since, docs} -> src/lib/job-kit-sync-server.ts -> Supabase RPC public.jobkit_sync(p_secret, p_device, p_label, p_since, p_docs) in the cabinetos project (uzrnnnnfdrqunyrysodz; migration 'jobkit_sync'): schema jobkit (docs (kind, id) with data jsonb (null = deleted), at, device, rev from a sequence; devices; config with the secret's sha256 only), RLS on with no policies, not exposed; SECURITY DEFINER, anon may run it (like rafsite_*); one round at a time (advisory lock), each doc kept only if newer (else returned as `lost` for the device to take), then up to 400 docs / about 2 MB of the other devices' changes after `since` (`more` to ask again), and the other devices last seen. Uses RAFSTATS_URL / RAFSTATS_KEY (JOBKIT_SYNC_URL / _KEY override them for tests) and JOBKIT_SYNC_SECRET (Vercel, sensitive, production + preview, set 19 Oct 2026 with the connector, never printed); without them it answers 503 sync-not-set-up and each device just keeps its own. **Words**: Settings 'Phone and computer' ('In step with your iPhone, last there 2 min ago', what's kept in step and what stays, 'Bring them into step now'); Today's last line adds the same; the sidebar 'The same app as on your phone, kept in step with it. WhatsApp chats stay on the device they came into.'; clearing and removing now say 'off your phone and computer'; the design request's mobile '(optional, stays on the device you type it on)'. Tested against the real function in a throwaway local Postgres 16 (scratchpad sync/supa-mock.mjs answers as PostgREST; sync/start.sh; sync/two.js, 40 checks as an iPhone 13 and a 1440 computer: the phone's jobs, a design request, three photos, a mark and the posted ticks go up, the mobile never leaves the phone, the computer gets them with thumbnails and no files and never tries to send them, Today and Settings say who it's in step with, a caption, the design request's words and a TikTok tick from the computer reach the phone with its file and mobile kept, an open design request updates by itself, the phone sends its photos and the computer shows them sent, the job's details reach the inbox once with the photo sent and the computer's caption, a design request removed on the computer goes from the phone, a job cleared elsewhere while the phone still has a photo of it is kept and put back, 320 with no sideways scroll, no page errors; sync/big.js: 150 photos (3.9 MB of thumbnails) up in 3 requests and down in 2 pages, Visit done on the computer's Diary leaves the phone's). The earlier suites still pass (no sync settings: each device on its own). Not tried on Raf's devices yet: open the Job Kit on the phone first (it fills the store), then on the computer.
- **WhatsApp chats in step, and new versions by themselves (19 Oct 2026, Raf, after the sync went live: 'In jobkit on desktop some things are not sync', then 'WhatsApp chats' and 'Sync them' to keeping them in the same store)**: **Chats**: kinds `wa` (a chat: messages, people, ticks, customer, trips to ServiceM8, design; without the local-only `remote`) and `wafile` (a 200 px JPEG preview of one of its photos, about 2 to 8 KB, id `<chat>/<file>`) in sync.ts; migration 'jobkit_sync_whatsapp' lets them into jobkit.docs and jobkit_sync. wa-store.ts: IndexedDB 'raf-job-kit-wa' version 2 with a 'thumbs' store; previews made when a chat is brought in (previewOf) and, for chats from before, once on open (ensureThumbs); each chat says where it was brought in (`importedOn`, the browser's label from device.ts) and is `remote` on the other device, where its photos and PDFs aren't (the bubbles show the previews, 'The photos show as small previews here. The full ones are on your iPhone and go into ServiceM8 from there, even when you send from here.'); bringing a chat in again on a device makes it that device's. Two copies are put together, not overwritten (mergeChat: every message, file and trip either has, each trip's sent / failed / note / touched together; ticks, customer and design from the newer one). A trip with files started on the computer is sent by the device that has them when it next syncs (kick skips trips with files on a remote chat; the computer shows '3 files to go from your iPhone'); attachments and notes are keyed in ServiceM8 (409 on a repeat), so two devices can never add one twice. A design request from a chat's photos is only offered where the photos are ('start it on your iPhone, where the full photos are'). 'Take this chat off the Job Kit' takes it off both (not while the device with its files still has some to send). Privacy page: the job app's notes and brought-in WhatsApp chats (messages and photo previews) kept in Supabase, Frankfurt, so the phone and computer show the same; Supabase listed for 'the visit counts and my job app'; updated 19 October 2026. **New versions**: next.config.ts `env.JK_BUILD` (Vercel's commit id, else the build time) is built into the page and the route /api/job-kit/version (JK_LIVE stands in for a newer one in tests); src/app/job-kit/update.ts checks 4 s after opening, when the app comes back and every 5 minutes; when a newer one is live the app shows 'A new version of the Job Kit is ready. Update' under the top bar and loads it by itself on Today with nothing being typed and no sheet open, or when the app comes back after 30 s away; by itself only once per version in a tab (sessionStorage raf_jobkit_reloaded_for), so it can't loop. **Done on every booking** went live in the same hour (see 'Visit done in the Diary'). Tested: sync/wa-sync.js (the phone brings Sarah's made-up chat in, its 3 previews go up; the computer shows the chat with previews and the note, picks her, unticks a photo, sends to #101 and sends no files itself; the phone takes the trip in and sends 2 photos, the PDF and one note; the computer shows 3 of 3; the untick reached the phone; a newer copy brought in on the phone reaches the computer; taking it off on the computer removes chat, previews and files from the phone), sync/update.js (a newer build: Today reloads once then shows the note, Settings shows the note and Update loads it; the same build: nothing), plus sync/two.js, sync/big.js and the earlier suites.

On a phone (iPhone, iPad, Android) it's `waze://?q=<address>&navigate=yes` with no new tab, so the Waze app opens straight away and starts driving there (Waze's https link would go through an in-app browser from the home-screen app first; waze:// does nothing if Waze isn't installed).
