- **Instagram and Facebook in the Job Kit (20 Oct 2026, Raf: 'is there a way to see in job kit social media profiles with followers and comments as a separate section, instagram, tiktok, youtube, facebook?', then 'start with instagram and facebook i have youtube for rafcarpentry')**: a Social screen in the Photos and posts section (rose; Today row 'Instagram and Facebook', sidebar 'Instagram and Facebook' with the count of new comments, new 'social' icon: a heart in a speech bubble). **How it connects**: one Facebook login through Raf's own Meta app (development mode, so only people with a role on the app can use it and no App Review is needed) gives a Page access token for the Raf Carpentry Page, which also reads the Instagram professional account linked to that Page (Instagram API with Facebook Login). Login asks for SCOPES in src/lib/meta-social.ts (pages_show_list, pages_read_engagement, pages_read_user_content, pages_manage_engagement, instagram_basic, instagram_manage_comments, business_management), or uses a Facebook Login for Business configuration when META_LOGIN_CONFIG_ID is set; META_SCOPES replaces the list (e.g. add ads_read if the Instagram fields are refused because the Page role comes through a business portfolio). Flow: POST /api/job-kit/social {action: 'start'} (behind the PIN) gives the dialog address with a signed state (HMAC with the app secret, 30 minutes); Facebook comes back to GET /api/job-kit/social/meta/callback (not behind the PIN; the state proves it; must be listed in the Meta app as a Valid OAuth Redirect URI: https://www.rafcarpentry.com/api/job-kit/social/meta/callback), which swaps the code for a long-lived user token (fb_exchange_token), reads /me, /me/permissions and /me/accounts (Pages with their tokens and instagram_business_account), picks the Page (the only one, the only one with Instagram, or the only one named Raf...; else Raf picks in the app) and redirects to /job-kit?social=ok | pick | cancelled | expired | <error>, which opens Social with a note and takes the ?social= off the address (on an iPhone home-screen app the login opens in a browser window: the note says to close it). Every Graph call sends appsecret_proof; Graph API v25.0 (META_API_VERSION). **Kept**: Supabase cabinetos project, migration 'jobkit_social': jobkit.social (key 'meta': who connected, the Pages with tokens SEALED with AES-256-GCM under a key made from JOBKIT_SYNC_SECRET, the picked Page, permissions given and declined, seenAt = the newest comment Raf has had in front of him) and jobkit.social_counts (followers and posts per platform, account and London day, written on every overview fetch); RLS on, no policies, one SECURITY DEFINER function public.jobkit_social(p_secret, p_action get|put|patch|del|counts, p) checking the same secret hash as jobkit_sync (src/lib/job-kit-sync-server.ts jobkitRpc). Comments, posts and pictures are never kept on the server: Meta -> server -> the device (raf_jobkit_social in localStorage, refreshed on open and when the app comes back, at most every 10 minutes). Page tokens made from a long-lived user token don't run out; a changed Facebook password, removing the app or losing the Page role stops them (then 'Facebook wants you to connect again'). If Instagram refuses the Page token, the user token (about 60 days) is tried. **Screen** (src/app/job-kit/social.tsx): the two profiles (picture, followers, '+30 this week', '+130 in 30 days' or 'since <day>' while the history is short, 'Counting from today' on the first day, a small line of followers over up to 90 days, Instagram posts and following, Open), Comments (top-level comments on the latest 12 Instagram and 10 Facebook posts, newest first, Raf's own left out; chips Not answered (N) / All / Instagram / Facebook; each with the platform, who, when, New, the text, the post it's on, replies with Raf's as You, '✓ You replied' or 'Not answered yet', Reply (a box; Send reply posts it: Instagram POST /<comment>/replies, Facebook POST /<comment>/comments as the Page; only when Raf presses Send; an answered one stays on screen until he leaves) and Open in Instagram / Facebook), Latest posts (3 a row, likes and comments, Reel/Photo/Video), then who connected, Change Page, Connect again (auth_type rerequest), Disconnect (two taps; follower history kept). On a computer the profiles sit side by side across the top and the posts and comments in two columns (ROOMY). Today: 'Question from @x on Instagram' (a question mark or price/quote/available words, bold) or 'Comment from ...' / '3 new comments: 2 on Instagram, 1 on Facebook' for unanswered comments newer than seenAt (the server's and this device's, so the phone and computer agree); the row's line 'Instagram 1,530 (+30) · Facebook 412 (−8) · 1 new comment'. **Live since 20 Oct 2026** (Raf connected from the computer at 20:24; his screenshot showed Instagram @rafcarpentry 728 followers, 216 posts, following 349, and the Facebook Page 'Rafal Carpentry Services' 80 followers, 10 comments with 4 not answered, the latest posts): Meta app 'Raf Job Kit' (App ID 1540364174782150, Business type, Development mode, made by Raf on developers.facebook.com: Create App, Others, Other, Business, no business portfolio; products Instagram with API setup with Facebook login, and Facebook Login for Business with the redirect URI above in Settings, Valid OAuth Redirect URIs). Vercel: META_APP_ID (plain, production, set with the connector) and META_APP_SECRET (sensitive, production, added by Raf), then Raf redeployed. The login used the scope list (no configuration needed); every permission was given (none declined); Facebook shared 4 Pages and the one with Instagram was picked by itself. jobkit.social_counts has the first day's counts (ig 728 / 216 posts, fb 80), so '+N this week' starts showing from 21 Oct. Tested against a pretend Meta (scratchpad social/meta-mock.mjs: dialog, token swaps with the exact redirect URI, appsecret_proof checked, /me/accounts with 1 to 3 Pages, Page, published_posts, IG user and media with nested comments, replies; modes deny, expired, igperm, pages, noreply) and the real function on a throwaway local Postgres 16 behind a PostgREST stand-in (social/supa-mock.mjs): social/ui.mjs, 54 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320, 1024 and 1440 (Connect, the 7 permissions, the long-lived swap, tokens sealed, followers counted, Not answered 3 and Raf's own left out, seen, an Instagram and a Facebook reply, empty reply off, Cancel, filters, history +30 / +130 / −8, Today's question line and row, New, the line gone once seen, the computer's two columns, Instagram through the user token, expired with the last numbers kept, three Pages and a pick, Not now, a forged state turned away, a declined permission pointed out and a refused reply keeping its words, Disconnect, no sideways scroll, no page errors). Next: YouTube (Raf has a Raf Carpentry channel: Data API v3 with an API key, channel statistics and comment threads), then TikTok numbers (Display API; no comment text there).
- **Threads on the Social screen (21 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Let's go with threads')**: the screen is now 'Social media' (Today row, sidebar and title; kicker 'Instagram, Facebook and Threads'; colour key line 'Job Kit jobs, posts and social media.'). **Code split**: src/lib/social-types.ts (types and answeredOf / ms / clip, no imports; SocialOverview v2 = {v: 2, meta: account + pages/page, threads: account, profiles, posts, comments, seenAt, problems, at}, each account {setUp, missing, connected, who, connectedAt, notGranted, error}); src/lib/social-store.ts (seal / unseal, the signed login state with its own tag per platform, kept / keep / forget rows, counts); src/lib/meta-social.ts (metaPart(), connectMeta, metaLoginUrl, metaStateOk, pickPage, replyMeta, disconnectMeta); src/lib/threads-social.ts (threadsPart(), connectThreads, threadsLoginUrl, threadsStateOk, replyThreads, disconnectThreads); src/lib/social.ts overview() puts the parts together (counts for every profile, profiles ig, fb, th, posts newest first, comments by their newest word, up to 100) and markSeen(at) keeps 'seen' {at} for all platforms (the old meta.seenAt is still read). One part failing never stops the other. /api/job-kit/social takes `platform: 'threads'` on start / reply / disconnect (reply text up to 500 characters on Threads). **Threads API** (developers.facebook.com/documentation/threads, checked 21 Oct 2026): login window https://threads.com/oauth/authorize (client_id, redirect_uri, scope, response_type code, state), back to GET /api/job-kit/social/threads/callback (the code's trailing #_ never reaches the server; ?social=threads-ok | cancelled | expired | <code>), code swapped by POST https://graph.threads.com/oauth/access_token (authorization_code), then GET /access_token grant_type th_exchange_token (60 days), refreshed by GET /refresh_access_token grant_type th_refresh_token whenever the overview runs and the token is over 3 days old (a refresh that fails is ignored while the token has time left), kept sealed as jobkit.social 'threads' {user {id, username}, token, until, refreshedAt, connectedAt}. Scopes THREADS_SCOPES: threads_basic, threads_read_replies, threads_manage_replies, threads_manage_insights, threads_content_publish. Reads (v1.0): /me (username, name, threads_profile_picture_url), /<user>/threads_insights?metric=followers_count (total_value), /me/threads (12, REPOST_FACADE left out, then 8), and per thread /<id>/conversation (reverse=false, every reply at any depth with replied_to, is_reply_owned_by_me, hide_status) and /<id>/insights?metric=likes,replies. A reply straight under the thread is a comment; everything below it are its replies (Raf's as You); Raf's own replies straight under his thread and hidden / blocked / restricted ones are left out. answeredOf(): answered = Raf has replied after their newest word; a follow-up after his reply makes it 'They wrote again' and it counts as new again (activeAt) on every platform. Replying: POST /me/threads {media_type TEXT, text, reply_to_id = their newest word in that thread} then /me/threads_publish {creation_id}; if Threads says not ready it checks the container's status (PUBLISHED = done, never published twice) and tries again for about 20 s. Errors threads-expired (only code 190 and the like) | permission | busy | gone | store | error | not-set-up. **Screen**: the profiles in a row (two on a phone held sideways, three from 1024), a dashed Connect card in the row for an account not connected (Connect Instagram and Facebook / Connect Threads, or 'Not set up yet: ... missing in Vercel'); 'Facebook (or Threads) wants you to connect again' with the button above, while the last numbers, posts and comments of that platform stay from this device (carry() in social.tsx; a platform that fails this time keeps its last ones); problems per platform under the profiles; chips Not answered / All / Instagram / Facebook / Threads (only those in use); Threads posts are shown by their words when there's no picture (a Facebook post with words only too), with likes and replies; 'Show N more' posts after 12; footer per account (Instagram and Facebook: who, Page, Change Page, Connect again, Disconnect; Threads: connected as @..., Connect again, Disconnect; each Disconnect two taps and only that account). An overview kept on the device from before (v1) is read as v2 (toV2). Today: 'Question from @leo.fits on Threads', '@sam wrote again on Threads', '2 new comments: 1 on Instagram, 1 on Threads'; row 'Instagram 728 · Facebook 80 · Threads 214 (+14)'. **Supabase**: migration 'jobkit_social_threads' (21 Oct 2026) lets 'th' into jobkit.social_counts and jobkit_social's counts. Vercel: THREADS_APP_ID (plain) and THREADS_APP_SECRET (sensitive), production; optional THREADS_SCOPES, THREADS_REDIRECT_URI, THREADS_GRAPH_API / THREADS_DIALOG (tests). Tested (scratchpad social/threads-mock.mjs on 4015: login window, the POST swap with the exact redirect URI, th_exchange_token, refresh, me, followers insight, threads with a repost, conversations with a follow-up, an own reply and a hidden one, insights, container + publish with modes slow, noinsights, expired, refreshfail, deny; social/start.sh starts both pretend platforms, the PostgREST stand-in and next dev; social/threads.mjs 64 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320, 1024 and 1440: a v1 overview kept on the phone, two Connect cards, connect, the 5 permissions, sealed token with 60 days, profile and counts, Not answered 2 / All 3, own and hidden replies left out, Sam's follow-up, the words-only tile, the 500-character box, a reply as container + publish, a slow one published once under Sam's newest, all three profiles in order, the Threads chip, +14 this week, the Today lines, seen, the refresh after 3 days, a failed refresh, no insights, expired keeping the last numbers, three in a row at 1440, Threads disconnected alone, Cancel, a forged state and a Facebook state turned away; social/ui.mjs (Instagram and Facebook) still passes). **Live since 21 Oct 2026** (Raf connected at 14:26 London as @rafcarpentry; 96 followers counted on 21 and 22 Oct; token sealed, runs to 20 Dec 2026 and is refreshed while the Job Kit is used): Meta app 'Raf Job Kit Threads' (Meta app ID 1137767009211766, use case Access the Threads API only, Development mode; Threads app ID 1630709658734036 = THREADS_APP_ID, set with the connector; THREADS_APP_SECRET added by Raf, sensitive, production; then redeployed). The tester invite in Threads (phone and threads.com, Settings, Account, Website permissions, Invitations) only shows Remove, no Accept, and connecting worked as it was. In Add People pick Threads Tester (the last choice), not Tester, or the box searches Facebook accounts. How it was set up: a separate Meta app with the 'Access the Threads API' use case (that use case can't share an app with others), its permissions added, https://www.rafcarpentry.com/api/job-kit/social/threads/callback as the Redirect Callback URL (also Uninstall and Delete callback URLs: same address is fine), Raf's Threads account added as a Threads Tester (App roles) and the invite accepted in Threads (Settings, Account, Website permissions, Invites); then THREADS_APP_ID (the Threads App ID, not the Meta App ID) and THREADS_APP_SECRET in Vercel and a redeploy.
- **Personal to do in the Job Kit (26 Oct 2026, Raf: 'create separate todo personal like daily chores unrelated to website and programming in a smart way'; previewed on the `personal-todo` branch, live since 26 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: an eighth section, **Personal**, slate blue (`.jk-sec-personal` #3b5a80), apart from the work To do. **Today**: a Personal box straight after To do (right: 'N today' / 'Nothing today'): what's late or due today with a tick box on each (up to 4), then 'Personal to do' with 'Next: Bins out, tomorrow · 1 any time'; on a computer the sidebar has Personal → Personal to do with the count. **The screen** ('personal', src/app/job-kit/personal.tsx): one box to type a chore as you'd say it, with what it understood under it as you type ('Bins out · Tomorrow · 19:00 · ↻ Every Tue'); groups Late (how late), Today, Tomorrow, This week, Later, Any time, and 'N done today' folded (tap a ticked box: Not done). Ticking a repeating one moves it to its next date ('Done: Bins out, back on Tuesday.' with Undo for 8 s); a one-off goes to Done today. Tap a chore: name, When (Today / Tomorrow / Weekend / Next week / No date, a date and a time), Repeat (Never, Every day, Weekdays, Every Tue, Every other Tue, Every month, Every year, Other…: every N days / weeks / months / years and which days), a note, Save, Delete (two taps, Undo). Empty: nine ideas to start from (bins, recycling, food shop, bedsheets, plants, smoke alarms, MOT, car insurance, boiler service) that go into the box for the day to be added. **Sums** (src/lib/chores.ts, no imports): parseChore reads times (7pm, 9:30, noon), repeats (every Tuesday, every other Thursday, every Mon, Wed and Fri, weekdays, weekends, on Tuesdays, daily, weekly, fortnightly, every 3 days, monthly, on the 1st every month, the last day, yearly, quarterly) and dates (today, tonight, tomorrow, this weekend, next week, next Friday, Sunday, in 3 weeks, 14 March, 3/12 UK style, on the 14th); the rest is the name. Weekly on set days, monthly and yearly come back on their own dates (a late tick never piles them up); every N days or weeks with no day counts from the day it was done. Days are local 'YYYY-MM-DD' worked out through UTC noon, so clock changes never move a day. **Phone and computer**: kept on the device (localStorage raf_jobkit_chores, changes waiting in raf_jobkit_chores_pend) and in step through its own table, not the jobs' sync (so an older Job Kit never sees a chore): Supabase cabinetos project, migrations 'jobkit_chores' and 'jobkit_chores_grants' (26 Oct 2026, live; table jobkit.chores, RLS on, no policies; public.jobkit_chores(p_secret, p_device, p_docs) SECURITY DEFINER, the sync secret, anon and service_role only), route /api/job-kit/chores behind the PIN. Each round sends what changed and gets the whole list back; the later change of a chore wins, a deleted one is kept as a date for 120 days. Every change is stamped on the store's clock (the device's clock plus its offset, learnt each round), so a phone a few minutes out can't win over a later change on the computer. One-offs ticked over 30 days ago are cleared. Line under the list: 'On this phone and your computer.' / saved here and waiting / no signal / no PIN. Tested (scratchpad chores/sums.mts, 53 checks on reading lines, steps, ticks and groups; chores/test.js, 57 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1440 with the live function in the throwaway Postgres: the box after To do, ideas, typing and the preview, groups in order, late, tick and Undo, done today and Not done, the sheet's repeats and Other…, Escape, delete and Undo, the store's rows, the box's ticks, the computer getting the same list and its tick and delete reaching the phone, offline then back online, the clock fix with a device 70 minutes fast, eight colour dots fitting at 320, no sideways scroll, no page errors). Not done: reminders that buzz the phone at the chore's time (would need Web Push, as the status app has).
- **Goodnotes on the job card (26 Oct 2026, Raf: 'is there a way to connect goodnotes with jobkit so my notes go straight to client card', then 'go'; previewed on the `goodnotes` branch, live since 26 Oct 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: Goodnotes has no API, so its own **Auto Backup** does the carrying: on the iPad, Goodnotes → Settings → Cloud & Backup → Auto Backup → OneDrive signed in as info@rafcarpentry.com, format PDF, 'Increase Auto Backup frequency' on (it uploads every 1 to 2 minutes after a change); notebooks in a Clients folder named with the ServiceM8 job number first ('1234 Smith kitchen'; '#1234', 'J1234' and 'Job 1234' read too). The Job Kit reads that OneDrive with the 'Raf Job Kit' app, app-only (src/lib/outlook.ts driveGraph, permission check 'files': Files.Read.All, Files.ReadWrite.All or Sites.Read(Write).All), which needs **Files.Read.All** added in Entra (API permissions → Microsoft Graph → Application permissions → Files.Read.All → Grant admin consent); nothing writes to OneDrive. **Work notebooks only** (26 Oct 2026, Raf: 'How do I link notebook to jobkit only the ones I use for work'): Goodnotes backs up every notebook, but the Job Kit only reads the ones in a Goodnotes folder called **Clients** (GOODNOTES_WORK_FOLDER to change it; found at any level of the backup, subfolders inside it count); other folders are only looked into far enough to find it, and nothing in them is kept or shown, so a personal notebook starting with a job number never lands on a job. To keep personal notebooks out of OneDrive altogether, Goodnotes' Auto Backup has excluded names (a notebook whose title contains one isn't backed up). **Server** (src/lib/goodnotes.ts): notebooks() lists every PDF in the Clients folder of the backup folder (GOODNOTES_FOLDER, default 'Goodnotes'), up to 4 folders deep and 60 folders, kept a minute; notebookThumb (OneDrive's medium thumbnail of the first page), notebookLink (OneDrive's pre-signed download link, about an hour, so a big notebook never passes through Vercel's 4.5 MB answer limit), notebookFile (server-side download, up to 40 MB). Errors in words: gn-consent (no Files.Read.All yet), gn-no-drive (info@ has no OneDrive: 'mysite not found'), gn-no-folder (no Goodnotes folder: the backup isn't on), gn-no-work (backing up, but no Clients folder yet), gn-secret, gn-busy, gn-gone, gn-too-big. Route /api/job-kit/goodnotes behind the PIN: list, thumb, open (the link), pdf (the PDF through the website, up to 4 MB, if the phone can't use the link), attach (the PDF onto the ServiceM8 job as 'Goodnotes - <name>.pdf' with attachPhoto, uuid from the job, notebook and its last change so the same version twice is one attachment, then touchJob so it shows in the diary). Matching (src/lib/goodnotes-match.ts, no imports): numberOf(name), notebooksFor(all, job number, linked paths), looseNotebooks (no number, not linked to any job). **Job card** (src/app/job-kit/goodnotes.tsx GoodnotesBox, after Notes and the Job diary): 'Goodnotes' with Refresh, 'Notebooks in your Clients folder named 1234 first. Checked just now.', each notebook with its first page (cached in Cache Storage raf-jobkit-thumbs-v1 by id and version), name, 'changed 12 min ago · 59 KB · Clients', **Open** (a full-screen viewer drawing every page with pdf.js, loaded only then: pdfjs-dist 6.4.299's legacy build, because the modern one uses Map.getOrInsertComputed that Safari lacks; pages drawn sharp for the screen as they scroll into view; Escape or Close) and **Put it on the ServiceM8 job** ('✓ On the ServiceM8 job'; after a newer backup 'Changed since: put the new version on ServiceM8'); no notebook yet: how to name one ('98 Baker'), and 'Link a notebook without a number (N)' to pick one (Link / Unlink; kept in the job's marks as gn, the sent versions as gnSent, both in step with the computer). The list of notebooks is kept on the phone (raf_jobkit_goodnotes) and checked again after 90 s. Tested (scratchpad gn/: made-up notebooks made as PDFs with Playwright (make.cjs), a pretend Microsoft sign-in and OneDrive (gnmock.mjs: listing, thumbnails by redirect, download links with or without CORS, modes for no permission / no OneDrive / no folder, a newer backup), the ServiceM8 stand-in; test.js, 33 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1440: the notebook for job 101 with its first page and not a personal '101 holiday list' outside Clients, the pages drawn sharp, page 2 on scrolling, Escape, the PDF on the ServiceM8 job with its name and size, still marked after a reload, a newer backup asking for the new version and sent as its own attachment, job 95's three pages, the pages through the website when the link is refused, job 98's naming help, only Clients notebooks without a number offered (not one outside it), link and Unlink, a linked one not offered on another job, each problem in words (no Clients folder too), no sideways scroll, no page errors). Not tried with the real OneDrive yet: needs Files.Read.All granted and Goodnotes' backup on; whether info@'s Microsoft 365 plan has OneDrive. **Setting it up (4 Nov 2026, Raf: 'can we finish setting up the goodnotes for jobkit')**: src/lib/outlook.ts kept its Microsoft sign-in token up to an hour, so after Files.Read.All was granted the Goodnotes box would have kept saying it can't read OneDrive; now a permission missing from a kept token (over 5 s old) signs in again once before saying it isn't granted (tokenFor(), used by graph() and reply()), so a grant works on the next Refresh (scratchpad gnfresh/test.mts: refused before, works straight after, one new sign-in, then kept). Goodnotes' Auto Backup puts everything in a folder called Goodnotes at the root of OneDrive by default, which is what the Job Kit looks for (GOODNOTES_FOLDER only if Raf types another name there). Set up on Raf's iPad on 4 Nov 2026 (Auto Backup on, OneDrive signed in, PDF, folder GoodNotes (OneDrive paths ignore case), Increase Auto Backup frequency on; a Clients folder made and his folder for this year moved into it). **Pages of a notebook on a job (4 Nov 2026, Raf keeps one notebook for the year with a job on each page: 'yes we have to do page linking for job'; previewed on the `goodnotes-pages` branch, live since 4 Nov 2026, Raf: 'Merge')**: the job's marks have gnPages {notebook path: page numbers} (in step with the computer like gn). Job card: 'Link pages from a notebook' (or 'from another notebook') lists every notebook in Clients except the job's own, newest change first (folder, 'named for job N'); tapping one opens the **page picker** (PagePicker in goodnotes.tsx: the whole notebook fetched to the phone once a visit, every page drawn small with pdf.js as it scrolls into view and kept as a JPEG so hundreds of pages don't fill an iPhone's memory; opens at the last page, where a new job goes, or on the job's pages when changing them; Small / Medium / Big (3 / 2 / 1 across on a phone, more on a computer), First page / Last page, a page on another job marked 'on #101 Sarah Jones', tap to tick; bar 'Pages 1 and 2 for #101 Sarah Jones', Link 2 pages / Link this page / Save pages, Clear, and 'Link the whole notebook instead' for one with no number). The card then shows 'Pages 1 and 2 / of 2026 Jobs · changed 3 min ago · Clients / 2026' with a picture of the first page (made on the device when the page is drawn, Cache Storage /__jk-gnp/<path>/<page>; the page number until then), **Open** (just those pages, picked out on the server so the phone doesn't download the year (route action 'pages', up to 4 MB, else the whole notebook and those pages from it), each marked 'p. 14', 'Whole notebook' / 'Just this job'), **Put these pages on the ServiceM8 job** ('Goodnotes - 2026 Jobs p1-2.pdf', only those pages; its uuid comes from what's on them (sha of the pages picked out with pdf-lib 1.17.1), so after the notebook changes elsewhere 'Put the latest on ServiceM8' adds nothing if those pages didn't change and says so (attachFile in servicem8.ts reports ServiceM8's 409)), Change pages, Unlink (two taps). A page past the end ('The notebook has 2 pages now, so page 3 isn't there any more') and a notebook renamed or moved out of Clients ('Not in your Clients folder any more') are said plainly. Pages are kept by number: a page put in the middle moves the later ones on one (the card says to check them with Open). Server: notebookPages(id, pages) in src/lib/goodnotes.ts (downloads up to 80 MB, gn-pages-gone with 'count|missing', gn-bad-pdf); route actions pages {id, pages} and attach {id, job, pages?} answering {uuid, at, same}. Also: numberOf no longer reads a date ('12.05.2026', '2026-10-12') or a year notebook ('2026', '2026 Jobs', '2026 notes') as a job number (a date-named notebook read as job 12 and couldn't be linked); a pdf.js document closing is waited for before the next opens (closeTask / openTask: opening one already on the phone straight after another closed caught the shared worker half shut, 'This PDF couldn't be opened here'), and a page still drawing when its notebook closes no longer throws. Tested on a production build (scratchpad gnpages/: make.mjs makes a 12-page year notebook with a job heading per page, a date-named one, '101 Jones pantry' and a personal one; mocks.mjs a pretend Microsoft sign-in and OneDrive from a folder (drive/) and ServiceM8 keeping attachments; lib.mts 7 checks on the server (pages picked out, the same pages the same fingerprint, pages past the end); sums.mts 23 checks; test.cjs 69 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1440, three clean runs: the job's numbered notebook as before, the year notebook first and the dated one offered, all 12 pages, opening at the end, sizes, ticks and the bar, linked with a picture, the menu chip's count, kept in the marks, Open with only those pages and no whole download, Whole notebook and back, on ServiceM8 as 'Goodnotes - 2026 Jobs p1-2.pdf' with 2 pages, unchanged pages not sent twice and said, a changed page sent as a new version, Change pages opening on them, Save pages, page 1 marked on job 101 from job 102, a whole dated notebook on job 103, kept after a reload, a page gone said in the viewer and when sending, Unlink two taps, a renamed notebook, no sideways scroll, no page errors). The preview can't reach OneDrive or ServiceM8 (MS_CLIENT_SECRET, SERVICEM8_API_KEY and JOBKIT_GITHUB_TOKEN are production only), so the first real try is on the live site with Raf's year notebook. **From Dropbox, not OneDrive (4 Nov 2026, the Job Kit showed 'Microsoft sent back an error: 400 BadRequest: Tenant does not have a SPO license': info@'s Microsoft plan through GoDaddy is email only, no OneDrive at all; Raf: 'go' to Dropbox; previewed on the `goodnotes-dropbox` branch, live since 5 Nov 2026, Raf: 'merge'; Raf made the Dropbox app 'Raf Job Kit' (Scoped access, Full Dropbox, the two permissions saved with the new console's Save changes) and added DROPBOX_APP_KEY / DROPBOX_APP_SECRET (sensitive, production only, so previews say Dropbox isn't set up) on 5 Nov 2026, redeployed, and connected the same evening: pages 144 and 145 of his 2026 notebook ('Untitled (1)') linked to job #161 and put on the ServiceM8 job, the first real use)**: Goodnotes' Auto Backup now goes to Raf's own Dropbox (rajan@op.pl; iPad: Storage Provider Dropbox, PDF, Increase Auto Backup frequency; set up 4 Nov 2026), which puts it in Goodnotes' app folder **/Apps/Goodnotes** (the iPad says '/Apps/GoodNotes 5'; Dropbox shows /Apps/Goodnotes, inside it GoodNotes/Clients/Rafał Solutions 2026/...; other /Apps folders starting 'goodnotes' are found by themselves; GOODNOTES_FOLDER to set it). src/lib/dropbox.ts: Raf's own Dropbox app (App Console: Scoped access, Full Dropbox, permissions files.metadata.read + files.content.read, Redirect URI https://www.rafcarpentry.com/api/job-kit/goodnotes/dropbox/callback; Vercel DROPBOX_APP_KEY, DROPBOX_APP_SECRET sensitive, Production + Preview); connected once from a job's Goodnotes box (Connect Dropbox: OAuth code flow, token_access_type offline, no scope param, signed state like the Meta login; GET /api/job-kit/goodnotes/dropbox/callback back to /job-kit?dropbox=ok|cancelled|expired|<code>, which job-kit-app.tsx hands to dropboxBack() and takes off the address, on the screen it was on); the refresh token is kept SEALED in jobkit.social under key 'dropbox' (social-store.ts, no migration: any key works), so the phone and computer share one connection; 4-hour access tokens made from it and remembered per server; an expired one is made again once. goodnotes.ts reads it all with one recursive list_folder (paged), keeps only PDFs under a Clients folder, paths from Clients ('Clients/Rafał Solutions 2026/Untitled (1)'); ids are Dropbox's 'id:...'; at = server_modified (to the second); thumbnails from get_thumbnail_v2 (works for PDFs though Dropbox only documents images; none = the page icon); open = get_temporary_link (4 hours; dl.dropboxusercontent.com allows CORS and Range). The route now needs only the PIN (pinGuard), adds actions connect {again} and disconnect, and list answers the account's email for a line 'From Goodnotes' backup in Dropbox (...)'. Errors: gn-not-connected (Connect Dropbox), gn-expired (Connect Dropbox again), gn-scope (tick the two permissions), gn-store, gn-no-folder, gn-no-work, gn-not-set-up (names the missing Vercel settings), gn-busy, gn-gone, gn-too-big. The OneDrive reading is gone (outlook.ts driveGraph left unused). **Big year notebooks** (Raf's 2026 one, 'Untitled (1)', was 166 MB on 4 Nov; RAFAL Solutions 2025 383 MB, 2024 798 MB): the phone opens a notebook straight from Dropbox's link in pdf.js with Range (disableAutoFetch, disableStream, 512 KB chunks), so the picker and a job's pages fetch only what's drawn; if that fails, a job's pages are picked out on the server (up to 4 MB), else the whole file comes with 'Opening the notebook… 45 of 166 MB'. Putting pages on ServiceM8 still reads the whole notebook on the server: up to 450 MB (PAGES_MAX; pdf-lib read a made-up 400 MB notebook in about 1 s at 1.3 GB peak, 171 MB in 0.5 s at 580 MB; Vercel gives 2 GB), maxDuration 90; bigger says to start a new notebook. Tested on a production build against a pretend Dropbox (scratchpad gndbx/: mocks.mjs: authorize page, token with code / refresh / invalid_grant, list_folder paging, temporary links with CORS, Range and preflight, thumbnails, modes deny, scope, expireNext, revoked, pageSize, nocors, norange, nothumb; a pretend jobkit_social; ServiceM8): api.mjs 37 checks (wrong PIN, not connected, the login address, a made-up state, cancelled, sealed token, the three Clients notebooks only, paging, a renewed token, thumbnail and none, link, pdf, pages, bad ids, 'GoodNotes 5', no Clients, no backup, missing permissions, revoked then Connect again, disconnect); test.cjs 80 checks as an iPhone 13, at 320 and 1440 (Connect Dropbox, back on the same job with the note, the sealed token, the picture of the first page, then all the page-linking checks with a folder 'Rafał Solutions 2026'); extra.cjs (no picture: page icon; link refused by the browser: opens through the website); big.cjs (a 171 MB, 106-page notebook). **Fixed with it, a Job Kit crash** (found by these tests, about 1 run in 4): an answer from /api/job-kit/sm8 cut off half way (signal lost or the app moving on while it arrived) was taken as OK with nothing in it (sm8-post.ts post() turned a failed res.json() into {}), so Current jobs was kept on the phone with no jobs and the app crashed on every open ('t is not iterable' in withCurrent: 'This page couldn't load') until its storage was cleared. Now a body that can't be read counts as no signal, fetchSm8 refuses a list without jobs, and savedSm8 ignores one kept like that.
- **Bing, ChatGPT and IndexNow (21 Oct 2026, Raf: 'ok let do the reminder about being more discovered by chat gpt bing webmaster tool')**: ChatGPT's search draws on Bing's index (plus OpenAI's own OAI-SearchBot), so the site tells Bing about new and changed pages itself. robots.ts already lets every crawler in (OAI-SearchBot, GPTBot, Bingbot, PerplexityBot included) and the Vercel firewall has no rules, so nothing blocks them. **IndexNow**: key 3e2f3206d1dcdbffc8a6b82fa9f88746 (public by design) in public/3e2f3206d1dcdbffc8a6b82fa9f88746.txt; src/lib/indexnow.ts + GET /api/indexnow, called by Vercel's cron once a day (vercel.json, `20 6 * * *` UTC, so between 7:20 and 8:19 in summer; Hobby runs it within that hour) with Authorization Bearer CRON_SECRET (Vercel, sensitive, production, set 21 Oct 2026 with the connector, never printed). A run reads the live sitemap, fetches every page (6 at a time, 12 s each), fingerprints what a search engine reads (title, description, the words, the pictures' files and alt text; scripts, styles and comments left out; sha256, 24 hex), compares with what was last sent (Supabase cabinetos project, rafsite.indexnow {url, hash, sent_at, gone_at} and rafsite.indexnow_runs, both RLS on with no policies, through public.rafsite_indexnow(p_secret, p_action diff|mark|run, p), the visit counter's secret RAFSTATS_SECRET; executable by anon and service_role only) and POSTs the new or changed pages plus any that left the sitemap to https://api.indexnow.org/indexnow {host www.rafcarpentry.com, key, keyLocation, urlList} (shared with Bing, Yandex, Seznam, Naver, Yep; not Google). 200 or 202 (key still being checked) = taken and remembered; anything else is logged and the same pages go again next day. The first run sends all 108 pages; after that usually only the home page (its photo of the day changes daily) and whatever a new job or post changed (the job, its blog post, My Work, its service and area pages, /designs...). By hand: the same GET with the secret, ?dry=1 to see what would go, ?all=1 to send everything. The function has no DELETE in it (the Supabase connector cancels statements it counts as destructive): pages that left the sitemap get gone_at, and the run log isn't trimmed (one row a day). **Raf's part (Bing Webmaster Tools)**: bing.com/webmasters, sign in, Add a site, Import from Google Search Console (brings rafcarpentry.com and its sitemap across; Bing says data shows after about 48 hours); then Sitemaps should list https://www.rafcarpentry.com/sitemap.xml, and the IndexNow page shows what the site sent. Its AI Performance report shows how often Copilot and Bing's AI answers cite the site (not ChatGPT). Tested (scratchpad indexnow/test.sh, 18 checks, the built site with the real function in a throwaway local Postgres 16 and a pretend IndexNow, indexnow/mock.mjs): the key file, 401 without or with a wrong secret, a dry run sends and keeps nothing, the first run sends all 108 with the right host, key and key location and no Job Kit or /d/ pages, a second run finds nothing new (the fingerprints are stable), a changed page and a page gone from the sitemap are sent once, a 403 keeps nothing so it goes again, 202 counts, all=1, every run logged. The first real run is the next morning's cron.
- **Cut list button in the wardrobe planner (22 Oct 2026, Raf: 'go' to week 1 of the 'Planner, languages and a cut list service' plan, after ChatGPT visitors from abroad used the planner; previewed on the `cutlist-button` branch)**: the planner's Send step ends with a box 'Building it yourself? / Get the cut list for this design' (cutBox() / cutSubmit() in planner-engine.ts, `.cutbox` in planner.css): every part with its size, the board layout and the edge banding, worked out in CabinetOS; 'Coming soon: leave your email and I'll send it when it's ready.' An email box and 'Get the cut list' (Enter works too); then 'Thanks. I'll email the cut list for this design to <email> when it's ready.'; changing the design brings the button back (changed() clears S.cut.sent). Nothing is sold or charged yet: it tests whether people want it. POST /api/cutlist-request {email, summary (the design's spec lines, no name or address), design (designPayload()), company (hidden box), ms} -> public.rafsite_cutlist(p_secret, 'add', p) (Supabase cabinetos project, rafsite.cutlist_requests {email, summary, design, country, host, created_at, sent_at}, RLS on, no policies; the visit counter's secret RAFSTATS_SECRET; the same email and design within a day kept once) -> 204. Bots: hidden box or under 4 s gets 204 and nothing kept; more than 5 from one address in 10 minutes 429. The tap is a stats goal 'cutlist' ('Cut list button (planner)' in src/lib/site-stats.ts, by the button's words 'Get the cut list'), so it shows under Ways to get in touch tapped. Job Kit Website stats: /api/job-kit/stats 'report' also brings `cutlist` (rafsite_cutlist 'list': total in the days, all, the latest 50), shown as a 'Cut list button (planner)' band (each email as a mailto, the day, the country, the design's Space and Doors lines, '(from a preview)' for other hosts; 5 then Show all) and on Today's Website line ('· 2 asked for a cut list'). /privacy has 'When you ask for a cut list' (updated 22 October 2026). Tested (scratchpad cutbtn/test.mjs, 47 checks at 1280, iPhone 13 and 320 against a pretend stats database: the box under the Send form, no email and a dotless email turned down, sent with Enter, the thanks line, the request kept with the design lines and payload and no name, host kept, a changed design asks again, the tap counted as the goal, bad email 400, hidden box and too fast keep nothing, twice kept once, the Job Kit report and band, wrong PIN, no sideways scroll, no page errors). With it, cabinetos-site's `done-for-you` branch adds the paid drawings and cut list service on cabinetos.co.uk/done-for-you (its AGENTS.md). **Emailed straight away (22 Oct 2026, Raf, after his own test to his Yahoo address got nothing because the button only kept the email: 'go' to emailing the real cut list)**: /api/cutlist-request keeps the request as before ('add' now answers its id), then requestCutlistFromCabinetOS (src/lib/cabinetos-inbound.ts: POST the same host as CABINETOS_INBOUND_URL, path /api/inbound/cutlist, Bearer CABINETOS_INBOUND_SECRET, 25 s) has CabinetOS work it out with its own cut list code and email it (parts per unit with sizes and edge banding, boards to buy, hardware, the workshop spreadsheet attached, 'Open it in CabinetOS, free' to /planner/<token>, which opens the design in their account after sign-up; cabinetos-site AGENTS.md 'Cut lists emailed from the wardrobe planner'); a sent one is marked with rafsite_cutlist 'sent' {id, parts, boards, token} (columns parts, boards, token added). Answers {sent, parts, boards}; sent false (CabinetOS down, design refused, not set up on previews) = kept, not emailed. The same email and design within a day isn't emailed twice (answers sent with again). The planner box now says 'Free, by email, with a spreadsheet for your cutting company', 'Working it out...' while it runs (about 2 s), then 'Sent to x: 61 parts on 13 boards.' (or 'I'll email ... shortly'). The Job Kit band marks each request 'Emailed: 61 parts, 13 boards' or 'Not emailed: send it yourself' and the header counts emailed. /privacy's cut list section says the design and email go to CabinetOS, which emails it through Resend. Tested (scratchpad cutmail/web-test.mjs, 33 checks at 1280, iPhone 13 and 320 with the website and CabinetOS dev servers and pretend Supabase, stats and Resend: sent line with the numbers, one email with the spreadsheet and the link, both sides marked sent with the same token, not twice for the same design, the Job Kit marks, no sideways scroll, no page errors; a refused design answers sent false).
- **Sharper stills from video (3 Oct 2026)**: the Palmers Green chimney-breast wardrobes job has no finished photos, only 1080 x 1920 clips, and its cover/blog hero had been taken mid-pan (blurry). Replaced with frame 2286 of IMG_2534.MOV (about 76.3 s), chosen by scoring every frame for sharpness (Laplacian variance: old 14, new about 250 to 330), then light denoise + unsharp mask: cover-sharp.jpg and blog hero-sharp.jpg. For future video-only jobs, pick stills the same way rather than at fixed times. g1 and g4 of that job are still soft (scores 32 and 19).

## Key Files
- src/lib/affiliates.ts — Central affiliate links config (all Amazon links in one place)
- src/lib/blog.ts — Blog utility (reads markdown, supports tools frontmatter)
- src/components/ToolsUsed.tsx — "Tools Used in This Post" component for blog posts
- src/components/LayoutShell.tsx — Conditionally hides nav/footer on /ms page
- GUIDE.md — Plain English guide for Raf on how to manage content

## How to Push Updates
```bash
cd /Users/rafaljanczy/Documents/App_Builds/raf-carpentry
# make changes or unzip update files
git add . && git commit -m "description" && git push
# Vercel auto-deploys in ~60 seconds
# Cmd+Shift+R to hard refresh browser
```

## How to Start a New Claude Chat
Upload this CLAUDE.md file and say:
"I'm continuing work on my raf-carpentry website. Here's the project context. I want to work on [specific task]."

At the END of each chat, ask Claude to update this file with any changes made.
